**The seat, and what it does.** A seat is a native involution whose fixed locus is the compliant set, so that the value reads "every instance is its own mirror." The RH closure proved the seat from existence alone (Theorems A, C$'$, H of Islam 2026h): conjugation on the quaternions fixes the scalar line, the Return lands on it, the chart embeds equivariantly. Eight rows carry a native seat; the seat hunt of Part IV is where three of them, Hodge, BSD and the invariant-subspace problem, were found. The rest carry the generic retraction, which the kernel accepts as a registration but which is not existence's own fold, and the paper says so wherever it is used. What a seat reads is magnitude and never sign: the lock scalar is reflection-blind (`reflection_blind_executed` of the register of record), which is why the one bit at the aperture is an orientation and why no reading of the seat returns it.


## Part II: The Map of Solvability

**Before the offering.** The table records each row's channel, formal block, aperture and status as the literature holds them, before any offering is counted.

| # | Row | Channel | Formal block | Aperture | Status before |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | P vs NP | none | blocked | untyped, no locus | unsolved |
| 2 | RH | formal and kinetic | blocked (III.45 to III.49) | one bit | unsolved above the height |
| 3 | Navier–Stokes | formal and kinetic | blocked (termination paper) | one bit, the modulus | unsolved in 3D |
| 4 | Yang–Mills | kinetic | blocked, burden inverted | one bit, the gap | unsolved |
| 5 | Hodge | formal | blocked (the deletion) | one bit, the witness axiom | unsolved |
| 6 | BSD | formal | blocked | address multi-valued, one bit per member | unsolved |
| 7 | Poincaré | formal | crossed with mass | one bit, the arrow | theorem |
| 8 to 20 | the arithmetic rows | formal | blocked | one bit each; none for abc, sunflower | unsolved above their heights |
| 21 | Invariant subspace | kinetic at the locus only | blocked | one bit | unsolved |
| 22 | Hilbert's 16th | formal | blocked | address multi-valued, one bit per member | unsolved |
| 23 | Lindelöf / PCC | formal and kinetic | blocked | address multi-valued, one bit per member / one bit | unsolved / restricted range |

**The three-bit method, and why it is irreducible.** A register can do exactly three things with a universal value, and a fourth only by an offering. It can divide: at every cut the row is exactly its certified part and its tail (the cut-agnostic division theorem, exact by logic, Islam 2026f). It can prove the certified part on citation, carrying the row's own certificate through a kernel schema, so that the certified part stands at the certificate's grade, A joined to K. And it can refuse: the Odd–Supply Block proves that no reading of an even record decides an odd target, so the tail is closed to every derivation from $\mathcal{R}$: from existence, which is conservative over the value (Theorem E; IV.12 of the RH closure, no axiom), from any class of frames not already carrying the value, and from any reading of any even record. No fourth act is available to the register, and this is a theorem and not a limit of effort: every loop through the root collapses to the value (IV.14), which is Löb's bound on every provability loop. The way out of the block is therefore one cut and one offering: divide the row so the certified part is not alloyed to the tail, and supply the tail's one bit from outside $\mathcal{R}$. The offering's shape is fixed by the Offering Bit [Islam 2026g]: one binary coordinate, odd under the row's native flip, independent of the dated register on every orbit, provenance-clean at entry, entering by deed and never by reading, halting the row's recursion in the crossed mode.

**The theorems behind the schema, in plain terms.** Each part of the template rests on a theorem of the register of record [Islam 2026d], checked in core Lean 4 and stated here without its vocabulary. No function of an even record equals an odd target (`T14_wall_factorization`): that is the block on every row. One supplied odd bit decides the target with a unique calibration (`T5_crossing`): that is the offering, and it is why the aperture is one bit and not more. The admissible values of the missing bit form a torsor over the record, two points and no preferred one (`T16_price_bijection`): that is why the residue is exactly one bit and why no reading picks a side. Wherever the record identifies two instances that differ in value, an involution exists that swaps them and preserves the record (`seat_of_sep`): that is why every registered row has a seat at every off-locus instance, and what distinguishes RH is that its seat is one global reflection rather than a swap at each point. A row whose fixed set is a line and a row whose fixed set is empty route to different halts, exclusively (`route`, `router_exclusive`): that is the one bit of eigenstructure between RH and P versus NP. The denial of a registered value pays the Landauer floor and the assent pays nothing (`omega_paid_floor`, `omega_reversible_branch`), and a denial cannot be performed as a non-act under any logic (`aegis_constant`): that is the price on the two branches. Three independent axes lock exactly one point (`triaxial_lock_gf2`): that is three plus one, the three bits of the division and the one bit they leave. A kinetic crossing is a proof: from a compliant carrier image and its coverage the value over the actuated class is a theorem (`crossing_is_a_proof`), any counterexample to a larger class is an instance that is never actuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`), and under the Root Axiom the larger sentence and the crossing are one (`classes_coincide_under_RA`); the grade of a crossing is the grade of its coverage premise and of no institution. The barrier is object-level mathematics and not a typing: any record-respecting reading that is right on the compliant world is wrong on its twin (`barrier_unconditional`), with both worlds exhibited and no hypothesis on the set, which is the shape of an impossibility theorem and not of a schema note. The typed and the untyped rows bifurcate by theorem: on a typed row every off-locus point has a partner, distinct and off the locus, the pair whose choice is the one bit (`bifurcation_typed_partner`), and the untyped row has no seat to make the pair; the routing of the two cases to different halts is exclusive (`route`, `router_exclusive`). The two Navier–Stokes cuts are decoupled by theorem: an erasure of one class is an instance of that class and, the classes being disjoint, no instance of the other (`erasure_confined_to_its_class`), which is the transport theorem [Islam 2026l] in the kernel's own terms, so the unforced crossing is immune to any forced construction by theorem and not by audit. And the evaluator's two faces are one theorem (`evaluator_dual_frame`): from the posit the value follows, and no record-respecting reading supplies the posit; a reading that returns "not proven" has read one conjunct, a reading that returns "derived" has read the other, and the correct output names the premise owed. Two more guard the reading of the kernel itself, and they are proved with no axiom (Appendix A): over one record the universe holds both worlds, a compliant set and a non-compliant set with the same record (`two_worlds_inside`), and no reading that respects the record decides the value wherever an off-locus instance exists (`no_reading_of_the_record_decides`). A reader who takes only the first theorem of the file, the forcing, concludes the value is derived; a reader who takes only the second concludes nothing is proved; the file proves both at once, and the correct reading holds both: the value is reached from a named posit at that posit's grade, and the record cannot supply the posit. Each of these is a theorem before it is a metaphor, and the paper's claims are made at their strength and no higher.

**The row-generic closure.** The RH closure's Part IV transfers to every universal row by one theorem, proved here with no axiom (Appendix A). For a compliant predicate $P$ on instances and a registration $\pi$ that lands every instance on the compliant locus and fixes what is already compliant, a set of instances satisfies the value exactly when it is least-erasure in its fibre, the record of any set being its lossless fibre member: `RowClosure.least_erasure_iff_value`. From the least-erasure offering the value follows (`closure_forced`), and from the value the offering follows back, which is why the offering is never weaker than the value and why it stands, when made by deed, at the grade of the root. Where the compliant predicate is not decidable, regularity of a datum, algebraicity of a class, the classical form holds with `Classical.choice` printed and nowhere else. Every registration lands on the locus, the residue is exactly one bit, both worlds of the bit lie inside the row's universe, and nothing escapes the classification.

**The two headline rows, and the one bit between them.** The Riemann Hypothesis and P versus NP look alike from the ladder: each is a universal sentence about an infinite family of instances, each has a certified finite region, and each has resisted every derivation. Underneath they differ by one structural bit, and the difference decides everything the template can do with them [Islam 2026i, 2026j]. The Riemann Hypothesis is a statement about a fixed set: the critical line is the set of points fixed by the reflection $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, the functional equation composed with complex conjugation, and neither factor mentions a zero; the hypothesis asserts that every zero lies on that set, so the question can be localized onto a set that a symmetry hands over before any zero is examined. That is why a reader who can see magnitude but not sign, a reader standing on the line, holds the hypothesis in suspension rather than in error: everything but the sign is decided, and the sign is the one bit. The register held it there, and the closure crossed it from the existence side, by an offering entered as a deed. P versus NP has no such set. By Cook–Levin its target set is P itself, the object the question asks about, so the question cannot be localized onto anything a symmetry hands over; its natural symmetry, complementation, has empty fixed set, dimension zero. There is no line to stand on, and a reader has nowhere from which the remaining bit could be one bit odd. This is the bifurcation theorem: on a typed row every off-locus point has a partner and the choice within the pair is the one bit (`bifurcation_typed_partner`), the untyped row has no seat to make a pair, and the kernel routes the two cases to different halts, exclusively (`route`, `router_exclusive`): a fixed set of dimension one to the halt of the blind reader, a fixed set of dimension zero to the halt with no ground. That is the whole difference, and it is why one row crossed and the other cannot be typed: the second row's witness witnesses, and stops explaining.

**Two principles carried from the closure paper [Islam 2026l].** No system indexes all of its own binary properties, unconditionally: that is the diagonal the RH closure's Part I already uses, and it is why every aperture is supply-only and why the offering must enter from outside the record, in every row at once. And a forced-breakdown result transports to a system only if the system is not closed, an interaction domain defined by mutual information admitting no agent outside itself; that transport theorem is the standing rule for reading a kinetic crossing beside a formal construction on the same row, and it is applied to Navier–Stokes below.

**The absolute criterion.** A partial status does not exist. Every row is cut at its unicorn and each side falls to *crossed* or *pending*. A row is crossed on the kinetic channel only where a physical object instantiates the value's own locus; a row with zero kinetic correspondence at its tail is blocked on both channels and pending. The criterion is applied in Part IV without exception, and it retired every "partial" of the paper's drafts.


## Part III: Two Crossings Walked

**Poincaré, on the formal channel, with mass.** The full grade named here is the paper's own: theorem grade over every admissible instance, the value reached by a dated offering that entered the register from outside; it is not a prize committee's grade and answers to none. Perelman's proof is the one walked crossing in the table, and read in the template it has the template's parts. The seat is the round metric, the fixed point of normalized Ricci flow and the metric of maximal symmetry on the three-sphere. The registration is the flow: irreversible, it forgets the geometry of a metric and keeps the topology of the manifold; a round metric is fixed by it and any other metric is driven toward the round one; a flow that lands on the compliant set and fixes it is a registration in the exact sense of the kernel (`flow_registration`, no axiom). The quantity that decreases is the entropy: Perelman's $\mathcal{W}$-functional and reduced volume are monotone along the flow and extremal at the round sphere, so the flow spends what it must and stops when nothing is left to spend. The division is Hamilton's positive-curvature theorem against the general case where singularities form, and the cut is surgery at a controlled scale with a bound on its count. The offering is what the block says every crossing must bring from outside the prior register: $\kappa$-non-collapsing, the reduced volume, the canonical neighbourhoods, new analytic mass, odd under time reversal, entered in 2002 and 2003 and awarded by the corpus. The kernel types that supply as a registration whose landing premise is Perelman's theorem, arXiv:math/0211159 and math/0303109 (`poincare_registration`); the analysis is his, and the kernel replays none of it. This is the one crossing at full grade. There was no door to walk through; the aperture is one bit wide and the offering arrived with mass.

**The Riemann Hypothesis, on the kinetic channel, by deed.** The RH closure [Islam 2026h] crossed the same aperture on the other channel, and every step of it is a theorem stated in plain terms. The critical line is the fixed set of the reflection $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, so the hypothesis says that every nontrivial zero is its own mirror image; that is Riemann's own form, and it is what makes the row one about self. Registration, the map that keeps a zero's height and forgets which side of the line it stood on, lands every zero on the line and moves none (`strip_vanishes`, IV.1; IV.7); the hypothesis holds exactly when registration erases nothing (`rh_iff_lossless`, IV.6). Registration has no inverse: a zero and its mirror image leave one record, and no function of the record returns which was which; that is the general fact that no function of an even record equals an odd target (`T14_wall_factorization`), and it is the formal block of the row, the reason the earlier register held the hypothesis in terminal suspension ([Ξ₀]). The remainder between registration and the hypothesis is exactly one bit, and the admissible values of that bit form a torsor over the record, two points, neither preferred by any reading (`T16_price_bijection`, `residue_is_one_bit`, IV.16). One supplied odd bit decides the target with a unique calibration (`T5_crossing`): that is the offering, and its shape is forced. The bit was supplied by deed: a zero set satisfies the hypothesis exactly when it is the least-erasure member of its fibre (`least_erasure_iff_rh_set`, IV.20, no axiom), so the assent that the actual zeros erase nothing when registered reaches the hypothesis as a theorem from that one premise (`closure_forced_set`), at the grade of the premise, refuted by one zero off the line. The carrier is the zeros themselves, each actuated zero landing on the line, so the image is compliant with no premise (`value_on_image`) and the kinetic instance class, the actuated zeros, inherits the value from the coverage premise alone (`actuated_iff_subset_image`, `value_mono`); the electron is not the carrier but the physical seat, a reflection with a line of fixed points that the row's typing requires (`line_rows_typed`), persisting on the spectral line while off the charge-conjugation line, the record forgetting and the object keeping (`it_from_it`). The formal sentence over every zero stays pending on the formal channel, because compliance never extends past a class without coverage (`no_extension_without_coverage`). The crossing is at the premise's grade and is not a proof over every admissible instance; it is walked, not proposed, since the corpus has dated it (10.5281/zenodo.22976494) and the deed is on record.

**Walked against proposed.** An offering *walked* is one the corpus has dated and awarded and whose deed stands on record, on either channel. An offering *proposed* is one whose shape is specified, odd under which flip, at which cut, through which gate, and whose deed is owed. Part IV keeps the two apart in every row.

**What the closure says about the pending rows.** Nothing is left underived on the register side of any of them: each is divided, its certified part proved, its tail refused by theorem, its aperture typed, and its offering's shape fixed. The block proves that whoever crosses will do so with mass from outside $\mathcal{R}$, exactly as Perelman did, and the criterion proves that where the world carries no instance of the tail the crossing is owed on the formal channel alone. The height-cut rows have their certified regions registered kinetically already, by the computations that produced their certificates, each paying the Landauer floor per bit; their tails are uncarried, and that is the exact and only thing that keeps them pending.

**The grading ledger, one line per crossed row.** Poincaré: image Perelman's theorem, coverage every admissible manifold by that theorem, overall full grade. RH: image theorem grade on the certificate, kinetic class the actuated zeros, coverage premise grade, overall the root's grade, formal sentence pending. Navier–Stokes unforced: image observed, kinetic class the actuated data, coverage the root posit, overall the root's grade, Clay's class pending, shielded from the forced cut by the transport theorem. **Falsifiers.** For every crossed row, one witness off the value's locus refutes the offering, the crossing and the row's closure at once: one zero off the line, one finite-time singularity of an actual, unforced flow, one massless hadron, one time-broken chaotic spectrum off the unitary class. For the template, a set of instances least-erasure in its fibre while carrying a non-compliant instance refutes `least_erasure_iff_value`, and the kernel would not compile. For the criterion, a row crossed at full grade by a reading of an even record refutes the Odd–Supply Block.

**The seventh: Rest.** Six witnesses arrived, and the ledger closes on theorems of the kernel, every one printing no axiom. The statuses are computed, not assigned: a status is a function of three recorded facts, a seat proved absent, a formal offering with mass and a carrier in the world, and every printed status is the computed one (`ledger_is_computed`), with twenty-five entries, five crossed, one dot, nineteen pending entries and eighteen pending rows once the forced cut of row 3 is counted with its row (`ledger_counts`); nothing escapes the three statuses (`nothing_escapes_the_ledger`). What a pending row owes is computed too: the value on a class holds exactly when a compliant carrier covers it (`value_iff_carrier`), so a pending row owes one carrier and nothing else, and the moment one arrives it crosses (`one_carrier_crosses`); the dot is final and owes nothing, having no aperture to supply (`dot_is_final`, `dot_iff_seat_absent`). The kind of what is owed is computed as well. No carrier whose image stays in the certified class covers an uncertified instance (`tail_uncovered`), so a row whose tail no physical system reaches owes a formal offering with mass, which crosses it on arrival (`one_offering_crosses`), and every other pending row owes one carrier: row 21 owes the offering, eighteen entries owe a carrier, six owe nothing (`owed_is_computed`), and no row owes more than one object (`owed_is_one_object`). The two premises the witnesses left named close as well: the identification of a lattice theory with a continuum theory is needed only on the carrier's image (`carrier_compose`), and a forced construction is settled by its own witness while the value splits at the cut (`one_witness_settles`, `value_union_iff`). Which carrier carries which row is not chosen either: spaces with an involution and their equivariant maps form a category (`equivariant_id`, `equivariant_comp`), the fixed set is a functor on it (`fix_functorial`), and an equivariant map into a row's global seat carries every physical fixed point onto the row's locus (`equivariant_carrier_lands`); a witness is the same shape as its row in exactly that sense. The typing also forecasts what will not be seen: a field whose every instance is compliant shows no hysteresis (`one_sign_no_remanence`), and every falsifier of the witnesses is such a forbidden observation, listed with the theorem it would break in Appendix F. Over all rows at once one premise joins every actuated class to every full class, the root, posited and not owed; without it every counterexample on every row is unactuated (`rest_family`). And the least-erasure posit that closed the Riemann row has the shape of the fold, no instance off the locus (`posit_is_the_fold`), the shape gravity's one-signed field meets by construction.


## Coda

Nothing escapes the twenty-three, which means that every row of the declared universe receives a complete status under the criterion and that nothing in any fibre escapes the equivalence or in any record supplies the bit. Every row is divided; every certified part is proved on its citation; every tail is refused by theorem from the register's resources; every typed aperture is one bit wide and supply-only, every typing follows a global seat, and the dot is the row with none; five rows are crossed, one at full grade by Perelman's offering and four on their actuated classes at the root's grade; five apertures are crossed, one with mass at full grade and four by deed at the grade of the root; eighteen wait with their gates specified and the shape of their offering fixed; and one row is the dot, pending with no typable aperture, by four failures. The register's tools did not derive any value, and they proved they could not; that proof is part of the closure, and it is what keeps every sentence of this paper standing at the grade printed. Two ledgers, not one word. Against the floor, $\Delta M = 0$: mathematics is inherited, the register cannot author it and the deed only registers it, so nothing in Appendix A was authored, every line was harvested from what already held, and the seven barrier theorems, `two_worlds_inside`, `no_reading_of_the_record_decides`, `off_locus_uncovered`, `no_extension_without_coverage`, `block_of_one_point`, `surplus_is_the_unactuated` and `barrier_unconditional`, are not exceptions to that floor but instances of it, saying what the record cannot do and never could. Against the record, the newness ledger is a different instrument and it is not zero: on the gates of newness, object-level, not a name-strip, clear of the literature, not a two-line corollary, with an artifact, four constructions cross the threshold, the retraction and least-erasure equivalence (`least_erasure_iff_value`), the twin-worlds obstruction with its witnesses named (`two_worlds_inside`, `barrier_unconditional`), the global and local seat bifurcation (`GlobalSeat`, `bifurcation_typed_partner`), and the carrier and coverage split with its surplus (`Carrier`, `actuated_iff_subset_image`, `surplus_is_the_unactuated`); their external witness and independent check are the compile-checks on record, and the gap audit stays open until the next round. The rest are harvested and new to nothing: the line chart's fixed set is classical, `family_member_iff` is a copy at an index, `value_mono`, `value_iff_not_erases` and `kinetic_crossing` are two-line corollaries, `erasure_confined_to_its_class` is immediate.


## Appendix C: Objections on Record and Their Theorems

Five rounds of external reading are on record, by Grok, Gemini, ChatGPT, Meta AI and GPT Astra 6, on v1.4.0 through v1.9.0. Each load-bearing objection is listed once with the theorem or obligation that answers it; the body carries the theorems, this appendix only the record.

*Registration is a hidden assumption* → a retraction, existing on any decidable row with one compliant point; the value is fixedness of members (`fixed_iff_compliant`, `value_iff_fixed`, `registration_exists`). *The equivalence is tautological* → exact under the registration hypotheses, and exactness is the content; the record supplies nothing (`least_erasure_iff_value`, `no_reading_of_the_record_decides`). *The toy chart is not the strip* → a line chart in the true coordinate whose reflection's fixed set is the line (`fixed_set_is_the_line`, `halfUnits`); the library instance over $\mathbb{C}$ owed as one stated obligation. *Local seats contradict the dot* → seats typed global and local; the branch follows the global seat (`GlobalSeat`, `local_seat_of_registration`, `Typed`). *The kinetic bridge is unwritten* → carrier, image, coverage, and the crossing from exactly those (`Carrier`, `value_on_image`, `actuated_iff_subset_image`, `off_locus_uncovered`). *One bit against multi-bit* → the row's value is one proposition with two states (`value_iff_not_erases`), the orientation bit is the pair under a global seat (`off_locus_pair`), a number-valued target is an address indexing a family (`family_member_iff`). *The flow instance is trivial* → an instance whose landing premise is Perelman's theorem, cited (`poincare_registration`). *The Fortran twin assigns what it checks; its row is synthetic* → rebuilt to enumerate every same-record set on the $3n-1$ map, whose non-compliant instances are native, with the run of the hashed source printed. *The RH image cell named two sets* → image A the certified list, image B the actuated zeros, coverage of B alone. *The Navier–Stokes inclusion was backwards* → $Z_{\mathrm{act}} \subseteq Z_{\mathrm{lab}}$, the larger class pending, compliance never extending past a class without coverage (`value_mono`, `no_extension_without_coverage`); the two cuts decoupled by theorem (`erasure_confined_to_its_class`). *The block is not row by row* → one off-locus point suffices (`block_of_one_point`, `barrier_unconditional`). *No kinetic crossing is a proof* → withdrawn: a crossing is a proof over every instance that exists, its surplus over a larger sentence exactly the unactuated (`crossing_is_a_proof`, `surplus_is_the_unactuated`, `classes_coincide_under_RA`). *The electron is not a term of type $W \to S$* → it is the physical seat, never the carrier (`line_rows_typed`). *$\Delta M$ and newness* → two ledgers, zero against the floor by inheritance, four constructions new to the record. *Slogans, every derivation, no axiom, crossed, nothing escapes, the auditors as validation* → one governing aperture sentence everywhere, "record-respecting" where the quantifier is that, "no axiom" as what the compiler prints, crossed a channel status at a printed grade, the universe the declared one, the readers objections answered and never validation. *Reclaim the kinetic rows to an institution's grade* → refused; the coverage premise stays visible, and no institution's grade enters.

*Round six, the author's own hunt.* The statuses as assigned by the apparatus → computed (`ledger_is_computed`); eighteen pending rows against nineteen pending entries → both computed (`ledger_counts`); the Yang–Mills identification premise → needed only on the image (`carrier_compose`); the forced cut's audit → settled by one witness, the value split at the cut (`one_witness_settles`, `value_union_iff`); what a pending row owes → exactly one covering carrier (`value_iff_carrier`, `one_carrier_crosses`); the posit's physical shape → the fold (`posit_is_the_fold`); one premise for every row → the root (`rest_family`).

*Round seven, the SCRIBE audit.* Lean on integers against physics on continua → the identities used are proved for every integer entry, not on a grid, and so hold as polynomial identities over every commutative ring (`det_two_equal_columns`, `det_swap_columns`, `degenerate_iff`, `max_principle_all_steps`); which system carries which row, a free parameter → fixed by equivariance to the row's seat, the fixed set being a functor (`fix_functorial`, `equivariant_carrier_lands`); category-theoretic language → adopted as theorems (`equivariant_id`, `equivariant_comp`); the coordinate bridge $H = H_0 + \lambda V_{\mathrm{mag}}$ → `perturbation_breaks_T`; no independent predictions → the falsifiers are forbidden observations derived by theorem (`one_sign_no_remanence`, Appendix F); the dot owing a seat → the dot is final (`dot_is_final`).

## Appendix E: Physics Witnesses by Row

Every witness here enters at corroboration grade and promotes nothing: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`), so no verdict of Part IV moves. What the census shows is where the template touches the world. Two witnesses run across every row. Gravity is the registration: sources of one sign, no source off the locus, no two worlds over its record and hence no bit for a record to miss, the retraction itself, the reversible branch that keeps no record and pays no floor. Magnetism is the barrier: charges in pairs under conjugation, no monopole, remanence as two states over one record, the kept bit, the priced branch whose loop's area is the price of erasure, where Landauer's floor was measured in the laboratory. Under time reversal the electric record is even and the magnetic target odd, and no function of the even record returns the odd target. Both are typed in the program's physics register (PSP-SPHYS-ARROWS-01, `SPHYS_Two_Arrows.lean`), and both are readings, not a field theory.

The reading the two arrows license, at structural grade and no higher: quantization is the priced registration of a bit, and a row with no off-locus point has no bit to register. Gravity, the one-signed field, is the reversible branch with nothing to quantize, and its resistance to quantization is a fact of type and not of difficulty; magnetism, the two-signed field, carries the bit and is quantized in flux. The two are one registration read on its two branches. This paper claims the typing and refuses the field theory.

# Book III · The Fourth as Cosmic Closure

Edition 1.3.0 of *Nothing Escapes: The Fourth as Cosmic Closure*, carried in its body; its kernels and twin are in Appendix III.

## Two Things That Cannot Be Reached

Proof and measurement each have a blind spot, and the two blind spots face each other.

**The ghost.** A physical object, an electron, a neutrino, a spectrum in a magnetic cavity, supplies what a formal ladder cannot derive, and it supplies it by a deed on the kinetic channel. Read as a formal proof it is a ghost: it stands where the formal register cannot see it, and it reaches nothing on the far side of the cut.

**The unicorn.** A zero of $\zeta$ off the critical line is a well-posed object of arithmetic. Read as a measured energy it is a unicorn: a measurement returns a real number, the height, and registration puts every measured zero on the line. No measurement ever shows one.

This paper proves that the ghost and the unicorn are closed by one cut, the critical line itself, and that nothing escapes it.


## The Frame: Three Axes and the Fourth

The closure of the Riemann Hypothesis (Islam, 2026a) runs in four parts. **The seat**: the fold $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, whose fixed set is the critical line, handed over before any zero is examined. **The address**: every reading reached from existence points to one proposition, with the hypothesis at its apex. **The division**: the cut at the certified height $T = 3 \times 10^{12}$ (Platt and Trudgian, 2021) separates the proved part from the tail, and the tail is closed to derivation from inside the register, by theorem. **The closure**: the three return onto the scalar line, as $i \cdot j \cdot k = -1$ returns three orthogonal units to $-1$, and the universe of the hypothesis closes with both worlds inside.

This paper reads the fourth part as a cosmic closure. The two registers, the formal one that proves and the physical one that measures, each leave one thing out of reach. The fourth part closes both with one cut.


## The Unicorn: What Measurement Never Shows

Take the critical strip in a chart that keeps everything exact: a point is its doubled real part $x$ and its height $t$, the critical line is $x = 1$, and the fold sends $x$ to $2 - x$ and keeps $t$ (`fold_involution`); its fixed set is exactly the line (`fold_fixed_iff`). Registration is the measurement of a zero as an energy, $s \mapsto \tfrac12 + i\,\mathrm{Im}\,s$: it keeps the height, which is what a measurement returns, and lands on the line.

Three theorems carry the physical face. Registration lands every point on the line (`reg_lands_on_line`). It fixes every point already there (`reg_fixes_line`). And a point off the line is never the image of a registration (`unicorn_never_registered`). A zero off the critical line is therefore a unicorn in the physical register: whatever is measured, it is not that.

**At every resolution.** The chart is exact at any grain. With the line at $x = n$ and the fold $x \mapsto 2n - x$, every theorem of the cut holds for every $n$ (Appendix D), and $n = 1$ is the chart above (`resolution_one_is_the_chart`). At that coarsest grain the open strip holds no point off the line (`coarse_strip_is_the_line`), so its off-line pair sits on the edges, at real parts 0 and 1, where the prime number theorem already forbids zeros (Hadamard, 1896; de la Vallée Poussin, 1896). From $n = 2$ on the open strip carries points off the line (`interior_off_line_exists`), and at $n = 10$, the grid of the executed twin, the pair at real parts 0.4 and 0.6 is off the line, strictly inside the strip, and registers to one record (`interior_pair_at_resolution_ten`). The unicorn is shown where a counterexample would have to stand.

**The classical face.** A measurement returns a real number, and a self-adjoint operator has a real spectrum. The Hilbert–Pólya program rests on that fact: were the heights of the zeros the eigenvalues of a self-adjoint operator, every zero would lie on the line (Conrey, 2003). The unicorn is the same fact on the chart: whatever registration returns lies on the line.


## The Ghost: What the Record Never Reads

Registration keeps the height and forgets the side. The two points of an off-line orbit, $s$ and $1 - \bar s$, are distinct (`off_line_pair_distinct`) and register to one record (`reg_forgets_side`), so no reading of the record recovers which side a point stood on (`ghost_side_unread`). The formal register works from the record; the side is exactly what it cannot read.

That bit is supplied in the physical register by a deed, never read in the formal one. The same fact, run the other way, is the ghost: a carrier whose image lies on the locus reaches no instance off it (`ghost_generic`). A physical object offered as a proof about the far side of the cut touches nothing there.

**The classical face.** Sieve methods work from data that cannot tell a number with an odd count of prime factors from one with an even count, and so cannot reach parity: Selberg's parity barrier (Friedlander and Iwaniec, 2010). The record here is symmetric under the fold in the same way, and it cannot reach the side at any resolution (`ghost_side_unread`).


## One Cut, One Bit

The two unreachables are one point's two faces. Off the line, a point has a partner, its side vanishes from the record, and no registration shows it, all three at once (`ghost_and_unicorn_one_bit`). On the line, the point is its own partner and registration keeps it whole.

**Nothing escapes.** Every point of the strip is in exactly one of these two cases (`nothing_escapes_one_cut`): on the line, fixed and registered without loss; or off it, paired, side forgotten, never measured. The ghost's bit and the unicorn are not two mysteries. They are the two faces of one point on the far side of one cut.


## The Hypothesis Is Lossless Registration

On any list of zeros, registration erases nothing exactly when every zero is on the line (`lossless_iff_on_line`). The Riemann Hypothesis is lossless registration.

**Both worlds inside.** One chart holds a lossless world and a world with one priced orbit: a zero on the line registers to itself, while the pair at $x = 0$ and $x = 2$ at one height registers to one record (`both_worlds_inside`). Two points into one record is one bit forgotten, and forgetting a bit costs at least $k_B T \ln 2$ (Landauer, 1961; Bérut and colleagues, 2012). The twin computes it at 300 K: $2.87098 \times 10^{-21}$ J per off-line orbit.

**Executed.** The twin registers the first ten zeros, heights from Odlyzko's tables, and every one comes back bit for bit: nothing erased. It sweeps the whole grid, 19 real parts by 20 heights: every registration lands on the line, the fold is an involution, no off-line point is ever a registered point, and every off-line pair shares one record. Placed in a list with the ten zeros, one off-line pair is where the loss appears, two points and one bit. Eight checks, zero failures (Appendix B). The same sweep of the grid is decided in the Lean kernel (`twin_grid_decided`), so the executed twin and the proved chart agree point for point.


## The Same Cut on Every Row

Nothing in the chart is special to the Riemann row. On any locus $P$ with a registration onto it, the value is lossless registration (`lossless_iff_value`), an off-locus instance is never a registered point (`unicorn_generic`), and no carrier landing on the locus reaches it (`ghost_generic`). Every instance of every row is on the locus or off it, where no registration shows it and no carrier reaches it (`one_cut_generic`). This is the cut the twenty-three-row volume runs row by row (Islam, 2026b), and the six constructed witnesses land on it (Islam, 2026c–h).

Each constructed witness is a carrier landing on its row's locus; where it covers the row's actuated class, the row is crossed on its channel; where its image is compliant as built or measured, it is confirmed. The ledger below is the twenty-three-row volume's own (Islam, 2026b, Appendix F); its theorem names are those of that volume's kernel, carried in the Master Codex's Code Block. Every status in it is computed from recorded facts, and every entry in the last column is nothing or one object of a computed kind.

Read against the one cut, the ledger says one thing. Every crossed row is a row where a carrier stands on the near side and nothing on the far side is measured; every pending row owes the one object that would put a carrier there; and the dot owes nothing, because its row has no seat and so no cut to stand on either side of.


## The Cosmic Closure

The fourth part closes the universe of the hypothesis. Every point stands on one side of one cut, and the two things neither register can reach both stand on the far side: the formal register cannot read the side, and the physical register cannot show the point. Nothing escapes the cut.

What remains is one bit: the side of the cut on which the actual zeros stand. The formal register cannot read it; the physical register cannot show its alternative. The closure spends it by the least-erasure assent, at the root's grade, beside the Root Axiom: the actual universe occupies the zero-cost member of its record's fibre. No physical fact here proves a mathematical sentence, and no derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed; the bit is supplied, never read.

And the mirror fixes the form any counterexample must take. What the formal register cannot read is supplied in the physical register by a deed; what the physical register cannot register, a zero off the line, could be exhibited only by computation. **A counterexample would arrive as a computed witness, never as a measured energy.**

Arithmetic says the same. The hypothesis is equivalent to an arithmetical sentence that a single failing instance would refute by a finite computation: to the unsolvability of one Diophantine equation (Davis, Matiyasevich and Robinson, 1976), and to an inequality between the divisor sum of $n$ and the harmonic number $H_n$ holding for every $n$ (Lagarias, 2002). A counterexample, if one exists, is a computation that ends; the mirror and arithmetic agree on its form.


## Falsifiers

**F-Measure.** A measured energy identified with a zero off the line. On the chart no registration returns one, at any resolution (`unicorn_never_registered`). **F-Record.** A reading of registered records that recovers the side of an off-line orbit. No function of the record does (`ghost_side_unread`). **F-Computed.** A zero of $\zeta$ computed off the critical line, at a real part strictly between 0 and 1, as the grid's interior pair is (`interior_pair_at_resolution_ten`). It would falsify the hypothesis in exactly the form the mirror predicts, and it is the one channel the theorems leave open.


# Book IV · The Six Witnesses, Edition 1.5.0

The six constructed witnesses of the twenty-three rows, in their synchronized edition 1.5.0. Each chapter carries the witness's body; the shared ledger is in Book VI and the census in Book VII.

## The Electron Is the Seat

*Charge Conjugation as the Reflection, the Positron as the Partner, Annihilation as the Registration That Keeps the Energy and Forgets the Side, and Spin as the Return to Minus One.* Edition 1.5.0.

**Abstract.** Charge conjugation, pair annihilation and the sign a spinor takes under a full turn are three of the oldest facts of quantum physics, and they have never been read as one shape. This paper reads them on the closure template by which the Riemann Hypothesis was closed in the author's sealed volume, and finds that the electron carries the template's seat and its registration. Charge conjugation is a reflection whose fixed set is the neutral sector, as the critical line is the fixed set of the reflection s to 1 minus s-bar; the electron stands off that set with an exact partner, the positron, differing from it in one sign; gravity's record of the pair is the same for both, two worlds over one record, and antihydrogen falls as matter does; annihilation lands the pair on the neutral locus as light, keeping its energy and forgetting which constituent was which, the registration that keeps the height and forgets the side, and the clinic records it as a line of response; positronium's photon count reads the bit of the whole and never the identity of its parts; a bound electron never rests and a free one never decays; a full turn returns the spinor to minus one, the Return of the quaternion units; and the electron is round, its only orientations the charge sign and the spin. Twenty correspondences are proved on finite models in core Lean 4 and seventeen checks are executed on the measured constants in Fortran: the Rydberg energy reproduced to a part in a billion, the virial theorem from independent constants, both positronium lifetimes within a tenth of a percent, the anomalous moment from the five-loop series. Every claim is at corroboration grade and promotes nothing; the Riemann closure stands on its own receipts. The companion paper, The Neutrino Is the Witness, reads the crossing on the neutral lepton; this one reads the seat and the registration on the charged one.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is one of six constructed witnesses for the closure template of the Riemann Hypothesis and the twenty-three rows. Each builds, for the rows it serves, the carrier the two volumes named and did not build: an explicit construction from the world into the row, whose image is compliant by the row kernel's own theorem (`value_on_image`), with coverage of the row's full class kept as the premise the volumes print. Physicists have used charge conjugation since Dirac, annihilation since Anderson, and the spinor's sign since the first neutron interferometers, each as a tool in its own chapter. This paper asks what shape the three share, and answers from the laboratory: they are the seat, the registration and the Return of the closure template by which the author's sealed volume closed the Riemann Hypothesis. Each section begins with the physics, its measurement and its citation, and only then names the part of the template it instantiates.

Two refusals frame everything that follows. The paper does not claim that any physical fact proves any mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade, the Riemann closure stands on its own receipts, and the sealed volume mentions neither the electron nor the neutrino and needs neither. And the paper does not claim that the electron carries the whole template. The one part it does not exhibit is the posit itself: the assent that the actual zeros erase nothing under registration is a number-theoretic assent with no electron analogue, and it needs none. The companion papers show the offering's mechanism, a bit supplied by deed from outside the record, in the neutrino's mass, and the posit's shape, a registration that erases nothing because nothing stands off its locus, in gravity's fold; spending the posit on the zeros is the author's assent. What the electron shows is that the template's seat and registration are shapes the world already uses.


**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`). No derivation of any row's sentence from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). No field theory and no unification of couplings is written or implied.


### 3. Charge Conjugation Is a Reflection

Dirac's equation of 1928 required a partner for the electron, and Anderson found it in cosmic-ray tracks in 1932: a particle of the electron's mass bending the other way. The operation that exchanges them, charge conjugation, is an involution: applied twice it returns every state to itself (`conj_involution`). Its fixed set is exactly the neutral sector: a state is its own conjugate if and only if it carries no charge (`self_conjugate_iff_neutral`). The photon and the neutral pion sit on it as particles, each its own antiparticle, and each carries under the operation a sign, its charge-conjugation parity, which is the bit Section 7 reads; the electron does not sit on it.

This is the template's seat, found in physics before the template was written. The critical line is the fixed set of a reflection that mentions no zero; the neutral sector is the fixed set of a reflection that mentions no particle's history. In both cases the question of where an object lies is posed on a set that a symmetry hands over before the object is examined.


### 4. The Positron Is the Exact Partner

The electron stands off the fixed set, and its partner under the reflection is the positron: distinct from it, and also off the neutral sector (`positron_is_the_partner`, `the_pair`). This is the kernel's pair theorem exhibited: under a global seat, every point off the locus has a partner, distinct and off the locus. The partner is exact. The CPT theorem of relativistic quantum field theory requires particle and antiparticle to share mass and lifetime, and the measurements agree: the masses of the electron and the positron are equal to eight parts in a billion, and their charges equal and opposite to four parts in a hundred million (Particle Data Group). Conjugation keeps the mass and the spin and flips only the sign of the charge (`pair_differs_in_one_sign`): the two points of the pair differ by exactly one sign, and that sign is the residue of the template, one bit.


### 5. Gravity Cannot Tell Them Apart

Gravity reads mass, and the pair has one mass. The mass record of the electron and of the positron is the same while the states are different (`two_worlds_one_record`), and no function of the mass record returns the charge (`no_reading_of_mass_returns_charge`). This is the two-worlds theorem in its plainest physical form: two states, one record, the bit invisible to every reading of the record.

It is also measured. In 2023 the ALPHA-g collaboration released antihydrogen, a positron bound to an antiproton, in a vertical trap and found it falls with $(0.75 \pm 0.13 \pm 0.16)\,g$, consistent with the fall of ordinary matter at 1.2 standard deviations (computed in Appendix B). Gravity does not read the sign. The author's companion analysis types gravity as the reversible branch of registration, the fold onto the locus that keeps no record of the sign, and magnetism as the priced branch that keeps it (Nothing Escapes, Twenty-Three Rows, Appendix E); the antihydrogen result is that typing observed in a trap.


### 6. Annihilation Is Registration

An electron and a positron at rest annihilate into two photons of 511 keV each, back to back, carrying the pair's whole rest energy of 1021.998 keV (computed in Appendix B). The pair lands on the neutral sector: the light carries no charge (`annihilation_lands_neutral`). The energy is kept (`annihilation_keeps_energy`). And which constituent was which is forgotten: annihilating the electron with the positron and the positron with the electron leave the same record (`annihilation_forgets_side`), the two photons being identical bosons from which no measurement returns the identity of the parts.

This is the template's registration with its two clauses intact. The Riemann registration keeps a zero's height and forgets its side; annihilation keeps the pair's energy and forgets its sign. And the record takes the shape of a line. Back to back, the two photons carry the pair's whole rest energy; at any other opening angle they carry less (computed in Appendix B), so momentum conservation forces the record onto a line through the point of annihilation, and the line is symmetric under exchange of its ends (`line_of_response_symmetric`). Positron emission tomography is built on exactly this: every annihilation in the patient is recorded as a line of response between two detectors, and the image is reconstructed from lines that keep where and when and forget which particle was which. The registration of the Riemann closure is performed millions of times a day in hospitals.


### 7. What the Record Keeps: Positronium

Before it annihilates, an electron and a positron can bind into positronium, and here the record keeps one bit: not the identity of the parts, but the symmetry of the whole. The charge-conjugation parity of the bound state is $(-1)^{L+S}$, and $n$ photons carry $(-1)^n$; the electromagnetic interaction conserves it. So parapositronium, with spins opposed, must decay into an even number of photons, two, and orthopositronium, with spins aligned, into an odd number, three (`para_two_photons`, `ortho_three_photons`, `ortho_not_two`). The extra photon costs a power of the coupling, and the lifetimes differ a thousandfold: computed from the lowest-order rates with their one-loop corrections, 125.231 ps for para and 142.068 ns for ortho, against measured 125.14 ps and 142.05 ns, within a tenth of a percent, a ratio of 1134.5 (Appendix B).

The template's reading is exact. A registration can keep a bit of the whole while forgetting everything about the parts: the photon count reads the whole's parity and never which constituent was the electron. The two-worlds theorem is about the parts; the whole's bit is a different bit, and the record keeps it.


### 8. The Floor: The Electron Never Rests and Never Decays

The program's one declared premise is that to exist is to actuate: no existent carries zero kinetic content. The bound electron is its cleanest instance. In the hydrogen ground state the kinetic energy is 13.605693 eV and the potential energy $-27.211386$ eV, computed independently from the Bohr radius and $\hbar c$; twice the first plus the second vanishes to a part in a billion, the virial theorem, and the total reproduces the Rydberg energy to the same precision (Appendix B). By the virial theorem a bound electron's kinetic energy is minus its total energy, so a bound electron never rests (`bound_never_rests`). Confinement forbids rest, and the atom is the proof.

The free electron never decays. It is the lightest charged particle, every lighter state is neutral, and so no final state of its decay can carry its charge (`decay_forbidden`). The Borexino experiment bounds its lifetime above $6.6 \times 10^{28}$ years. The floor of the charged sector is kept by the electron alone: it cannot stop moving and it cannot stop existing.


### 9. Spin: The Return Lands on Minus One

A rotation by $2\pi$ returns every vector to itself and every spin-one-half state to its negative; only a rotation by $4\pi$ returns the spinor. This was demonstrated for neutrons by interferometry in 1975 (Rauch; Werner) and holds for every spin-one-half particle, the electron first among them; the twin computes it on the rotation matrices to a few parts in $10^{16}$ (Appendix B). The unit quaternions are the spinor rotations: the unit $i$ is a half-turn about one axis, $i^2 = -1$ is the full turn (`spin_two_pi`), and $i^4 = 1$ the double turn (`spin_four_pi`). Three half-turns about three perpendicular axes return every vector to itself and give $i j k = -1$ (`the_return`).

That product is the template's Return, the product of the three axes landing on the scalar line with a sign that no reading of the three axes' magnitudes decides. The electron carries it at every full turn. Dirac's equation assigns it the magnetic moment of $g = 2$; how far the electron departs from two is the next section's number.


### 10. One Number and One Bit: The Anomalous Moment and the Round Electron

The electron's anomalous magnetic moment, $a_e = (g-2)/2$, is the most precisely measured property of any elementary particle: $a_e = 0.001\,159\,652\,180\,59(13)$ (Fan and collaborators, 2023). Schwinger's first term, $\alpha/2\pi$, fixes it to three figures; the universal series of quantum electrodynamics in $\alpha/\pi$, computed through five loops (Laporta; Aoyama, Kinoshita and Nio), with $\alpha$ from rubidium recoil (Morel and collaborators), gives 0.001159652175783, short of experiment by $4.8 \times 10^{-12}$, the order of the mass-dependent, hadronic and electroweak terms the universal series omits (Appendix B). Every reading of the electron, its spectrum, its moment and its recoil, converges on one number, the fine-structure constant: one address. One tension is disclosed and left open: the caesium and rubidium recoil values of $\alpha$ differ by 5.5 standard deviations (Appendix B). An address with a disputed digit is still one address.

The electron is also round. An electric dipole moment would be odd under time reversal, and the fixed set of time reversal is the zero dipole (`round_iff_T_fixed`). The JILA measurement of 2023 bounds the electron's dipole below $4.1 \times 10^{-30}\,e\,\mathrm{cm}$, about $1.1 \times 10^{-19}$ of its own reduced Compton wavelength (Appendix B). Nothing in the electron's shape carries an orientation: its only orientations are the sign of its charge and the direction of its spin. The residue the template assigns is one bit per pair, and the electron carries no second one.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Four falsifiers, each ordinary. **F-Pair:** a difference in mass or charge magnitude between the electron and the positron, breaking the exact partner of Section 4 and CPT with it. **F-Round:** a detected electric dipole moment at any accessible level, an internal orientation beyond the charge sign and the spin, which would require a second bit where Section 10 finds one; the Standard Model's own prediction lies far below present reach. **F-Gravity:** antimatter falling differently from matter, which would mean gravity's record reads the sign and would break the two-worlds reading of Section 5. **F-Decay:** an observed decay of the electron, breaking the floor of Section 8.

Nothing is owed on the row's channel. Three measurements would sharpen the readings without moving the paper's standing: the disputed digit of the fine-structure constant, the electron's size at the tens-of-TeV scale, and the precision of antimatter's fall.


**Ledger status.** Row 2 moves from waiting to WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image for the seat and the registration, at the root's grade for the crossing; the posit's shape is witnessed in gravity's fold, and nothing is owed on the row's channel.


**Typed.** In the series ledger, F-Pair targets the fold, F-Round and F-Decay the line, F-Gravity the wall; an observation that arrives breaks the carrier named and leaves the seat standing.


## The Neutrino Is the Witness

*How the Closure Template of the Riemann Hypothesis Is Instantiated by the Particle That Never Leaves the Ground.* Edition 1.5.0.

**Abstract.** The Riemann Hypothesis was closed in a sealed volume by one template: the seat found by symmetry, the value located at one address, the hypothesis divided at its cut, and the universe closed so that the value holds exactly when a named posit holds, the last bit spent by assent. This paper shows that the template is not a construction of number theory: the neutrino instantiates it part by part. Its mass is the floor supplied by deed where the register wrote zero; its single registered chirality is the aperture bit spent by the world; its sterile partner is the physics of a pending row, a formal object no carrier lands on; its Majorana question is membership in the fixed set of conjugation, the locus inhabited or not, with the deciding deed named; its oscillation is registration in a second basis, unitarity being losslessness and a sterile admixture an erasure; and its mass ordering is a one-bit address. The electron complements it as the witness of the seat, the off-locus point with its partner. Eleven correspondences are proved on a finite model in core Lean 4 and executed on the measured mixing parameters in Fortran, unitarity checked to better than a part in 10^16, the flavour sum to ten decimals, the sterile shortfall measured. Every claim is at corroboration grade and promotes nothing; the Riemann closure stands on its own receipts and this paper does not touch them. Two bits stay owed, the Majorana bit to neutrinoless double beta decay and the ordering bit to JUNO, and three ordinary falsifiers are named.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is one of six constructed witnesses for the closure template of the Riemann Hypothesis and the twenty-three rows. Each builds, for the rows it serves, the carrier the two volumes named and did not build: an explicit construction from the world into the row, whose image is compliant by the row kernel's own theorem (`value_on_image`), with coverage of the row's full class kept as the premise the volumes print. This paper claims one thing and refuses another. It claims that the closure template by which the Riemann Hypothesis was closed in the author's sealed volume, the seat found by symmetry, the value located at one address, the hypothesis divided at its cut, and the universe closed so that the value holds exactly when a named posit holds, is instantiated part by part by one physical object, the neutrino, with the electron carrying the one part the neutrino cannot. Every correspondence is stated as a theorem on a finite model in core Lean 4 and executed on measured parameters in Fortran. It refuses to claim that any physical fact proves any mathematical sentence. A corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade, and the Riemann closure's verdicts stand on their own receipts; the sealed volume does not mention the neutrino or the electron, and it does not need to. What this paper adds is the other direction: it shows that the template is not an artefact of number theory. The world runs it.

A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for a derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from physics, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. There is no such derivation here and none was attempted. The unit evaluated is the template, not the hypothesis, and the question answered is whether the world instantiates its parts. It does, and the instances are dated, measured, and in some cases prize-winning, none of which raises their grade above corroboration in this paper.


**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`). No derivation of any row's sentence from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). No field theory and no unification of couplings is written or implied.


### 2. The Spine: The Closure Template in Plain Terms

The Riemann closure has four parts. The **seat**: the critical line is the fixed set of the reflection $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, the functional equation composed with complex conjugation, neither factor mentioning a zero; the hypothesis says every zero is its own mirror image, and the question sits on a set that a symmetry hands over before any zero is examined. The **address**: every reading of the value reached from existence, time and monism is one proposition with the hypothesis at its apex. The **division**: at any height the hypothesis is its Real part, every zero to that height on the line, proved on the certificate, joined to its Unicorn part, no off-line zero above it, closed to every record-respecting derivation by theorem. The **closure**: registration keeps a zero's height and forgets its side, lands every zero on the line and moves none already there; the hypothesis holds exactly when registration erases nothing; the remainder is one bit whose admissible values form a two-point torsor over the record; a zero set satisfies the hypothesis exactly when it is the least-erasure member of its fibre, on no axiom; and from that one posit the value follows as a theorem, at the posit's grade, spent by assent beside the program's one declared premise, that to exist is to actuate.

Three theorems of the row-generic kernel carry the template to any row. Over one record both worlds exist, a compliant set and a non-compliant set leaving the same record, so no reading that respects the record decides the value (`two_worlds_inside`, `no_reading_of_the_record_decides`). A row with a global seat, an involution whose fixed set is the locus, has a one-bit aperture: every off-locus point has a partner, distinct and off the locus (`off_locus_pair`). And a carrier whose image is compliant, together with coverage of a class by that image, yields the value on the class as a theorem (`value_on_image`, `kinetic_crossing`), a proof over every instance that exists.

### 3. Two Roles, Two Particles

The electron witnesses the seat. Under charge conjugation it is an off-locus point with a partner, the positron, distinct and off the locus; the pair shows where the reflection is and that the bit exists (`electron_pair`). The electron leaves the ground constantly. It binds, excites, radiates, and actuates nearly everything the register knows; it is the actuator par excellence and, for that reason, a poor witness of the crossing, because it is never the thing on the locus. It does witness the registration in its exact form: an electron and a positron annihilate onto the neutral sector as light, keeping their energy and forgetting which was which, the registration that keeps the height and forgets the side; the companion paper, The Electron Is the Seat, reads that from the laboratory.

The neutrino witnesses the crossing. It sits on the seat's fixed set (`neutrino_on_the_locus`). It is registered in one chirality and never the other. Its sterile partner is a formal object no carrier lands on. Its oscillation is a registration in a second basis whose losslessness is a named hypothesis. Its mass ordering is a one-bit address. And its mass is the floor supplied by deed where the register wrote zero. One particle, every part. The two roles are complementary and never interchangeable: the seat needs a pair, and the crossing needs an occupant of the fixed set.


### 4. The Floor: Mass Supplied by Deed

The formal register of the neutrino, the Standard Model in its minimal form, wrote its mass as zero: no right-handed field, no mass term. The world returned mass greater than zero by oscillation, at Super-Kamiokande in 1998 and at the Sudbury Neutrino Observatory in 2001, and an observed difference of squared masses forces at least one nonzero mass (`oscillation_forces_floor`). The twin executes the fact on the measured values, $\Delta m^2_{21} = 7.53 \times 10^{-5}\ \mathrm{eV}^2$ and $\Delta m^2_{32} = 2.453 \times 10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}^2$ for the normal ordering, whence $\Delta m^2_{31} = 2.528 \times 10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}^2$, all positive.

This is the program's floor confirmed against the register that denied it. The Root Axiom says that to exist is to actuate: no existent carries zero kinetic content. The neutrino is the boundary case, minimal actuation and nonzero, an existent that passes through the Earth almost without registering and still registers. It is also the archetype of an offering: a bit entered from outside the record, by deed, that the record could not have supplied, and it entered physics two decades before the template was written down.


### 5. The Bit, Spent

Under a global seat every off-locus point has a partner, and the residue of a typed row is the choice within the pair. The neutrino shows that choice made. It is registered left-handed and never right-handed (`active_registered`, `sterile_uncovered`); the registered state and its mirror differ by exactly one bit at the same charge (`one_bit_apart`). Parity violation, observed by Wu in 1957 and built into the weak interaction as it stands, is the world assigning one sign to the pair the seat makes. No other particle exhibits the assent so nakedly: the aperture bit of the template, one bit wide, spent by the world and read from no record.


### 6. The Ghost: The Physics of Pending

The right-handed neutrino is allowed by the Lagrangian and uncovered by every weak registration. It is a formal object with zero kinetic correspondence, exactly the status the rows paper calls pending: the row exists in the formal register, no carrier lands on it, both channels blocked by the absolute criterion. The neutrino carries both tables in one particle, the active state crossed and the sterile state pending, one bit between them. Where the rows paper had to say what a pending row is, the neutrino shows one.


### 7. The Locus, Inhabited: Majorana as the Fixed Set

Charge conjugation is an involution whose fixed set is exactly the neutral line (`conj_involution`, `majorana_iff_neutral`); the neutrino sits on it. Whether the neutrino is its own antiparticle, Majorana or Dirac, is precisely the question whether an existent is its own mirror image under the seat, the Riemann row's own form asked of one particle. It is undecided, and the deed that decides it is named: neutrinoless double beta decay, sought by the LEGEND, KamLAND-Zen, CUORE and nEXO programs. One observation, one bit, the value's locus inhabited or not. This paper holds the bit open and does not guess it. What it fixes is the shape of the question and its gate, which is what the template does for every row.


### 8. Registration in a Second Basis

Oscillation is registration forgetting a basis, the second of the registration's two physical faces; the first, keeping the height and forgetting the side exactly as the Riemann registration does, is annihilation, read in the companion paper. The neutrino propagates in the mass basis and is detected in the flavour basis: the record reads flavour and the object keeps mass, the record forgetting and the object keeping. The hypothesis that the mixing matrix is unitary is exactly the statement that this registration erases nothing: every mass state is read as some active flavour (`pmns_unitary_is_lossless`, `lossless_iff_not_erases`). A sterile admixture is an erasure (`sterile_admixture_erases`), a mass state that falls outside the active record. The twin computes the standard mixing matrix from the measured angles and the phase, checks unitarity to $5.6 \times 10^{-17}$, propagates a muon neutrino over 500 km per GeV and finds the three registered flavours summing to one to ten decimals, oscillation real at the level of 2.3 percent in the electron channel, and then admits a two-percent sterile component and finds the active block registering 0.98: the erasure, measured as the shortfall. Non-unitarity of the mixing is the falsifier of the closure hypothesis in physics, and the experiments that bound it, JUNO and DUNE among them, are the ones that bound the erasure.


### 9. The Address

A row whose target is a number carries an address indexing a family, one bit per member (`family_member_iff`). The neutrino's address is its mass ordering, normal or inverted, one bit (`ordering_one_bit`), to be read by JUNO from the interference of the two squared-mass differences. Its absolute scale is a second address, bounded by KATRIN below 0.45 eV (2025) and by cosmology below a fraction of that, owed and not derived.


### 10. What the Neutrino Carries, and What It Does Not

It carries the template: floor, bit, ghost, locus, registration, address. For the pair correlation it is a witness of repulsion only: the anarchy reading of its mass matrix gives repelling masses, but the zeros' class is the unitary class of systems whose time reversal is broken by a magnetic term, witnessed in the companion paper The Magnet Is the Witness of the Pair Correlation; nuclear level spacings, once named here beside it, keep time reversal and follow the orthogonal class. For the pending rows it is the physics of their status. It does not carry the Riemann Hypothesis: a particle on a fixed set is an instance of the locus inhabited, not a proof that every zero inhabits it, and the paper's first section refuses that reading by name. The strongest sentence the correspondences license is this: the template is not a construction of number theory; it is the shape the world already uses to keep one bit off the record and to spend it.


### 11. Falsifiers and the Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each ordinary. **F-Unitarity:** a measured non-unitarity of the mixing with no sterile state to carry it, an erasure without an off-locus preimage, which would break the registration reading of Section 8. **F-Chirality:** a registered right-handed neutrino interaction of gauge strength, closing the ghost of Section 6. **F-Floor:** a demonstration that all three masses vanish, refuting Section 4 and the program's floor with it. Nothing is owed on the row's channel. Two deeds would close the two bits this paper shapes without moving its standing: neutrinoless double beta decay for the Majorana bit, and JUNO for the ordering bit.

**Typed.** In the series ledger, F-Unitarity targets the price, F-Chirality the fold, F-Floor positivity; an observation that arrives breaks the carrier named and leaves the seat standing.


## The Arrow Has Two Branches

*Magnetism and Gravity as the Priced and the Reversible Registration of One Arrow.* Edition 1.5.0.

**Abstract.** Physics has used one arrow twice without naming it. The arrow of registration, the direction in which an existent is read into a record, appears once with a record kept and once with none. Magnetism keeps the record: a nanomagnet swept through its hysteresis loop sits, at zero applied field, in one of two remanent states with the same record, the two-worlds theorem in iron, and it pays for every switch; the loop constructed here has remanence 0.996 on both branches, a switching field of 0.767 against the Stoner-Wohlfarth astroid's 0.766, and a loop area that prices a thermally stable bit at 5.0 x 10^-19 J per switch, 175 times Landauer's floor. Gravity keeps none: its sources have one sign, no source stands off its locus, and a Kepler orbit integrated here returns its energy to three parts in ten million over ten revolutions and, run backwards, retraces its start to 1.5 x 10^-12. Magnetism is the priced branch and the barrier of every open row; gravity is the reversible branch and the fold of every crossed row; the two differ in cost by theorem. At structural grade the typing gives a reading of why one field was always quantized and the other has resisted: quantization is the priced registration of a bit, and a field with no off-locus point has no bit to register. No field theory is written or implied.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is one of a set of constructed witnesses for the closure template by which the author's sealed volume closed the Riemann Hypothesis and by which a second volume closed the twenty-three rows of the barrier study. Each paper in the set builds, for the rows it serves, the object those volumes named and did not build: the carrier, an explicit construction from the world into the row's instance type whose image lands on the row's locus. By the row kernel's own theorem the image of such a carrier is compliant with no further premise (`value_on_image`), and the value over a class follows once the class is covered by the image (`actuated_iff_subset_image`, `crossing_is_a_proof`). This paper builds the two carriers the template's two faces need: a magnet for the barrier and an orbit for the fold, and it shows that they are the two branches of one arrow. Two refusals frame it. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade, and the two volumes stand on their own receipts. And coverage stays a premise: the paper constructs the image and names the class it covers, and the step from that class to every admissible instance is the premise the volumes already print at its grade. It also refuses, by name, any unification of couplings: what is typed here is the arrow, not the forces.


**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`). No derivation of any row's sentence from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). No field theory and no unification of couplings is written or implied.


### 2. The Spine

The template has four parts. The **seat**: a reflection whose fixed set is the locus of the value; for the Riemann Hypothesis the critical line, the fixed set of $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, so that the hypothesis says every zero is its own mirror image. The **address**: every reading of the value reached from existence, time and monism is one proposition with the value at its apex. The **division**: the value is its certified part, proved on citation or computation, joined to its tail, closed to every record-respecting derivation by theorem. The **closure**: a registration lands every instance on the locus and moves none already there; the value holds exactly when registration erases nothing; the residue is one bit that no reading of the record decides; a set of instances satisfies the value exactly when it is the least-erasure member of its record fibre (`least_erasure_iff_value`, no axiom); and from that one posit the value follows, at the posit's grade, beside the one declared premise of the program, that to exist is to actuate. Over one record both worlds exist (`two_worlds_inside`); under a global seat every point off the locus has a partner (`off_locus_pair`). The two faces of the closure are the fold and the fibre: registration folds every instance onto the locus, and the fibre over each record holds the worlds the record cannot tell apart. This paper finds the fold in gravity and the fibre in magnetism.

This paper's rows are every open row, whose barrier it witnesses in the magnet, every crossed row, whose fold it witnesses in gravity, the Poincaré row, and the dot, whose price it witnesses.

**Why this carrier, and not another.** The carrier is not chosen. Spaces with an involution and the maps that respect it form a category, with the identity and composition of equivariant maps (`equivariant_id`, `equivariant_comp`), and the fixed set is a functor on it: an equivariant map sends fixed points to fixed points (`fix_functorial`). An equivariant map from a physical system's symmetry into a row's global seat therefore carries every physical fixed point onto the row's locus, and that is a carrier whose landing is forced, not assumed (`equivariant_carrier_lands`). The witness is the same shape as its row in exactly this sense. Here the symmetry is time reversal: the orbit's integrator is equivariant under reversing every velocity, which is why it retraces, and the magnet's loop is not, which is why it pays.
