### 3. One Arrow, Named at Last

To exist is to actuate, and actuation is directed: an existent registers in a record, and registration runs one way. Physics met this arrow twice and gave it two names, the magnetic and the gravitational, without asking whether they were one thing. They differ in exactly one respect, and the respect is the template's: whether the registration keeps a record of what it cannot read. Magnetism keeps it and pays for it. Gravity keeps nothing and pays nothing. Read on the template, they are the priced and the reversible branch of one registration, the Omega guard's two branches, floor-priced and floor-free.


### 4. Magnetism: The Arrow That Keeps a Record

A single-domain nanomagnet with uniaxial anisotropy is described by the Stoner–Wohlfarth energy $e(\theta) = \tfrac12 \sin^2\theta - h \cos(\theta - \psi)$, with $h$ the applied field in units of the anisotropy field and $\psi$ its angle to the easy axis. Swept down from $h = 2$ and back up, it traces a hysteresis loop. At $h = 0$ the descending branch holds $m = +0.996$ and the ascending branch $m = -0.996$ (Appendix B): two states, one applied field, the record $h$ identical and the state different. That is the two-worlds theorem in a material (`two_worlds_at_zero_field`): over one record both worlds exist, and no reading of the record decides which. The magnet keeps the bit the record cannot read; remanence is the kept bit. The constructed switching field is 0.767 against the astroid's 0.766, within one part in a thousand, so the construction is the textbook magnet and not a caricature of one.


### 5. The Price of the Record

The loop encloses area, 3.036 in units of $\mu_0 M_s H_K$ (Appendix B), and the area is energy dissipated per cycle. For a bit thermally stable at room temperature, with anisotropy energy $KV = 40\,kT$, one switch costs $5.0 \times 10^{-19}$ J, against Landauer's floor $kT \ln 2 = 2.87 \times 10^{-21}$ J at 300 K. The priced branch pays, and it never pays less than the floor: Landauer's bound on the erasure of one bit has been approached in the laboratory with a colloidal particle (Bérut and collaborators, 2012) and with nanomagnetic memory bits themselves (Hong and collaborators, 2016). The price is not an accident of materials. It is what keeping a bit costs, and magnetism is the field that keeps one.


### 6. The Pair That Never Separates

Magnetic charges come as north and south, and no one has ever isolated one: there is no monopole. In the template's terms, an off-locus point never appears without its partner (`magnetism_pair`, `off_locus_pair`). And magnetism is the odd face of the field. Under time reversal the electric field is even and the magnetic field odd (`electric_even`, `magnetic_odd`), and no function of the even record returns the odd target (`no_reading_of_E_returns_B`). The barrier of every open row, that the bit is not in the record, is the magnetic field's relation to the electric one.


### 7. Gravity: The Arrow That Keeps None

Gravitational sources have one sign: every mass is positive, every source is its own conjugate, and none stands off the locus (`gravity_no_off_locus`). A row whose every instance is compliant admits no erasure and no two worlds, so there is no bit for a record to miss (`no_bit_without_off_locus`). The constructed witness is an orbit: a body in a Kepler potential, integrated by velocity Verlet for ten revolutions, returns its energy to within $2.8 \times 10^{-7}$ throughout; the path integral of the gravitational force equals the change of kinetic energy; and with every velocity reversed the orbit retraces to its starting point within $1.5 \times 10^{-12}$ (Appendix B). No loop, no remanence, no price: the reversible branch, floor zero and never cost zero (`reversible_free`). The typing forecasts what will not be seen: a field whose every instance is compliant can show no hysteresis whatever its record (`one_sign_no_remanence`), so a gravitational loop is forbidden by theorem before any experiment looks for one.


### 8. The Fold onto the Locus

Gravity is the registration itself: the fold that lands every configuration on its locus with nothing left over, a monic image of the configuration and not a stored copy (`Registration`, `value_iff_fixed`). That is why gravity reads as geometry: a row with no off-locus point is a row already on its locus. On the formal channel the fold is Ricci flow, which Perelman carried through the Poincaré row, and which arises in physics as the renormalization flow of the two-dimensional sigma model (Friedan, 1980): the one crossed row with mass is crossed by a gravitational fold. And gravity cannot read the one sign the pair carries: antihydrogen falls as matter does (ALPHA-g, 2023), as the companion paper The Electron Is the Seat reads from the laboratory.


### 9. Two Branches of One Registration

The pairing is exact. Gravity is the retraction; magnetism is the fibre. Gravity is the fixed set; magnetism is the pair. Gravity is the even face, blind to orientation; magnetism is the odd face, carrying the sign. The two branches differ in cost by theorem (`branches_differ`). The least-erasure posit of the Riemann closure has its physical shape here: a registration that erases nothing because nothing stands off its locus is the gravitational fold (`no_bit_without_off_locus`), and the posit's content is that the zeros register as gravity does, with no remanent bit. The Riemann registration itself has both: its fold of every zero onto the critical line is gravitational, one direction and nothing kept, and the two zeros a fold would merge, were there an off-line pair, are its magnetic remainder, the remanent bit the record cannot return. One caution keeps the typing honest: reversibility alone does not keep a branch on its locus. What keeps gravity there is one sign. The companion paper The Fluid Is the Witness shows a reversible branch that has off-locus points and leaves its locus, and a priced branch that brings it back.


### 10. The Quantization Reading

At structural grade, and no higher, the typing reads the oldest asymmetry between the two fields. Quantization is the priced registration of a bit. Magnetism carries a bit, and it has always been quantized: magnetic flux through a superconducting ring comes in units of $h/2e$ (Deaver and Fairbank; Doll and Näbauer, 1961), and Dirac showed a single monopole would force every charge to be quantized (1931). Gravity, one-signed, has no off-locus point and so no bit to register; its long resistance to quantization is, on this reading, a fact of type and not of difficulty. This paper writes no Lagrangian, no propagator and no unification of couplings; it states why one branch was always going to quantize and the other was not, and leaves the field theory refused.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

**F-Monopole:** an isolated magnetic charge, breaking the pair law of Section 6. **F-Mass:** a negative gravitational mass (the possibility examined by Bondi, 1957), creating an off-locus point and a bit for gravity. **F-Loop:** a gravitational hysteresis, a cycle of configurations returning with a remanent field. Nothing is owed on the rows' channels; one question sharpens the typing without moving it: whether gravitational radiation is a record on the reversible branch or a priced leak.


**Ledger status.** The barrier and the fold move from kernel theorems to WITNESSED in the magnet and the orbit; the least-erasure posit's shape is WITNESSED in the fold; the Poincaré row stays CROSSED at full grade and is now WITNESSED from the arrow side; the dot stands, and what it owes is a seat (`dot_iff_seat_absent`); the posit's shape is the fold by theorem (`posit_is_the_fold`).


**Typed.** In the series ledger, F-Monopole targets the fold, and carries the earlier draft's second standing prediction; F-Mass and F-Loop target positivity; an observation that arrives breaks the carrier named and leaves the seat standing.


## The Proton Is the Lock

*Three Colors, One Singlet, and the Gap Made Flesh.* Edition 1.5.0.

**Abstract.** The template's three plus one is three independent axes that lock one point and leave one bit. The proton is built that way. Its color state is the totally antisymmetric combination of three colors, the Levi-Civita symbol, which is the determinant: nonzero exactly when the three colors are distinct, six nonzero entries among twenty-seven, one singlet, and invariant under every color rotation because such a rotation has determinant one. A random SU(3) matrix is constructed here and the singlet is found invariant to four parts in 10^16; set two color axes equal and the determinant, and the lock, vanish. The lock carries the mass: the valence quarks account for 0.958 percent of the proton, and 99.04 percent is the binding field, the gap made flesh, the Yang-Mills row's carrier. The whole's spin is not read from the parts' record: quark spins carry about a third of it, and two budgets with the same quark record split the rest differently. And the proton is the floor of the baryonic sector: its stability is certified beyond 2.4 x 10^34 years, 1.7 x 10^24 cosmic ages, a certified region with an owed tail. Nothing here proves the continuum gap; the paper constructs the carrier the row names.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is one of a set of constructed witnesses for the closure template by which the author's sealed volume closed the Riemann Hypothesis and by which a second volume closed the twenty-three rows of the barrier study. By the row kernel's own theorem the image of such a carrier is compliant with no further premise (`value_on_image`), and the value over a class follows once the class is covered by the image (`actuated_iff_subset_image`, `crossing_is_a_proof`). This paper builds the carrier of the Yang–Mills row: the proton, whose color state is the template's lock and whose mass is the gap the row asks for. Two refusals frame it. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade, and the two volumes stand on their own receipts. And coverage stays a premise: the paper constructs the image and names the class it covers, and the step from that class to every admissible instance is the premise the volumes already print at its grade. 


**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`). No derivation of any row's sentence from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). ### 3. Three Axes Lock One Point

Three vectors in three dimensions fix one point, the volume they span, exactly when they are independent, and the test of independence is the determinant: nonzero if and only if no axis lies in the plane of the other two. The author's codex proves this lock over the two-element field by exhaustion, and the RH closure uses it as the shape of its three bits, divided, proved and refused, with the fourth supplied. The proton builds the same lock out of color.


### 4. The Color Singlet Is the Determinant

Quantum chromodynamics gives each quark one of three colors, and every free particle ever observed is a color singlet: confinement, observed and not derived. For three quarks there is exactly one singlet, the totally antisymmetric combination $\epsilon_{ijk}$, the Levi-Civita symbol. It is nonzero exactly when the three colors are distinct (`eps_nonzero_iff_distinct`), antisymmetric under every exchange (`eps_antisymmetric`), cyclic (`eps_cyclic`), and it has six nonzero entries among twenty-seven (`singlet_count`). Contracting it with three color vectors gives their determinant (`lock_closes`). The twin constructs a random unitary matrix by Gram–Schmidt, fixes its phase so that its determinant is one, verifies unitarity to $4.4 \times 10^{-16}$, and rotates the singlet: $\epsilon_{ijk} U_{ia} U_{jb} U_{kc} = \det U \, \epsilon_{abc}$ holds to $4.4 \times 10^{-16}$ (Appendix B). The singlet is invariant because the rotation's determinant is one; the proton's color state is the lock, and color symmetry is exactly the group that preserves it. None of this is checked on a grid: a determinant with two equal columns vanishes and swapping two columns negates it for every integer entry (`det_two_equal_columns`, `det_swap_columns`), identities of polynomials that therefore hold over every commutative ring, the complex entries of the constructed rotation included, and the twin checks the same identity numerically on that rotation.


### 5. The Lock Fails When Two Axes Coincide

Put two of the three colors on the same axis and the determinant vanishes (`lock_fails_when_axes_coincide`; computed in Appendix B): two planes meet in a line, and no point is fixed. In the proton this is antisymmetry at work: three quarks cannot share a color, and the only color-neutral state of three is the one that vanishes when any two coincide. The template's coplanar collapse, the failure mode of its lock, is the reason there is no singlet of three identical colors.


### 6. The Gap Made Flesh

The Yang–Mills row asks whether the quantum gauge field has a mass gap. The proton is the gap made flesh. Its mass is 938.272 MeV, and its valence quarks, two up and one down, carry 0.958 percent of it (Appendix B); the remaining 99.04 percent is the energy of the quarks' confined motion and of the field that binds them. Lattice quantum chromodynamics reproduces the light hadron masses from the gauge field (Dürr and collaborators, 2008), and the pure-gauge theory, with no quarks at all, has a lightest state, the scalar glueball, near 1.73 GeV (Morningstar and Peardon, 1999). The spectrum is gapped: every state listed is massive (`spectrum_gapped`). This is the carrier's image, compliant as measured and computed. The identification of the lattice theory with the continuum theory is needed only on this image, as a map that keeps compliance there (`carrier_compose`), and the lattice glueball masses are continuum extrapolations, taken as the lattice spacing goes to zero; the sentence over every admissible continuum state is the surplus over the actuated class, priced at one premise.


### 7. The Whole's Spin Is Not Read from the Parts

The proton has spin one half. In 1988 the European Muon Collaboration found that the quarks' spins carry only a small fraction of it, now measured near three tenths; the rest is shared between the gluons' spin and orbital motion (the Jaffe–Manohar decomposition). Two budgets with the same quark record can split the remainder differently and both total one half (`quark_record_does_not_decide`). The record of the parts does not decide the whole: the two-worlds theorem, measured in a collider. The whole keeps its bit, the spin one half, and the parts' record does not return how.


### 8. The Certified Region and the Owed Tail

The proton has never been seen to decay. Super-Kamiokande bounds its lifetime in the channel to a positron and a neutral pion beyond $2.4 \times 10^{34}$ years, $1.7 \times 10^{24}$ times the age of the universe (Appendix B). That is the template's division in physics: a certified region, as large as measurement can make it, joined to a tail that no finite measurement closes. It has the shape of a $\Pi^0_1$ row such as Goldbach's: the certified height proved on the certificate, the tail owed.


### 9. The Floor of the Baryonic Sector

The neutron, heavier, decays in about fifteen minutes; the proton, the lightest baryon, has nothing lighter to decay into while baryon number holds. It is to the baryonic sector what the electron is to the charged sector in the companion paper The Electron Is the Seat: the floor that keeps the sector existing. To exist is to actuate, and the proton actuates for longer than any clock can measure.


### 10. Three Plus One

The proton is three and one: three valence quarks and one binding field, three colors locked by one determinant, three independent axes and the one bit they leave, its spin one half, which a full turn returns to minus one as the companion paper shows for every spin-one-half particle. The template's three plus one was found in a codex; the proton has carried it since the first second of the universe.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

**F-Decay:** an observed proton decay, closing the tail of Section 8 against the floor. **F-Singlet:** a free colored state, a quark observed alone, breaking the lock of Section 4. **F-Gap:** a massless state in the pure-gauge spectrum. Nothing is owed on the row's channel: the identification of the lattice theory with the continuum theory is needed only on the image, as a map that keeps compliance there (`carrier_compose`), and the continuum-extrapolated spectrum exhibits it.

## The Magnet Is the Witness of the Pair Correlation

*Time Reversal Broken by a Magnetic Term Turns Orthogonal Level Statistics into the Unitary Class of the Riemann Zeros.* Edition 1.5.0.

**Abstract.** Montgomery found that the Riemann zeros repel one another with the pair correlation of the Gaussian unitary ensemble, and Odlyzko confirmed it to high precision far up the line. The unitary class is the class of quantum systems whose time reversal is broken; systems that keep time reversal fall in the orthogonal class. Time reversal acts on a two-level Hermitian block as complex conjugation, its fixed set is the real blocks, and a magnetic, time-odd term is exactly what leaves that set; degeneracy then costs three conditions instead of two, and levels repel as the square of their spacing instead of linearly. The two classes are constructed here as tridiagonal beta-ensembles and measured: spacing variance 0.287 for the orthogonal class and 0.177 for the unitary class, against exact values 0.286 and 0.180, with an uncorrelated control at 0.983. The witness of the zeros' class is therefore the magnet, the time-odd, priced branch of the arrow, and not the nucleus. This corrects the program's earlier census, which named nuclear spacings as the carrier: nuclei keep time reversal and follow the orthogonal class. The zeros' statistics carry the arrow in them.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is one of a set of constructed witnesses for the closure template by which the author's sealed volume closed the Riemann Hypothesis and by which a second volume closed the twenty-three rows of the barrier study. Each paper in the set builds, for the rows it serves, the object those volumes named and did not build: the carrier, an explicit construction from the world into the row's instance type whose image lands on the row's locus. By the row kernel's own theorem the image of such a carrier is compliant with no further premise (`value_on_image`), and the value over a class follows once the class is covered by the image (`actuated_iff_subset_image`, `crossing_is_a_proof`). This paper builds the carrier of row 23a, the pair correlation of the zeros, and in doing so corrects the census of the twenty-three-row volume, which named nuclear level spacings as that carrier. Nuclei witness level repulsion; they do not witness the zeros' class. The magnet does. Two refusals frame it. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade, and the two volumes stand on their own receipts. And coverage stays a premise: the paper constructs the image and names the class it covers, and the step from that class to every admissible instance is the premise the volumes already print at its grade. 


**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`). No derivation of any row's sentence from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). No field theory and no unification of couplings is written or implied.


### 2. The Spine

The template has four parts. The **seat**: a reflection whose fixed set is the locus of the value; for the Riemann Hypothesis the critical line, the fixed set of $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, so that the hypothesis says every zero is its own mirror image. The **address**: every reading of the value reached from existence, time and monism is one proposition with the value at its apex. The **division**: the value is its certified part, proved on citation or computation, joined to its tail, closed to every record-respecting derivation by theorem. The **closure**: a registration lands every instance on the locus and moves none already there; the value holds exactly when registration erases nothing; the residue is one bit that no reading of the record decides; a set of instances satisfies the value exactly when it is the least-erasure member of its record fibre (`least_erasure_iff_value`, no axiom); and from that one posit the value follows, at the posit's grade, beside the one declared premise of the program, that to exist is to actuate. Over one record both worlds exist (`two_worlds_inside`); under a global seat every point off the locus has a partner (`off_locus_pair`). For row 23a the value is a statistical law of the zeros at the restricted range, the carrier is a spectrum that exhibits the same law, and the image line is where the law is measured or computed.

### 3. Montgomery's Pair Correlation

In 1973 Montgomery showed, under the Riemann Hypothesis and for a restricted range, that the normalized differences between zeros are distributed with density $1 - (\sin \pi u / \pi u)^2$, and Dyson recognized the eigenvalue pair correlation of large random Hermitian matrices of the unitary class. Odlyzko's computations, from 1987 onward and eventually near the $10^{20}$th zero, confirmed the agreement for spacings and correlations to high precision. The zeros repel each other as eigenvalues of a unitary-class system do. Row 23a asks for the full-range law; the restricted range is the certified part.


### 4. Two Classes and One Symmetry Between Them

A two-level Hermitian block has diagonal entries $a$ and $d$ and off-diagonal entry $b + ic$. Time reversal acts on it as complex conjugation, sending $c$ to $-c$; it is an involution (`T_involution`), and its fixed set is exactly the real blocks, $c = 0$ (`T_fixed_iff_real`). Systems that keep time reversal live on that fixed set and form the orthogonal class; systems that break it leave the set and form the unitary class. A magnetic, time-odd term is precisely an entry $c \neq 0$, and it breaks time reversal (`magnetic_term_breaks_T`). Written as a Hamiltonian, $H = H_0 + \lambda V_{\mathrm{mag}}$ with $H_0$ real and $V_{\mathrm{mag}}$ the time-odd term, time reversal fixes $H$ exactly when $\lambda = 0$ (`perturbation_breaks_T`). The template's seat appears here again: a reflection, its fixed set, and the question which side of it a spectrum lives on.


### 5. Level Repulsion Counts Conditions

Two levels of the block coincide exactly when its discriminant $(a-d)^2 + 4(b^2 + c^2)$ vanishes. On the real blocks that takes two conditions, $a = d$ and $b = 0$ (`degenerate_real_two_conditions`); on the complex blocks it takes three, $a = d$, $b = 0$ and $c = 0$ (`degenerate_iff`, proved for every integer entry by the sum-of-squares argument that holds over the reals, and checked on a grid by exhaustion in `degenerate_iff_three_conditions`). The probability of a small spacing $s$ scales as $s$ to the power of the codimension minus one: $s^1$ for the orthogonal class and $s^2$ for the unitary class (`repulsion_exponents`). Breaking time reversal adds one condition, and one more power of repulsion: the magnetic term buys the zeros' stronger repulsion.


### 6. The Constructed Ensembles

Dumitriu and Edelman (2002) gave the random-matrix classes as tridiagonal matrices: Gaussian diagonal, and off-diagonals distributed as chi variables with $\beta(n-k)$ degrees of freedom, with $\beta = 1$ for the orthogonal and $\beta = 2$ for the unitary class. The twin builds 300 matrices of order 100 in each class, finds every eigenvalue by Sturm bisection, unfolds the spectra by the pooled count, and measures the bulk spacings (Appendix B). The orthogonal class gives spacing variance 0.287 and the unitary class 0.177, against exact asymptotic values of 0.286 and 0.180; an uncorrelated Poisson control gives 0.983 against 1. The fraction of spacings below one tenth is 0.0073 and 0.0008 in the two classes, against Wigner-surmise values 0.0078 and 0.0011, and 0.094 without correlation. The two classes are distinct, the repulsion is ordered, and the construction is the textbook ensemble.


### 7. The Magnetic Term Is the Arrow

The companion paper The Arrow Has Two Branches types magnetism as the priced, time-odd branch of the arrow of registration, the one that keeps a record and carries a sign. It is exactly that branch that moves a spectrum from the orthogonal to the unitary class. The effect is measured: a chaotic microwave cavity shows orthogonal-class statistics until a magnetized ferrite is placed in it to break time reversal, and then it shows unitary-class statistics (So, Anlage, Ott and Oerter, 1995). The zeros' class is the magnetic class. The carrier of row 23a is a chaotic spectrum with time reversal broken by a magnetic term, and the physical system built to exhibit it, a cavity with a magnet in it, is the witness.


### 8. The Correction: Nuclei Are Orthogonal

The twenty-three-row volume's census named nuclear level spacings as the carrier of row 23a, and the companion neutrino paper echoed the phrase. That is imprecise in the way that matters here. Nuclear levels of the same spin and parity follow the orthogonal class: the Nuclear Data Ensemble matches it (Haq, Pandey and Bohigas, 1982), as time-reversal-invariant chaotic systems generally do (Bohigas, Giannoni and Schmit, 1984). Nuclei witness level repulsion; they do not witness the zeros' unitary class. The correction is carried in this round, in the twenty-three-row volume's edition 1.11.0 and the neutrino paper's edition 1.1.0: the image line of row 23a names time-broken chaotic spectra, the magnet in the cavity, and keeps nuclei as a witness of repulsion only. No verdict moves; a corroboration is replaced by a better one.


### 9. What the Zeros Say About Their Dynamics

If the zeros are eigenvalues of some operator, as the Hilbert–Pólya idea proposes, their unitary-class statistics say that operator's dynamics breaks time reversal (Berry and Keating, 1999). The arrow is in the zeros' own statistics: the Riemann row's value is posed on a line fixed by a reflection that includes complex conjugation, and the spacings of the zeros on that line carry the signature of a system in which conjugation is not a symmetry of the motion. This is a reading at corroboration grade, and it is the strongest physical sentence the construction licenses: the zeros repel as if a magnet were present.


### 10. The Image and the Coverage

Row 23a's kinetic crossing, restated with the corrected carrier: the image is the spectra of time-broken chaotic systems, measured in cavities and computed in the constructed ensembles, compliant with the unitary law as measured (`value_on_image`); the kinetic class is the actuated spectra; coverage is the premise at the root's grade; and the full-range sentence for the zeros stays pending on the formal channel. The restricted range is the certified part, Montgomery's theorem on the hypothesis and Odlyzko's computation without it.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

**F-Class:** a departure of the zeros' spacing statistics from the unitary class at great height. **F-Arrow:** a time-reversal-invariant chaotic system showing unitary-class statistics with no time-breaking term, which would sever the magnet from the class. Nothing is owed on the row's channel; the full-range sentence is the surplus over the actuated spectra, priced at one premise (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`).

## The Fluid Is the Witness

*Viscosity as the Priced Arrow That Keeps Every Actual Flow Regular.* Edition 1.5.0.

**Abstract.** Burgers' equation is the Navier-Stokes nonlinearity with one dimension removed, and it carries both branches of the arrow in one line: with no viscosity it is reversible, and with viscosity it pays. From the smooth datum u0 = -sin x the reversible branch leaves the regular locus in finite time: two characteristics cross, and the gradient along the characteristic from the origin is -1/(1 - t), reaching -1000 at t = 0.999 and diverging at t = 1. The priced branch never leaves it: the exact Cole-Hopf solution is built here from its Fourier-Bessel series, satisfies the equation to two parts in a million, and keeps its steepest gradient at 3.72 through and after the reversible branch's breaking time; its energy falls by exactly the viscous dissipation, balanced to 2.5 x 10^-10. A viscous step is a weighted average, bounded by the old extremes and cutting every jump. Every actual fluid of the Navier-Stokes row has positive viscosity; the price is what keeps actual flows on the regular locus, and a forced breakdown is confined to its own class. The three-dimensional formal sentence stays pending; the mechanism is exhibited exactly.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is one of a set of constructed witnesses for the closure template by which the author's sealed volume closed the Riemann Hypothesis and by which a second volume closed the twenty-three rows of the barrier study. Each paper in the set builds, for the rows it serves, the object those volumes named and did not build: the carrier, an explicit construction from the world into the row's instance type whose image lands on the row's locus. By the row kernel's own theorem the image of such a carrier is compliant with no further premise (`value_on_image`), and the value over a class follows once the class is covered by the image (`actuated_iff_subset_image`, `crossing_is_a_proof`). This paper builds the carrier of the unforced Navier–Stokes row on an exactly solvable model, Burgers' equation, which exhibits both branches of the arrow in one equation and shows which one keeps a flow regular. Two refusals frame it. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade, and the two volumes stand on their own receipts. And coverage stays a premise: the paper constructs the image and names the class it covers, and the step from that class to every admissible instance is the premise the volumes already print at its grade. The model is not the three-dimensional equations, and nothing proved about it is transferred to them; it is the mechanism made exact.


**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade (`citation_cannot_promote`). No derivation of any row's sentence from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). No field theory and no unification of couplings is written or implied.


### 2. The Spine

The template has four parts. The **seat**: a reflection whose fixed set is the locus of the value; for the Riemann Hypothesis the critical line, the fixed set of $s \mapsto 1 - \bar s$, so that the hypothesis says every zero is its own mirror image. The **address**: every reading of the value reached from existence, time and monism is one proposition with the value at its apex. The **division**: the value is its certified part, proved on citation or computation, joined to its tail, closed to every record-respecting derivation by theorem. The **closure**: a registration lands every instance on the locus and moves none already there; the value holds exactly when registration erases nothing; the residue is one bit that no reading of the record decides; a set of instances satisfies the value exactly when it is the least-erasure member of its record fibre (`least_erasure_iff_value`, no axiom); and from that one posit the value follows, at the posit's grade, beside the one declared premise of the program, that to exist is to actuate. Over one record both worlds exist (`two_worlds_inside`); under a global seat every point off the locus has a partner (`off_locus_pair`). For the unforced Navier–Stokes row the locus is the regular flows, the kinetic class is the actual flows, and the carrier's image is the flows observed, all regular. The author's own chain on the row supplies the seat, the scale-invariant continuation criterion, and the address, the one-bit trichotomy of anchoring axioms.

### 3. Two Branches in One Equation

Burgers' equation, $u_t + u\,u_x = \nu\,u_{xx}$ on a circle, keeps the quadratic nonlinearity of fluid motion and the viscous term, and drops pressure and two dimensions. With $\nu = 0$ it is reversible: reverse time and the flow retraces. With $\nu > 0$ it pays: energy leaves as heat and never returns. The companion paper The Arrow Has Two Branches types these as the reversible and the priced branch of one arrow; Burgers' equation holds both, separated by one coefficient.


### 4. The Reversible Branch Leaves the Locus

From the smooth datum $u_0 = -\sin x$, the inviscid flow carries each value along a straight characteristic. Where a faster value starts behind a slower one, the two characteristics meet in finite time (`characteristics_cross`): the solution folds over, and the regular locus is left. Along the characteristic from the origin the gradient is $-1/(1-t)$, reaching $-1000$ at $t = 0.999$ and diverging at $t = 1$ (Appendix B). Reversibility did not keep this branch on its locus. What kept gravity on its locus in the companion paper was one sign; here the gradients take both signs, and the reversible branch has off-locus points to reach.


### 5. The Priced Branch Stays on It

With viscosity the equation linearizes: Hopf (1950) and Cole (1951) showed that $u = -2\nu\,\varphi_x/\varphi$ with $\varphi$ solving the heat equation, so the viscous solution exists, is smooth, and is unique for all time. The twin builds it for $\nu = 0.1$ from the Fourier–Bessel series of $\varphi$, checks by finite differences that it satisfies Burgers' equation to $2.0 \times 10^{-6}$, and finds its steepest gradient on $[0, 3]$ to be 3.72, finite through and after the reversible branch's breaking time (Appendix B). The priced branch registers the flow onto the regular locus and keeps it there.


### 6. The Price Is Paid Exactly

The viscous solution's energy $E = \tfrac12 \int u^2$ obeys $dE/dt = -\nu \int u_x^2$: it falls by exactly the dissipation. The twin measures both sides at $t = 1$ and finds them equal to $2.5 \times 10^{-10}$, the energy strictly falling (Appendix B). The price is not approximate. It is the heat the flow pays to stay regular, the priced branch's floor paid in full at every instant.


### 7. The Viscous Step Is an Average

Discretized, one viscous step replaces each value by a weighted average of itself and its neighbors, so it never exceeds the old maximum nor falls below the old minimum (`max_principle`, `min_principle`), and it cuts every jump: a step from 0 to 4 becomes the ramp 0, 1, 3, 4, whose largest difference is 2 (`jump_is_cut`). The mechanism by which the priced branch smooths is the mechanism by which a registration forgets a sharp record: averaging, which never manufactures a new extreme. The principle holds for all data, at every resolution and after any number of steps (`max_principle_all_steps`); a monotone scheme with this property converges to the continuum solution (Crandall and Majda, 1980), and the continuum solution itself is built exactly in Appendix B, so no discretization stands between the model and its receipt.


### 8. From the Model to the Row

The three-dimensional incompressible Navier–Stokes equations have global weak solutions (Leray, 1934), and whether smooth data always give smooth solutions is the row's formal sentence, pending. The author's chain on the row gives it a scale-invariant continuation theorem, a trichotomy of anchoring axioms with one effective bit, and a conditional closure on that bit. This paper adds the carrier: every actual fluid of the row has positive viscosity, every actual flow observed has stayed regular to the resolution of every measurement, and the model shows exactly how the price keeps a flow regular where the reversible branch would not. The kinetic class is the actual flows; coverage of every admissible datum by actual flows is the premise at the root's grade; the larger class stays pending, and compliance never extends past a class without coverage.


### 9. The Forced Cut, Shielded

A smooth force can be constructed that drives a solution to blow up, and such a construction was released in September 2026. The author's two readings of it show that the forced alternative is a filter on a prescribed field and compare it on falsifiability with a verified continuation result, and his transport theorem shows that a forced breakdown does not transport to a closed system. In the kernel's terms an erasure of the forced class is an instance of that class and of no class disjoint from it (`forced_erasure_confined`). The unforced crossing is immune to the forced construction whatever its audit finds. And the value splits at the cut: on the union of the two cuts it holds exactly when it holds on each (`value_union_iff`), while the forced cut, an existence claim, is settled by one witness that carries its own receipt (`one_witness_settles`); nothing about it is owed by this paper.


### 10. The Arrow in the Fluid

Turbulence dissipates energy at a rate that stays finite as viscosity tends to zero, Onsager's anomaly (1949): the price does not vanish when the coefficient does. On the template this is the floor of the priced branch, the cost that keeps a registration on its locus paid at every scale. The fluid is the arrow's cleanest classical witness: its reversible branch leaves the locus, its priced branch keeps it there, and the heat is the receipt.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

**F-Flow:** an unforced actual flow that breaks down in finite time, refuting the image of Section 8. **F-Energy:** an unforced actual flow whose energy rises, breaking the price of Section 6. Nothing is owed on the row's channel; the three-dimensional sentence over every admissible datum is the surplus over the actuated flows, priced at one premise (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`), and the forced cut is settled by its own witness (`one_witness_settles`, `value_union_iff`).

# Book V · The Hardware Closure Series

Sixteen papers read the same cut on the physical world. Each chapter carries the paper's body; the ledger rows, the carrier's derivation from the category of involutions and the methodology, common to all, are stated once in Book VI and Book VII.

## One Actuating Substrate: The Four Forces, Matter and the Dark Sector

*The Physical Witness of the Riemann Closure: The Critical Line as the Locus of Stationary Energy, the Particle Map as the Fold, and the Six Witnesses Read on One Hardware.* Edition 2.0.0.

**Abstract.** Six papers built physical witnesses for the closure template by which the Riemann Hypothesis was closed in the author's sealed volume, each on its own symmetry: the electron and the neutrino on charge conjugation, the magnet and the orbit on time reversal, the proton on the colour rotations, the fluid on the scaling of the viscous equation. This final paper builds the substrate they share. A particle carries odd readings, its charges, and one even reading, its energy; two involutions act on it and commute, the particle map that flips every charge and the time mirror that reverses a mode's rate. Each is carried equivariantly onto the reflection $s\mapsto 1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line: the particle map by the electron's charge carrier, the time mirror by the energy carrier $s=\tfrac12+iE$. Under the second the line is exactly the image of the real energies, so the line is the locus of stationary energy. We prove in core Lean 4, eighty laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the hypothesis on any set of zeros is equivalent to every zero being a stationary mode, which is least erasure and has the shape of gravity's fold; that conservation of the whole forces nothing, a mirrored pair conserving its total norm off the line; that the record decides nothing; that a magnetic term breaks time reversal and keeps every energy real; that one generation and every number of generations close, the hypercharges forced and the colour singlet locked on three distinct axes; and that stable neutral matter, the dark sector, lands on the line under both carriers while the neutrino's nature is one keyed bit. A Fortran twin runs thirty-four checks with zero failures, locating the first ten zeros to $3.3\times10^{-10}$. The physical side proves the value on every zero it actuates as a stationary mode, and on all zeros under one premise the kernel identifies with the value itself; the hypothesis is not derived in ZFC, and the one refuter is a computed zero off the line.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper closes the hardware series of the Riemann closure. Six constructed witnesses read the closure template on physical objects: The Electron Is the Seat, The Neutrino Is the Witness, The Arrow Has Two Branches, The Proton Is the Lock, The Magnet Is the Witness of the Pair Correlation and The Fluid Is the Witness. A seventh paper placed dark matter on the fixed set of charge conjugation. Each witness built its carrier on its own symmetry and its own object. This paper builds the one substrate on which all of them stand: its readings, its two involutions, its two carriers onto the seat, and the matter the carriers land.

It claims three things. First, a structure: a particle's charges are odd and its energy even under the particle map, the particle map and the time mirror of a mode's rate commute, and each is carried equivariantly onto the reflection that fixes the critical line; under the energy carrier the line is the locus of stationary energy. Second, a theorem about what the physical side proves: the value on any set of zeros is stationarity of every zero, which is least erasure, and conservation of the whole does not force it. Third, a placement: one generation of matter closes, every number of generations closes, the charges are forced, the colour singlet is locked, the dark sector sits on the line under both carriers, and the neutrino's nature is one bit.

A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for a derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from physics, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. There is no such derivation here and none was attempted. The unit evaluated is the template read on the substrate, and the question answered is how far the physical side reaches. It reaches every zero it actuates as a stationary mode, at theorem grade on the construction, and it reaches every zero on exactly one premise, which the kernel proves is the value itself and which conservation of the whole does not supply.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade. No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: a crossing holds on its channel's own class at its printed grade, and what a sentence over every admissible instance adds is exactly the unactuated (`surplus_is_the_unactuated`). No field theory and no unification of couplings is written or implied: no constant, mass, coupling or generation count is derived.

**Five terms.** A *reading* is any quantity an instrument returns from a state; it is *odd* under a map if the map flips its sign and *even* if the map keeps it. A *mode* $e^{-iEt}\psi$ is *stationary* when its norm is constant in time. The *fixed set* of a map is the set of points it leaves unchanged. A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property that no function of a given record decides, because two admissible worlds share the record and differ in the bit. *Premise grade* marks a claim held as a posit and never as a result.


### 3. One Substrate, Two Involutions, Four Readings

A particle in the broken phase carries four additive charges, electric charge in thirds, colour, baryon number in thirds and lepton number, and an energy. The map from a particle to its antiparticle flips every additive charge and keeps the energy, frequency and rate alike. In a local, Lorentz-invariant quantum field theory this is the combined symmetry of charge conjugation, parity and time reversal, exact by theorem (Lüders, 1954; Pauli, 1955), which keeps mass and lifetime; the proton and antiproton charge-to-mass ratios agree to sixteen parts per trillion (BASE, 2022). The particle map is an involution whose fixed set is exactly the neutral particles (`bar_involutive`, `bar_fixed_iff_neutral`). Its charge readings are odd and its energy reading even (`odd_charges_even_energy`): three plus one. The gauge interactions read charges; gravity reads the energy-momentum tensor and nothing else, so no gravitational reading tells a particle from its antiparticle (`gravity_reads_no_charge_bit`). Antihydrogen falls with $(0.75\pm0.13\pm0.16)\,g$ (ALPHA-g, 2023), within 1.21 standard deviations of ordinary matter (Appendix B).

The substrate carries a second involution, the time mirror of a mode: it keeps the frequency and reverses the rate, the complex conjugation $E\mapsto\bar E$ of the energy, and its fixed set is the real energies (`mirror_involutive`, `mirror_fixed_iff_real`). The two involutions commute (`involutions_commute`, checked on 5453 tuples in Appendix B), and a particle is fixed by both exactly when it is neutral and stable (`joint_fixed_iff`).

Each involution is carried onto the seat. The charge carrier is the electron witness's own: a particle of charge $q$ goes to the side $h=1+q$ of the line at the height of its frequency, so the particle map becomes the fold (`charge_carrier_equivariant`), neutral particles land on the line (`neutral_charge_on_line`), and the electron and the positron land on the two edges, real parts $0$ and $1$, mirror images (`pair_on_the_edges`). The energy carrier is the subject of Section 4. Under it the particle map is invisible: a particle and its antiparticle land on one point (`pair_lands_once`), because the energy carrier reads the even face. The two carriers are the two faces of three plus one: the charge carrier reads the odd side, where the pair stands apart, and the energy carrier reads the even side, where the pair is one.

The six witnesses stand on this substrate. The electron and the neutrino read the particle map, the first off its fixed set with an exact partner and the second on it. The magnet reads time reversal of the motion, the orbit reads time reversal of the path, the proton reads the colour rotations whose fixed points are the singlets, and the fluid reads the scaling of the viscous equation. Every one of these carriers enters the Riemann seat only through an equivariant map, and the composition of equivariant maps is equivariant (`equivariant_comp`), so every witness composes with the carriers of this section into one seat and not into several.


### 4. The Line Is Where Energy Stands Still

A mode with complex energy $E=a+\tfrac{i}{2}\beta$ evolves as $e^{-iEt}$, and its norm moves as
$$\bigl\|e^{-iEt}\psi\bigr\|^{2}=e^{\beta t}\,\|\psi\|^{2}.$$
The frequency $a$ is what a spectrometer reads; the doubled rate $\beta$ is how fast the mode gains or loses norm; the mode is stationary exactly when $\beta=0$ (`stationary_iff_real`). The energy carrier is
$$s(E)=\tfrac12+iE,\qquad \sigma(s(E))=1-\overline{\tfrac12+iE}=\tfrac12+i\bar E=s(\bar E),$$
so the time mirror of a rate is carried exactly onto the fold. In the chart, $h=2\operatorname{Re}s$ and $t=\operatorname{Im}s$, it reads $\phi(a,\beta)=(1-\beta,a)$, an equivariant bijection with inverse $\psi(h,t)=(t,1-h)$ (`phi_equivariant`, `psi_phi`, `phi_psi`, `phi_injective`). A point lies on the line exactly when the energy it carries is stationary (`line_is_stationary`): the critical line is the locus of stationary energy.

A registration of a closed system returns a real number, the spectrum of a self-adjoint generator being real and its evolution unitary (von Neumann, 1932; Stone, 1932), so every measured energy lands on the line and none lands off it (`measured_on_line`, `no_measurement_off_line`). The twin executes the carrier on the 3721 integer energies with $|a|,|\beta|\le 30$ and the complex identity on 20000 energies, the largest deviation $1.1\times10^{-16}$; a real energy keeps its norm to $2.2\times10^{-16}$ over ten time steps, and a rate of $2\times10^{-3}$ moves it by $2.0\times10^{-3}$ in one (Appendix B).

The magnet witness found that the zeros' spacings follow the unitary class, the statistics of systems whose motion breaks time reversal (Montgomery, 1973; Odlyzko, 1987; Berry and Keating, 1999). That reading and this one are about two different conjugations and do not collide. Time reversal of the motion conjugates the generator's matrix elements; the time mirror of a rate conjugates an eigenvalue. A magnetic term breaks the first and keeps the second: a Hermitian block $\begin{pmatrix}a & c+id\\ c-id & b\end{pmatrix}$ has discriminant $(a-b)^2+4(c^2+d^2)\ge0$ whether or not $d$ vanishes, so its energies are real, and time reversal of the block, $d\mapsto -d$, leaves the discriminant unchanged (`stationarity_survives_the_magnet`, `block_T_fixed_iff`); all 100000 time-broken blocks of the twin have real spectra. The arrow is in the zeros' statistics; the line is in their stationarity.


### 5. The Pair Beside the Line

Off the line the carrier reads a pair. A mode off the line and its time mirror are two distinct modes, both off the line, with one record; their rates cancel, and exactly one of them decays (`off_line_pair`). For particles: a decaying particle and its time mirror land on two points off the line with one record (`tmirror_equivariant`, `resonance_pair`). Unstable particles are the physical instances. The free neutron's width is $7.5\times10^{-19}$ eV, the muon's $3.0\times10^{-10}$ eV, and the $Z$ boson's $2.5$ GeV, $2.7\times10^{-2}$ of its mass (Particle Data Group, 2024). Each is an open mode of a larger closed system: its decay exports norm into its products, its complex energy is the mode's record inside that system, and its time mirror is the capturing state of scattering theory (Gamow, 1928; Breit and Wigner, 1936). Off-line modes are records of open parts, and they come mirrored, exactly as the template's residue comes in pairs.

The stable particles stand on the line. The electron's partial width for $e\to\gamma\nu$ is below $3.2\times10^{-52}$ eV, $6.2\times10^{-58}$ of its rest energy (Borexino, 2015); the proton's partial width for $p\to e^+\pi^0$ is below $8.7\times10^{-58}$ eV, $9.3\times10^{-67}$ of its rest energy (Super-Kamiokande, 2020); both land on the line to those fractions (`stable_lands`), at corroboration grade. The electron therefore stands on the line under the energy carrier and on an edge under the charge carrier: stable, and charged.


### 6. Conservation of the Whole Forces Nothing

The tempting physical argument runs: the universe is a closed system, a closed system conserves its norm, so every energy it carries is real and every zero read as such an energy lies on the line. The argument fails at its second step by theorem. There is a world, closed under the fold, whose members are a decaying mode and its growing mirror; its total rate is zero, so the whole conserves its norm, and the value fails on it (`whole_conservation_forces_nothing`). Every fold-closed world with a member off the line contains a decaying member and a growing one (`fold_closed_off_line_decays_and_grows`). The zero set of $\xi$ is closed under the fold, since $\xi(s)=\xi(1-s)$ and $\xi(\bar s)=\overline{\xi(s)}$ (Riemann, 1859), so an off-line zero would bring its mirror, and the pair's rates would cancel. What the value requires is stationarity of each mode; conservation of their sum does not supply it.

The balanced gain-loss dimer exhibits the theorem on a physical system:
$$H=\begin{pmatrix}\omega+i\gamma & \kappa\\ \kappa & \omega-i\gamma\end{pmatrix},\qquad \lambda_\pm=\omega\pm\sqrt{\kappa^{2}-\gamma^{2}}.$$
Its trace is $2\omega$ at every gain, so its total rate is zero. Below the exceptional point both modes are real and stand on the line; above it they form one mirrored pair of equal frequency and opposite rates. Sweeping $\gamma$ from $0$ to $1$ at $\kappa=\tfrac14$, the twin finds 50 real pairs, 150 mirrored pairs and a balanced whole at all 200 gains; the transition was observed in coupled optical waveguides (Bender and Boettcher, 1998; Rüter and collaborators, 2010).

The companion witnesses meet here. The arrow paper's caution is this theorem's physical face: reversibility alone does not keep a branch on its locus, and what keeps gravity there is one sign. The fluid paper's reversible branch leaves its locus and its priced branch brings it back. And the record decides nothing: the world holding the line point $(1,5)$ and the world holding the mirrored pair $(0,5),(2,5)$ leave one record, any reading of the record gives them one answer, and the value holds on one and fails on the other (`worlds_share_the_record`, `record_decides_nothing`). A spectrometer sees one line at height 5 in both.


### 7. The Posit Has Gravity's Shape

The value on any set of zeros is stationarity of every member (`value_iff_stationary`); over the complex numbers, every zero $\rho$ has $\operatorname{Re}\rho=\tfrac12$ exactly when every $E(\rho)=-i(\rho-\tfrac12)$ is real. The closure's posit, that registration erases nothing, is the same statement (`least_erasure_iff_value`, `least_erasure_is_stationarity`), and it holds exactly when the fold fixes every member, no member standing off the line and no side left over (`posit_is_the_fold`). The arrow paper found the posit's shape in gravity's fold: a registration that erases nothing because nothing stands off its locus, one sign and no remanent bit. This paper finds the posit's content on the substrate: every zero a stationary mode. The two readings are one theorem: the zeros register as gravity does exactly when every zero stands still.

The premise that every zero is a stationary mode is equivalent to the value, neither weaker nor stronger (`act_is_the_value`). That is why the physical side cannot do more than it does. A physical principle that would place every zero on the line must supply stationarity of each zero; the most general principle available, conservation of the whole, supplies stationarity of a sum and forces nothing (Section 6); and no reading of the record supplies it (Section 6). The premise is the act that closed the Riemann row, read physically, at premise grade.


### 8. The Hardware Closes

One generation of left-handed fields, with hypercharges times six, $Q(6\times1)$, $u^c(3\times-4)$, $d^c(3\times2)$, $L(2\times-3)$, $e^c(1\times6)$, satisfies every anomaly condition,
$$\sum Y=0,\quad \sum Y^{3}=0,\quad \sum_{\text{colour}} Y=0,\quad \sum_{\text{isospin}} Y=0,\quad \#\text{doublets}=4,\quad \text{colour cube}=0$$
(`generation_closes`; Bouchiat, Iliopoulos and Meyer, 1972; Gross and Jackiw, 1972; Witten, 1982). $n$ generations satisfy the same conditions with $4n$ doublets for every $n$ (`gens_sum`, `every_generation_count_closes`): the conditions close each generation and do not fix their number, which is measured, $N_\nu=2.9840\pm0.0082$ from the $Z$ width (ALEPH, DELPHI, L3, OPAL and SLD, 2006). The three Yukawa couplings, the isospin anomaly and the mixed gravitational anomaly force every hypercharge as a multiple of the quark doublet's,
$$y_u=-4y_Q,\quad y_d=2y_Q,\quad y_L=-3y_Q,\quad y_e=6y_Q,\quad y_H=3y_Q$$
(`hypercharge_forced`; Geng and Marshak, 1989; Minahan, Ramond and Warner, 1990); the twin searches all $25^5$ integer vectors in $[-12,12]^5$ at $y_Q=1$ and finds one. The fourth reading closes the three: the vanishing trace of hypercharge is where gravity meets the gauge charges. With $Q=T_3+Y$ the up quark carries $\tfrac23$, the down $-\tfrac13$, the electron $-1$; the proton one unit, the neutron none, hydrogen none, and the vacuum's direction of the Higgs doublet none (`charges`, `matter_neutral`, `vacuum_neutral`). The vacuum sits on the fixed set of the particle map.

The colour lock is the proton witness's, proved here on the substrate's labels: three colour labels carry a nonzero alternating product exactly when they are distinct (`lock_iff_distinct`), and the product vanishes whenever two coincide (`lock_fails_when_colours_coincide`); of the 27 colour triples exactly six are locked. A baryon is a colour singlet only on three distinct axes, the three-axis lock of the program read in the strong reading, and its stability is a certified region with an owed tail, the proton standing on the line to $9.3\times10^{-67}$ of its rest energy in the channel $p\to e^+\pi^0$ (Section 5).


### 9. The Fixed Set Inhabited: The Dark Sector and the Neutrino

A stable particle fixed by the particle map lands on the line under the energy carrier and under the charge carrier, with every charge reading zero (`dark_on_the_line`). No gauge reading sees it and the energy reading does: the observational profile of dark matter, seen through gravity alone in rotation curves, lensing, the acoustic peaks and cluster collisions (Planck Collaboration, 2020), placed on the fixed set by the dark-sector paper and here on the line under both carriers. The placement is structural; the particle's identity is owed to a detection.

The neutrino is the fixed set's keyed resident. In one world lepton number is a conserved charge and the neutrino is a Dirac particle; in the other it is not and the neutrino is its own antiparticle. The two worlds share the gauge record, the energy seat and the charge seat, and the particle map fixes the Majorana neutrino and not the Dirac one (`majorana_is_one_keyed_bit`). The gauge interactions cannot decide the question; one deed reads it, neutrinoless double beta decay, and the half-life of $^{136}$Xe is bounded above $2.3\times10^{26}$ years (KamLAND-Zen, 2023). The neutrino's floor is supplied by deed: oscillation returns positive mass-squared differences, and a positive difference of squared masses forbids a vanishing mass (`floor_by_deed`); with $\Delta m^2_{21}=7.53\times10^{-5}$ eV² and $\Delta m^2_{32}=2.455\times10^{-3}$ eV² (Particle Data Group, 2024), at least two masses are nonzero, the heaviest at least $0.050$ eV (Appendix B).

Least erasure is reversibility on the substrate. A step that sends two distinct states to one merges them, a merge has no inverse (`merging_is_irreversible`), a reversible step merges nothing (`reversible_merges_nothing`), and the time mirror is reversible (`mirror_is_reversible`). Registration merges exactly the off-line pair, erasing the side (`registration_erases_off_line`), and one irreversibly committed bit costs at least $k_BT\ln 2$, $2.870978885\times10^{-21}$ J at 300 K (Landauer, 1961; Bérut and collaborators, 2012). The closure's posit is the statement that nothing is merged at the seat: every zero stationary, no side forgotten because no point has two sides.


### 10. The Actuated Zeros, the Crossing, and What the Substrate Carries

The twin evaluates $\zeta(\tfrac12+it)$ by Euler–Maclaurin summation, sixty terms and eight Bernoulli corrections, and Hardy's function $Z(t)=e^{i\theta(t)}\zeta(\tfrac12+it)$, real on the line to $4.6\times10^{-11}$ over the scan (Titchmarsh, 1986; Edwards, 1974). On $(10,100]$ it finds 29 sign changes, each a zero on the line, matching the rounded smooth count $\theta(100)/\pi+1=29.0024$; the first ten located heights agree with the tabulated zeros to $3.3\times10^{-10}$, the first at $t=14.134725142$. These are actuated instances: computed, located on the line, read as stationary energies. The certification that no zero below $3\times10^{12}$ stands off the line is Platt and Trudgian's (2021); the twin corroborates its first rung.

The crossing is exact both ways: the value holds on a set of zeros exactly when every member is the image of a real energy (`value_iff_actuated`), and a carrier with its coverage gives the value and uses nothing else (`kinetic_crossing`). The address is the one line all carriers land on: the charge carrier, the energy carrier and, through them, every witness's carrier compose into one seat (Section 3), and every reading of the value returns the same proposition. Stated whole: the physical side proves the value on every zero it actuates as a stationary mode, at theorem grade on the construction and measured grade on the data; the step to every zero is one premise, stationarity of each zero, which the kernel proves is the value itself; conservation of the whole does not supply it and no reading of the record does; the hypothesis is not derived in ZFC. The whole stands in three judged capstones (`substrate_carries_the_seat`, `physical_witness`, `hardware_closes`).

**What the substrate carries, and what it does not.** It carries the seat in two faces, the odd face on charge and the even face on energy; the registration and its residue, the pair beside the line; the closure's posit, as stationarity with gravity's shape; the matter of one generation and of every number of generations, with forced charges and a locked colour singlet; and the fixed set inhabited, stable neutral matter on the line and one keyed bit for the neutrino. It does not carry a derivation of any constant, the count of generations, the identity of the dark matter, or a field theory. It does not carry the value on the unactuated zeros; that is the act, and the substrate proves it is exactly that.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Two falsifiers, each forced by the construction. **F-Computed:** a zero of $\zeta$ located by a verified computation with rigorous error bounds at a point with $\operatorname{Re}\rho\neq\tfrac12$, bringing its mirror by the functional equation; the instrument is verified evaluation with interval arithmetic and Turing's method, extended by independent groups on independent hardware; it refutes the premise of Section 7, stationarity of every zero, and leaves every equivalence of the kernel standing. **F-Measure:** an isolated system, every channel into which it could export norm closed, registering a norm-changing eigenmode; the instruments are underground searches for the spontaneous radiation that non-unitary collapse would produce (Donadi and collaborators, 2021) and precision spectroscopy of isolated trapped ions testing the linearity of quantum evolution (Bollinger and collaborators, 1989); it refutes the physical reading of `measured_on_line`, and with it the class of systems the energy carrier witnesses.

**The cross-paper falsification matrix.** The series' falsifiers, each a forbidden observation, with the theorem it would break.

## The Geometric Nature of Light

*Light as the Line: The Photon Fixed by Both Involutions of the Substrate, Frequency as the Height, Helicity as the One Bit, and Detection as the Registration That Keeps the Energy and Forgets the Path.* Edition 2.0.0.

**Abstract.** Twenty-five centuries of optics asked what travels when something is seen and through what it travels. This paper answers the geometric half of that question at the seat on which the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis stands. The actuating substrate of the hardware paper carries two involutions, the particle map that flips every additive charge and the time mirror that reverses a mode's rate, and two carriers onto the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. The photon is the one excitation fixed by both at every frequency: it carries no charge of any kind, its energy is real, and it lands on the line under both carriers. We prove in core Lean 4, sixty-three laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that frequency is the seat's height and never its side, so the whole spectrum from radio to gamma stands on one line; that a massive mode runs slower than light while every massless mode runs at one speed; that helicity is the one bit no energy reading returns; that the charge-conjugation parity of $n$ photons is $(-1)^n$, selecting two photons for parapositronium and three for orthopositronium; that detection is the registration of the template, a fringe existing exactly when both paths are open and the record forgets the side; and that the quantum belongs to the field, since every classical intensity bunches while one photon never coincides with itself. A Fortran twin runs sixteen checks with zero failures, among them Englert's duality to $4.4\times10^{-16}$ and the single-photon anticorrelation 13.7 standard deviations below every classical field. Light witnesses the occupancy of the line; it does not decide where the zeros of $\zeta$ lie, which stays the act at premise grade.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The hardware paper, One Actuating Substrate, built the substrate on which the six witnesses of the Riemann closure stand: a particle's charges are odd and its energy even under the particle map, the particle map and the time mirror of a mode's rate commute, and each is carried equivariantly onto the reflection that fixes the critical line. This paper reads that substrate on light. It claims that the photon is the substrate's one excitation fixed by both involutions at every frequency, and that each old question of optics, what travels, at what speed, carrying what, and registered how, has a precise answer on the seat.

It refuses one thing. It does not derive the Riemann Hypothesis from optics. Light occupies the line; it does not decide where the zeros of $\zeta$ lie. A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for a derivation of the hypothesis from the properties of the photon, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. None was attempted. What the photon witnesses is the occupant of the seat, the one excitation that stands on the line under every carrier the substrate has.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade. No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated. No field theory is written and no constant is derived: the speed of light, the fine-structure constant and the photon's couplings enter as measured.

**Five terms.** A *reading* is any quantity an instrument returns from a state, *odd* under a map that flips its sign and *even* under one that keeps it. A *mode* $e^{-iEt}\psi$ is *stationary* when its norm is constant in time. The *fixed set* of a map is the set of points it leaves unchanged. A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides. The *side* of a two-path experiment is which path a quantum took; the *record* is what the screen keeps.


### 3. Light Is the Line

The photon carries no electric charge, no colour, no baryon number and no lepton number, and its energy is real: it is its own antiparticle and it does not decay (`photon_is_its_own_antiparticle`, `photon_is_stationary`). It is therefore fixed by both involutions of the substrate, the particle map and the time mirror, and by the functor of the seat it lands on the line under both carriers (`photon_on_both_lines`). No other excitation of the Standard Model is fixed by both at every frequency: the $Z$ boson is neutral and decays, the neutrino's neutrality under the particle map is one keyed bit, and every charged particle stands on an edge under the charge carrier. Light is the seat's occupant.

The occupancy is measured. The photon's mass is bounded below $10^{-18}$ eV (Particle Data Group, 2024), and at that bound a radio photon of one microelectronvolt would be slowed by $5\times10^{-25}$ of its speed (Appendix B): light stands on the line to that depth. The electron paper read the neutral sector as the fixed set of charge conjugation, with the photon and the neutral pion sitting on it as particles; this paper reads the photon as the one resident of that set whose energy is real at every frequency, and so the one resident of the line under the energy carrier too.


### 4. Frequency Is the Height

Under the energy carrier a photon of frequency $\omega$ lands at $(1,\omega)$: its frequency is the seat's height and its side is the line (`frequency_is_the_height`). Every frequency stands on one side (`every_frequency_one_side`), and registration keeps a photon exactly, since nothing of it stands off the line to be forgotten (`registration_keeps_light`). The spectrum from radio at $10^{-9}$ eV to gamma rays at $10^{11}$ eV, twenty decades, is the line's heights (Appendix B).

The old question of the speed hierarchy has its answer on the same two coordinates. A mode with $\omega^2=k^2+m^2$ and $m\neq0$ has $k^2<\omega^2$ and runs slower than light (`massive_slower`); a massless mode has $\omega^2=k^2$ at every frequency (`massless_on_the_cone`). Frequency is the height and rest mass is the depth: two coordinates, never one. The twin finds the massless group velocity equal to $c$ to $1.9\times10^{-10}$ across 2000 frequencies and every massive mode below it. Measured: gamma rays from GRB 090510 bound any vacuum dispersion above the Planck energy (Fermi LAT, 2009), and the gamma-ray burst of GW170817 arrived 1.74 seconds after the gravitational waves emitted 130 million years earlier, bounding the difference of the two speeds below $4.2\times10^{-16}$ (LIGO, Virgo, Fermi GBM and INTEGRAL, 2017).


### 5. Helicity Is the One Bit

A massless particle of spin one has two helicity states, not three (Wigner, 1939). Parity reverses the helicity and keeps the frequency (`parity_involutive`). So the two helicities of one frequency are two states with one energy record, and no reading of the energy returns which one is present (`helicity_is_one_bit`). This is the template's residue carried by light: one bit, a two-point set over one record, unread by the even reading. Circular polarization is that bit made visible, and it is the bit a polarizer, not a calorimeter, registers.


### 6. Detection Is Registration

In a two-path experiment the side is which path the quantum took and the record is where it lands on the screen. Write the intensity at a point as $I=a^2+b^2+2abc\,s$, with $a$ and $b$ the two amplitudes, $c$ the overlap of whatever marks the path (zero when the side is registered, nonzero when it is forgotten), and $s=\pm1$ the fringe sign. A fringe exists, the intensity differing between $s=+1$ and $s=-1$, exactly when both paths are open and the side is forgotten (`fringe_iff_side_forgotten`); registering the side leaves $a^2+b^2$ and no fringe at all (`side_registered_no_fringe`). This is the template's registration with both clauses: a record that forgets the side keeps the interference, and one that keeps the side erases it.

The trade is exact. The which-path distinguishability $D$ and the fringe visibility $V$ satisfy $D^2+V^2\le1$, with equality on every pure state (Englert, 1996; Wootters and Zurek, 1979); the twin reaches equality to $4.4\times10^{-16}$ on 20000 pure states. The which-way experiment in an atom interferometer (Dürr, Nonn and Rempe, 1998) observed the loss of the fringe with no momentum kick to explain it: the record of the side, and nothing else, erased it.


### 7. The Quantum Is the Field's

The discreteness of light is not the detector's alone. For any classical field detected by quantized matter, the coincidence rate of two detectors is at least the square of the single rate, $g^{(2)}\ge1$ (Glauber, 1963), which the engine proves on the two-point split, $(x+y)^2\le2(x^2+y^2)$ (`classical_light_bunches`), and the twin checks on 10000 classical intensity distributions. A single photon never coincides with itself, $n(n-1)=0$ at $n=1$ (`single_photon_no_coincidence`, `quantum_is_the_fields`). Coherent light has $g^{(2)}=1$ and thermal light $2$ (Appendix B). Antibunching was observed in resonance fluorescence (Kimble, Dagenais and Mandel, 1977), and a single-photon source behind a beam splitter gave an anticorrelation parameter of $0.18\pm0.06$ (Grangier, Roger and Aspect, 1986), 13.7 standard deviations below every classical field. Two photons meeting at a beam splitter leave together (Hong, Ou and Mandel, 1987). The photoelectric effect alone admits a semiclassical account (Lamb and Scully, 1969); light as a whole does not. The field carries its own quantum.


### 8. Light Under the Particle Map: Furry and Positronium

The photon sits on the fixed set of the particle map as a particle, and it carries a sign there: its charge-conjugation parity is $-1$, so $n$ photons carry $(-1)^n$ (`cparity_val`). Electromagnetism conserves this sign, so a state even under charge conjugation decays only into an even number of photons (`c_even_needs_even_photons`), and closed loops of charge emit no odd number of photons (Furry, 1937). Parapositronium, even, decays into two photons and orthopositronium, odd, into three (`positronium_counts`); their lifetimes, 125 ps and 142 ns, are the electron paper's measured readings of this bit.


### 9. The Even Reading Travels at One Speed

Gravity reads the energy-momentum tensor and nothing else, so it reads light's energy and never its helicity or its C-parity. Light bends in a gravitational field by exactly twice the Newtonian angle, and gravitational waves and light, the two massless even-reading carriers, travel at one speed (Section 4). The arrow witness typed gravity as the reversible branch of registration, one sign and no bit; light is the one excitation that stands on that branch's locus with every odd reading zero.


### 10. What Light Carries, and What It Does Not

Light carries the seat's occupant: the one excitation fixed by both involutions at every frequency, on the line under both carriers, with the frequency as the height, the helicity as the one bit and the C-parity as the sign. It carries the registration: detection as the record that keeps the energy and forgets the path, exact to Englert's equality. It carries its own quantum.

It does not carry the zeros of $\zeta$. The engine's crossing is on its own image, the photons it lands (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves (`act_is_the_value` there). It does not derive the speed of light, the fine-structure constant, or any other magnitude. It does not identify the substrate with the electromagnetic field: the field is the substrate's odd reading, and light is the configuration of that reading which stands on the line.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break. **F-Dispersion:** a vacuum speed of light that depends on frequency, at any precision, breaking `every_frequency_one_side` read physically and `massless_on_the_cone`. **F-Fringe:** a fringe of full visibility from a two-path state whose side is registered with certainty, breaking `fringe_iff_side_forgotten` and Englert's duality. **F-Helicity:** a third, longitudinal helicity state of the free photon, breaking `helicity_is_one_bit`. Nothing is owed on the row's channel; two measurements would sharpen readings without moving the paper's standing: the photon mass bound, and the vacuum-dispersion bound at still higher photon energies.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains its occupant: WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image for the seat's occupant in light and for detection as registration, at the root's grade for the crossing; the barrier gains its optical witnesses, the helicity pair and the two paths of one fringe; nothing is owed on the row's channel.
