## Electromagnetism on the Riemann Locus

*Charge as the Side, the Electric Field as the Line, the Magnetic Sign as the One Bit, and Duality as the Quarter Turn Whose Square Is Charge Conjugation.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** Electromagnetism is the best-measured interaction in physics and the one whose discrete symmetries are cleanest: charge conjugation reverses every field, time reversal reverses the magnetic field and keeps the electric one, and a duality rotation exchanges them in the vacuum. This paper reads the field on the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. Two carriers land electromagnetism on the seat. The charge carrier sends a particle of charge $q$ to the side of the line and carries charge conjugation onto the reflection, so the neutral states are the line. The field carrier $s=\tfrac12+iF$ on the Riemann–Silberstein amplitude $F=E+iB$ carries time reversal, the complex conjugation of $F$, onto the reflection, so the purely electric fields are the line and the magnetic sign is the one bit a field and its time reverse do not share. We prove in core Lean 4, seventy-one laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that charge conjugation, parity and time reversal are involutions with $CT=P$ and $CPT$ the identity on the field; that duality is a quarter turn whose square is charge conjugation and whose fourth power is the identity, anticommuting with time reversal as $-i$ does with complex conjugation; that the energy density is invariant under all of them while both Lorentz invariants reverse under duality; that light is null and stays null under duality; that a magnetic term breaks time reversal and keeps every energy real; that charge is conserved by pair creation and forced in thirds; and that the Aharonov–Bohm record reads flux modulo one quantum. A Fortran twin runs seventeen checks with zero failures, the quantum Hall and Josephson constants exact in the SI. The paper joins the gravity–magnetism branches of the arrow witness: gravity the even, one-signed reading, magnetism the odd reading that keeps a priced bit. It decides nothing about the zeros of $\zeta$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series, dedicated to one interaction. The hardware paper, One Actuating Substrate, put the four readings of the substrate on one seat: three gauge readings odd under the particle map and one, gravity's, even. The arrow witness split registration into two branches, gravity the reversible branch that keeps no bit and magnetism the priced branch that keeps one. This paper reads the electromagnetic interaction itself on the seat, in its charges, its fields, its discrete symmetries, its duality, and its quantum registrations, and joins it to those two branches.

It claims that electromagnetism carries the seat twice. Through the charge carrier, charge conjugation is the reflection and the neutral states are the line. Through the field carrier, time reversal is the reflection, the purely electric fields are the line, and the sign of the magnetic field is the one bit a field and its time reverse do not share. Duality, the rotation that exchanges electric and magnetic, is the quarter turn of the seat's Return: its square is charge conjugation.

A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for a derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from Maxwell's equations, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. None was attempted. The field witnesses the seat's shape in the most precisely measured interaction there is; where the zeros lie stays the act, at premise grade.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade. No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated. No field theory beyond Maxwell's is written and no constant is derived: the fine-structure constant, the charges' magnitudes and the speed of light enter as measured or as defined by the SI.

**Five terms.** A *reading* is any quantity an instrument returns from a state, *odd* under a map that flips its sign and *even* under one that keeps it. The *Riemann–Silberstein amplitude* is $F=E+iB$ in units with $c=1$. *Duality* is the rotation $E\mapsto B$, $B\mapsto -E$, which is $F\mapsto -iF$. The *fixed set* of a map is the set of points it leaves unchanged. A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. Charge Is the Side

A particle of charge $q$, in units of the charge quantum, lands at $h=1+q$ at the height of its frequency, so charge conjugation, $q\mapsto-q$, becomes the reflection $h\mapsto2-h$ (`charge_carrier_equivariant`). The neutral particles are the line (`neutral_charge_on_line`), the electron and the positron land on the two edges, real parts $0$ and $1$, mirror images (`pair_on_the_edges`), and the particle map is an involution whose fixed set is exactly the neutral states (`bar_fixed_iff_neutral`). The electron witness built this carrier on the leptons; here it is the carrier of the whole interaction.

Charge is conserved at the seat. Pair creation makes a particle and its antiparticle, whose charges cancel (`pair_creation_conserves_charge`), and the electron, the lightest charged particle, does not decay: its lifetime in the channel $e\to\gamma\nu$ exceeds $6.6\times10^{28}$ years (Borexino, 2015). Charge is forced in thirds: the Yukawa couplings and two anomaly conditions fix every hypercharge as a multiple of the quark doublet's (`hypercharge_forced`), and with $Q=T_3+Y$ the up quark carries $\tfrac23$, the down $-\tfrac13$, the electron $-1$, hydrogen and the vacuum none (`charges_in_thirds`). The electron and the positron carry charges equal and opposite to four parts in a hundred million (Particle Data Group, 2024).


### 4. The Electric Field Is the Line

On one axis pair the field is the Riemann–Silberstein amplitude $F=E+iB$ (Silberstein, 1907; Bialynicki-Birula, 1996). Time reversal keeps the electric field and reverses the magnetic one, so it is the complex conjugation $F\mapsto\bar F$. The field carrier
$$s(F)=\tfrac12+iF,\qquad \sigma(s(F))=1-\overline{\tfrac12+iF}=\tfrac12+i\bar F=s(\bar F),$$
carries time reversal exactly onto the fold; in the chart it reads $(E,B)\mapsto(1-B,E)$ (`field_carrier_equivariant`). A field is fixed by time reversal exactly when $B=0$ (`T_fixed_iff_electric`), and it lands on the line exactly then (`electric_on_the_line`): the line is the purely electric field, the field of charges at rest. The twin executes the carrier on 3721 integer fields.


### 5. The Magnetic Sign Is the One Bit

A field with $B\neq0$ and its time reverse are two fields, both off the line, with one electric component, one energy density and one record (`magnetic_sign_is_the_bit`). The energy density $E^2+B^2$ is even under time reversal, charge conjugation and parity alike (`energy_even`), so no reading of the energy returns the sign of the magnetic field. That sign is the one bit: a moving charge's field and the field of the same charge moving backward share every even reading and differ in it. The arrow witness found it in iron, remanence $\pm0.996$ at zero applied field, two worlds with one record, the bit kept at a price above Landauer's floor; the product $E\cdot B$ is odd under time reversal and under parity and even under charge conjugation (`EB_odd_under_T_and_P`).


### 6. Charge Conjugation, Parity and Time Reversal on the Field

On the field, charge conjugation reverses both components, $F\mapsto-F$; parity reverses the polar electric field and keeps the axial magnetic field; time reversal keeps the first and reverses the second. Each is an involution (`C_involutive`, `P_involutive`, `T_involutive`), charge conjugation after time reversal is parity (`CT_is_P`), and the three together act as the identity on the field (`CPT_trivial`): each of $E$ and $B$ is odd under exactly two of the three. This is the field's form of the CPT theorem (Lüders, 1954; Pauli, 1955), and it is why the substrate's particle map, carried by CPT, keeps every field reading that gravity sees. The twin executes the table on 3721 fields.


### 7. Duality: The Quarter Turn Whose Square Is Charge Conjugation

In the vacuum Maxwell's equations are invariant under the rotation $E\mapsto B$, $B\mapsto -E$ (Heaviside, 1893; Larmor, 1897), which is multiplication of $F$ by $-i$. Its square is $F\mapsto-F$, charge conjugation (`duality_squared_is_C`), and its fourth power is the identity (`duality_fourth_is_identity`): duality is a quarter turn, and its square lands on minus one exactly as the spinor's full turn and the quaternion units' product $ijk=-1$ do in the electron witness. It anticommutes with time reversal, $TD=C\,DT$, as $-i$ does with complex conjugation (`duality_anticommutes_with_T`). The energy density is invariant under it (`energy_duality_invariant`), and both Lorentz invariants, $E^2-B^2$ and $E\cdot B$, reverse under it (`invariants_flip_under_duality`).

Sources break the duality. Electric charges exist; magnetic charges have not been found (Particle Data Group, 2024). The question whether they do is the duality's own bit: a world in which every particle carries the same ratio of magnetic to electric charge is carried by one global duality rotation to a world with no magnetic charge at all, so a monopole is physically distinct only if some particle's ratio differs from another's. Were one found, Dirac's condition would force every electric charge to be a multiple of a quantum fixed by the monopole's charge (Dirac, 1931). This paper types the question and does not answer it; the arrow witness's F-Monopole names the observation that would.


### 8. Light Is Null

A plane wave carries an electric field perpendicular to its magnetic field and equal to it in magnitude: both Lorentz invariants vanish (`plane_wave_null`), and duality, which reverses both invariants, keeps a null field null (`null_preserved_by_duality`). Light is therefore the duality-neutral field: the configuration of the electromagnetic reading on which the rotation that exchanges electric and magnetic acts without changing any invariant. The twin builds 5000 random plane waves and finds both invariants zero to $10^{-15}$, and the dual of each is again a plane wave along the same direction. The companion paper The Geometric Nature of Light reads the photon on the seat as the one excitation fixed by both involutions of the substrate at every frequency.


### 9. The Magnet Keeps Every Energy Real, and Registration Lands on Integers

A magnetic field breaks time reversal of the motion of a charge and keeps every energy real. A Hermitian block with a magnetic phase has a nonnegative discriminant whether or not the phase vanishes (`stationarity_survives_the_magnet`, `magnetism_odd_branch`), so its energies are real; the twin checks 100000 such blocks. An electron in a uniform field of one tesla has Landau levels $(n+\tfrac12)\hbar\omega_c$ with $\hbar\omega_c=1.1577\times10^{-4}$ eV, each real, each degenerate $2.418\times10^{14}$ times per square metre (Landau, 1930; Appendix B). The magnet witness read the zeros' statistics as those of time-broken motion; the Landau problem is the cleanest such motion, and a charged particle in a magnetic field has been proposed as a realization of the Berry–Keating Hamiltonian, its spectrum following the smooth count of the zeros (Sierra and Townsend, 2008). The magnet breaks time reversal of the motion and keeps every energy stationary: the arrow is in the statistics and the line in the stationarity.

Electromagnetic registration lands on integers. The Hall resistance of a two-dimensional electron gas in a strong field is $h/(\nu e^2)$ with $\nu$ an integer (von Klitzing, Dorda and Pepper, 1980), the integer a topological invariant of the filled bands (Thouless, Kohmoto, Nightingale and den Nijs, 1982), reproducible across materials to parts in $10^{10}$ (Jeckelmann and Jeanneret, 2001); the flux through a superconducting ring comes in quanta $h/2e$; and a Josephson junction under microwaves steps at voltages $n\,hf/2e$ (Josephson, 1962). With $e$ and $h$ exact in the SI of 2019 the twin computes $h/e^2=25812.80745930\ \Omega$, $h/2e=2.0678338485\times10^{-15}$ Wb and $2e/h=4.8359784842\times10^{14}$ Hz/V. Each is a registration that lands the field on a locus of integers and keeps nothing off it. The Aharonov–Bohm phase reads the flux enclosed by a path only modulo $h/e$ (Aharonov and Bohm, 1959): two fluxes one quantum apart are two worlds with one record (`aharonov_bohm_two_worlds`), the twin finding the phase equal to $7.3\times10^{-15}$ over 2000 fluxes.


### 10. Gravity and Magnetism: One Registration, Two Branches

The arrow witness split registration into two branches. Gravity is the reversible branch: its sources have one sign, it reads energy and nothing else, and no gravitational reading tells a charge from its antiparticle (`gravity_even_branch`); antihydrogen falls as hydrogen does (ALPHA-g, 2023). Magnetism is the priced branch: time reversal reverses the magnetic field (`magnetism_odd_branch`), so the magnetic sign is a bit, and a stable magnetic bit at $5.0\times10^{-19}$ J per switch costs 174 Landauer floors at 300 K (Appendix B). Electromagnetism carries both faces of the substrate's three plus one on one field: its charges and its magnetic sign are odd, its energy density even, and the even part is what gravity reads. The two readings differ in strength by the ratio of the Coulomb to the gravitational attraction in hydrogen, $2.27\times10^{39}$, with $\alpha=7.2973525737\times10^{-3}$ computed from the constants (Appendix B). This paper derives neither number; it states which reading each belongs to.

What the field does not carry is the zeros of $\zeta$. The engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), the fields and charges it lands, and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break. **F-Charge:** a process that creates or destroys net electric charge, the decay of the electron among them, breaking `pair_creation_conserves_charge` and the seat reading of Section 3. **F-Thirds:** a free particle whose charge is not a multiple of one third of the electron's, breaking `hypercharge_forced` and `charges_in_thirds` read physically. **F-Reality:** a magnetic field that drives a bound charge's energy off the real axis in a closed system, breaking `stationarity_survives_the_magnet` read physically. Nothing is owed on the row's channel. Two measurements would sharpen readings without moving the paper's standing: a magnetic monopole, which would read the duality's bit, and the resolution of the disputed digits of the fine-structure constant.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains its electromagnetic readings: WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image for the seat on both carriers of the field and for the Return in duality, at the root's grade for the crossing; the barrier gains the magnetic sign and the Aharonov–Bohm record; the pair correlation gains the Landau levels; nothing is owed on the row's channel.

## Immanent Gravity

*Gravity as the Substrate Reading Its Own Record: One Sign and No Screening, Blind to Composition, Reversible with No Bit, Every Clock Moved Together, and the Deficit of Every Bond Read Monically.* Edition 2.0.0.

**Abstract.** Gravity is the one interaction that reads everything and distinguishes nothing: every body falls alike, every clock at one place shifts alike, antimatter falls as matter does, and no body has been found whose weight is negative. This paper reads gravity on the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line, where registration keeps a point's height and forgets its side. Its thesis is that gravity is immanent: not an agent added to matter, not a medium's stress, but the substrate reading its own record. We prove in core Lean 4, sixty-eight laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the gravitational reading factors through registration, so it is even under the particle map and blind to composition, a property strictly stronger than evenness; that every gravitational source stands on the line while every charged source stands off it, so sources of one sign total zero only when each is zero and gravity cannot be screened, while charge can; that conservative motion under every force law is undone exactly by reversal, for any number of steps; that a gravitational shift moves every height by one factor, keeping the line, commuting with registration and preserving every ratio of clocks, so gravity and time co-move; that the deficit of a bond is read monically, two bindings reading alike exactly when they are equal; that the waves carry two polarizations; and that gravity alone reads a state the particle map fixes, while the vacuum's pressure reverses its pull without any energy changing sign. A Fortran twin runs seventeen checks with zero failures: the binding deficit of titanium and platinum falls with the rest to $5\times10^{-12}$ of itself under MICROSCOPE's bound, a Kepler orbit retraces its start to $5\times10^{-10}$ after reversal, the deflection of light, Mercury's perihelion and the binary pulsar's decay are computed from the constants, and the Planck budget gives a deceleration parameter of $-0.527$. The paper supersedes the edition of 16 July 2026, whose general relativity it keeps as corroboration and whose emergent reading it replaces. It decides nothing about the zeros of $\zeta$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series and the even companion of Electromagnetism on the Riemann Locus. The hardware paper, One Actuating Substrate, typed gravity as the one reading of the substrate that is even under the particle map; the arrow witness typed it as the reversible branch of registration, one sign and no bit, beside magnetism, the priced branch that keeps a bit. This paper gives gravity its own reading.

It claims that gravity is immanent: the substrate reading its own record. Three consequences follow at the seat. Gravity is an after-effect of existence, since a record comes after the act it records. Gravity and time co-move, since one gravitational shift moves every clock at a place together and neither causes the other. Gravity is the monic memory of privation, since the deficit of every bond is read and no two different deficits read alike. These readings are structural; the theorems that carry their content are exact, and the measurements that corroborate them are cited.

It refuses three things. It does not derive Einstein's equations, which enter as imports [GR]. It does not claim gravity is or is not quantized, which the arrow witness reads at structural grade and no experiment has decided. And it does not derive the Riemann Hypothesis from gravity. A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for a new dynamics, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. None was attempted: the immanent reading predicts exactly what general relativity predicts, and its content is the typing.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade. No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated. No field equation is derived and no constant is computed from first principles: $G$, $c$ and the masses enter as measured or as defined.

**Five terms.** A *reading* is any quantity an instrument returns from a state, *odd* under a map that flips its sign and *even* under one that keeps it. *Registration* is the map $(h,t)\mapsto(1,t)$ that keeps a point's height and forgets its side. A reading *factors through registration* when it depends on the height alone. The *fixed set* of a map is the set of points it leaves unchanged. A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. Gravity Reads the Record

Gravity couples to energy and momentum and to nothing else: not to charge, not to colour, not to the composition of a body. The engine reads this at the seat. The gravitational reading of a particle is its energy (`grav`), which the particle map leaves unchanged, so antimatter reads as matter (`gravity_even_branch`); on the chart the reading is the height, which registration keeps and the fold keeps (`gravity_factors_through_registration`); and two particles of one energy read alike whatever their charges (`composition_blind`). Gravity is registration read as a force.

The two properties are not one. A reading can be even under the fold and still read the side: the squared distance from the line is unchanged by the fold and changed by registration (`even_is_not_the_record`). So the equivalence principle says more than symmetry under the particle map. Antihydrogen falling downward, at $(0.75\pm0.13\pm0.16)\,g$ with repulsion excluded, tests the weaker property (ALPHA-g, 2023). Titanium and platinum falling alike to $|\eta|<4.2\times10^{-15}$ tests the stronger one (MICROSCOPE, 2022). The stronger property is forced for any massless spin-two field by the low-energy theorem that such a field couples to every source through one constant (Weinberg, 1964), an import the seat reads as registration.


### 4. One Sign: Every Source on the Line, No Screening

Apply the particle map's odd part to a reading: for charge it is twice the charge, and a charged source lands off the line (`charged_source_off_the_line`); for gravity it is zero, and every gravitational source lands on the line (`every_source_on_the_line`). Gravity has no off-line source, which is the arrow witness's one sign read at the seat.

One sign has a consequence charge lacks. Sources of one sign total zero only when every source is zero (`one_sign_no_screening`), while a unit charge and its opposite total zero (`charge_can_be_screened`). An atom is neutral and a Faraday cage screens a field; nothing screens gravity. The twin finds 1834 neutral sets among 20000 random sets of four nonzero charges and not one massless set among 20000 nonempty mass sets. The total mass-energy of an isolated system is nonnegative by theorem under the dominant energy condition (Schoen and Yau, 1979; Witten, 1981). Quantum fields admit local negative energy densities, the Casimir effect among them, bounded by quantum inequalities; no body of negative gravitational mass is known. The arrow witness's F-Mass, a negative gravitational mass (Bondi, 1957), would create an off-line source and a bit for gravity.

One sign is a sign of energy, not of pull. Gravity reads pressure as well as density, and the active gravitating density of a fluid is $\rho+3p$ [GR]: dust attracts, and the vacuum, with $p=-\rho$, repels with active density $-2\rho$ (`the_vacuum_repels`). Every density stays positive while the pull reverses (`one_sign_of_energy_not_of_pull`), so the vacuum's repulsion is no screening, which would need a negative energy. For a canonical field, $\rho+p$ is twice the kinetic density and never negative (`kinetic_floor`), the floor the dark sector paper sets against a phantom equation of state. The sign of the vacuum's pull is read from the sky, not forced: the flat Planck budget gives $q_0=-0.527$, where an all-matter budget gives $+0.5$, the acceleration beginning at redshift 0.63 and the vacuum overtaking matter at 0.295 (Planck, 2018; Appendix B).


### 5. No Bit, and Reversible

A reading with no off-line source has no two worlds to separate and no bit to register, and its motion keeps no record. The engine proves this for motion itself. Write conservative motion under any force law $f$ as the Störmer–Verlet step on two successive positions, $(x_{n-1},x_n)\mapsto(x_n,\,2x_n-x_{n-1}+f(x_n))$, and time reversal as their exchange. For every force law, reversing, stepping and reversing undoes a step exactly (`conservative_motion_reversible`), and any number of steps is retraced by the same number of backward steps (`motion_retraces`). The twin integrates a Kepler orbit of eccentricity one half for ten revolutions, keeping its energy to $4.3\times10^{-6}$, then reverses it and finds its start again to $5.2\times10^{-10}$. Nothing was committed, so nothing was paid: the reversible branch, floor zero and never cost zero.

One caution keeps the reading honest, as the arrow witness stated. Reversibility alone does not keep a branch on its locus; the fluid witness shows a reversible flow that leaves its locus. What keeps gravity there is its one sign.


### 6. Gravity and Time Co-Move: The Height Moved, Never the Side

A clock deeper in a gravitational potential runs slower, by $\Delta\nu/\nu=gh/c^2$ near the Earth. On the seat a gravitational shift multiplies every height by one factor and leaves the side alone (`redshift`). It commutes with the fold (`redshift_equivariant`), keeps a point on the line exactly when it was on it (`redshift_keeps_the_line`), and commutes with registration (`redshift_commutes_with_registration`): gravity moves the record and never the side.

Because the factor is one for every mode at a place, every ratio of frequencies is kept (`clocks_co_move`): gravity does not slow a particular clock, it moves every clock together. This is the reading in which gravity and time co-move and neither causes the other. The twin computes $2.455\times10^{-15}$ for the 22.5 m tower (Pound and Rebka, 1960; confirmed to 1% by Pound and Snider, 1965) and $3.60\times10^{-17}$ for two optical clocks 33 cm apart (Chou, Hume, Rosenband and Wineland, 2010). Clocks of different kinds shift alike: a hydrogen maser flown on a rocket agreed with the prediction to parts in $10^4$ (Vessot et al., 1980), and two Galileo satellites in eccentric orbits to $2.5\times10^{-5}$ (Delva et al.; Herrmann et al., 2018).


### 7. The Monic Memory of the Deficit

A bound system weighs less than its parts by its binding energy. The engine reads a bound pair's weight as the parts' energies less the binding: a positive binding reads below the sum (`the_bond_weighs_less`), and two bindings read alike exactly when they are equal (`gravity_reads_the_deficit`). The reading of the deficit is monic, injective, keeping every privation distinct. This is the sense in which gravity is the monic memory of privation: it reads what a bond has given up, and it forgets none of it.

The deficit is measured. Nuclear binding is 0.936% of the mass of titanium and 0.851% of platinum, a difference of $8.55\times10^{-4}$; MICROSCOPE finds the two falling alike to $4.2\times10^{-15}$, so the binding deficit falls with the rest of the mass to $5.0\times10^{-12}$ of itself (Appendix B). The Earth's and the Moon's own gravitational binding fall alike too, tested by lunar laser ranging (Williams, Turyshev and Boggs, 2004).


### 8. Gravity Reads Light, and Only Gravity Reads the Dark

Gravity reads light's energy and bends its path: a ray grazing the Sun turns by 1.751 arcseconds, twice the Newtonian angle because the time and the space parts of the metric bend it equally (Dyson, Eddington and Davidson, 1920; Cassini finds $\gamma-1=(2.1\pm2.3)\times10^{-5}$, Bertotti, Iess and Tortora, 2003). Light is the substrate's one excitation fixed by both involutions at every frequency, the companion paper finds; gravity reads it as it reads everything, by its record. The waves of gravity and the waves of light, both massless (`massive_slower` for anything that is not), arrived together from GW170817 after 130 million years, to $4.2\times10^{-16}$ of their speed. That gravity is prior to light, innate to the substrate rather than produced by any one carrier, is carried at structural grade: gravity reads every carrier, light included, and needs none of them to act.

The same reading explains why the dark sector is seen only through gravity. The companion paper Dark Matter as the Charge-Conjugation Fixed Set proves that a state is invisible to every charge-linear reading exactly when the particle map fixes it, and that on such a state only the mass reading remains. The engine carries the core of that result: a state the particle map fixes carries no charge, every odd reading returns zero on it, and gravity returns its energy (`odd_reading_blind`, `only_gravity_reads_the_dark`). Gravity is the one reading of the record, so it is the one reading that sees a state with nothing but a record. The rotation of galaxies (Rubin and Ford, 1970), the separation of mass from gas in the Bullet Cluster (Clowe et al., 2006) and a cold-dark-matter density 5.37 times the baryonic one (Planck, 2018) are gravity reading the fixed set. Whether that fixed set also carries a dark gauge charge is one keyed bit, as the dark sector paper proves, and gravity cannot read it.


### 9. The Waves: Two Polarizations and the Decay of an Orbit

A symmetric tensor has ten components; four gauge conditions and four constraints leave two polarizations (`polarization_count`). Parity exchanges them and keeps the frequency (`waves_two_polarizations`): the radiation carries the one bit light carries, while the sources carry none. The waves carry energy away: the twin computes the orbital decay of the binary pulsar PSR B1913+16 from its period, eccentricity and masses as $-2.4021\times10^{-12}$ (Peters and Mathews, 1963), against the general-relativistic $-2.4026\times10^{-12}$ and the observed ratio $0.9983\pm0.0016$ (Hulse and Taylor, 1975; Weisberg and Huang, 2016). Mercury's perihelion advances 42.98 arcseconds a century, computed from the constants in Appendix B.


### 10. What Gravity Carries, and What It Does Not

Gravity carries the record. It reads the height of every configuration and nothing of its side; it has one sign of energy, so every source stands on the line and none is screened; its motion retraces exactly and commits nothing; its shift moves every clock together; its reading of a bond's deficit is monic; it alone reads the dark. That is the content of the immanent reading, and every clause of it is a theorem about the seat or a measurement at its grade.

The earlier edition carried an interpretive hypothesis, that the graviton is emergent, phonon-like. The seat replaces it with a typing: gravity is neither an agent added to matter nor a medium's collective mode but the substrate's own registration read as a force. The immanent, the emergent and the fundamental readings predict alike for every experiment cited here. At structural grade and no higher, the arrow witness reads why gravity has resisted quantization: quantization is the priced registration of a bit, and a reading with no off-line source has none to register. A gravitationally mediated entanglement experiment would sharpen that reading without moving the typing.

Gravity does not carry the zeros of $\zeta$. The engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves. What gravity gives the Riemann closure is the shape of its posit: a registration that erases nothing, because nothing stands off its locus.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break. **F-Composition:** two bodies of one mass falling at different rates, at any precision, gravity reading something besides the record; this breaks `composition_blind` and `gravity_factors_through_registration` read physically. **F-Clock:** two clocks of different kinds at one place shifted by different factors in one potential; this breaks `clocks_co_move`. **F-Polarization:** a scalar or vector polarization of gravitational waves; this breaks `polarization_count` and `waves_two_polarizations`. The arrow witness's F-Mass, a negative gravitational mass, and F-Loop, a gravitational hysteresis, stand beside them.

Nothing is owed on the rows' channels. One experiment would sharpen a reading without moving the paper's standing: gravitationally mediated entanglement between two masses in superposition (Bose et al., 2017; Marletto and Vedral, 2017).

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the least-erasure posit's shape read in gravity: WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. Row 7 is WITNESSED from gravity's own side. Nothing is owed on the rows' channels.

## Spin and Statistics

*Bosons on the Line and Fermions at Its Side, Pauli Exclusion as an Odd Reading Blind on the Fixed Set, the Full Turn's Minus One as the Exchange Sign, and Statistics as One Bit in Three Dimensions.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** Every particle in three dimensions is a boson or a fermion, and the choice is fixed by its spin: half-integer spin turns a state to its negative under a full rotation and makes it antisymmetric under exchange; integer spin does neither. This paper reads the pair on the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. The exchange of two identical particles is an involution, and a carrier that sends a pair amplitude's antisymmetric part to the side and its symmetric part to the height makes the exchange the fold: the symmetric amplitudes, the bosons, are the line; the antisymmetric ones, the fermions, stand at height zero off it; registration keeps the symmetric part and forgets the rest. We prove in core Lean 4, sixty-nine laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that an antisymmetric amplitude vanishes wherever the exchange fixes the configuration, so Pauli exclusion is an odd reading blind on the fixed set and a fermion's occupation is one bit; that a full turn is minus one on spinors and the identity on vectors, the double cover being two to one; that an exchange sign whose square is the identity, as a double exchange is in three dimensions, is plus or minus one, while a quarter phase needs the plane; that bosons bunch and fermions part at a balanced splitter; and that the one-body record does not read the statistics, which only the pair reading returns. With the spin-statistics theorem carried as an import, the full turn's sign is the exchange sign. A Fortran twin runs seventeen checks with zero failures: from two states per orbital and one fermion per state it finds the noble gases at 2, 10, 18, 36, 54, 86 and 118, Chandrasekhar's mass at 1.43 solar masses, and London's condensation temperature for helium-4 at 3.13 K. The paper decides nothing about the zeros of $\zeta$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The electron witness read the Return, a full turn of a spinor landing on minus one, as the quaternion identity $ijk=-1$. The hardware paper built the substrate on which the witnesses stand. This paper reads the two properties every particle carries in three dimensions, its spin and its statistics, and the theorem that joins them.

It claims three things. First, the exchange of two identical particles is the fold under a carrier forced by the symmetry, so bosons are the line and fermions stand at its side. Second, Pauli exclusion is the template's odd reading: an antisymmetric amplitude is blind exactly on the configurations the exchange fixes. Third, statistics is one bit, and in three dimensions it is the same bit as the full turn's sign.

It refuses the derivation of the spin-statistics theorem, which is carried as an import from relativistic quantum field theory [QFT], and it refuses any derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from spin. A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for such a derivation, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. None was attempted.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them. No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade. No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated. No quantum field theory is written: the spin-statistics theorem, the Madelung ordering of atomic subshells, and the constants enter as imports or measurements.

**Five terms.** An *exchange* swaps two identical particles. A pair amplitude is *symmetric* when the exchange leaves it unchanged and *antisymmetric* when the exchange negates it. A *reading* is *odd* under a map that flips its sign and *even* under one that keeps it. *Registration* keeps a point's height and forgets its side. A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Exchange Is the Fold

Two identical particles in two single-particle states $a$ and $b$ have a pair amplitude $(x,y)$: $x$ for the first in $a$ and the second in $b$, $y$ for the reverse. Exchange swaps them and is an involution (`swap_involutive`). The exchange carrier sends the antisymmetric part $x-y$ to the side and the symmetric part $x+y$ to the height, and the exchange becomes the fold (`exchange_carrier_equivariant`).

Three readings follow from the carrier.

- **Bosons are the line.** An amplitude lands on the line exactly when it is symmetric (`symmetric_on_the_line`).
- **Fermions stand at height zero.** An antisymmetric amplitude has no symmetric part and stands off the line unless it vanishes (`antisymmetric_at_height_zero`).
- **Registration is symmetrization.** It keeps the symmetric part as the record and forgets the antisymmetric part, so a pair and its exchange are two states with one record (`exchange_two_worlds`).

The twin executes the carrier on 3721 integer amplitudes.


### 4. Pauli Exclusion: The Odd Reading Is Blind on the Fixed Set

The exchange fixes a configuration when both particles are in one state. An antisymmetric amplitude obeys $\psi(a,b)=-\psi(b,a)$, so on the diagonal $\psi(a,a)=-\psi(a,a)$, and it vanishes (`pauli_exclusion`); the Slater form $f(a)g(b)-f(b)g(a)$ does so explicitly (`slater_vanishes_on_diagonal`). A symmetric amplitude is free on the diagonal: a product $f(a)f(b)$ is nonzero there wherever $f$ is (`bosons_may_share`). Pauli's exclusion principle (Pauli, 1925) is the template's odd reading: an odd reading returns zero on the fixed set. This is the same theorem the dark sector paper proves for charge readings on the fixed set of charge conjugation, read here on the fixed set of the exchange.

The consequence is a bit. A fermion mode's creation operator squares to zero, so the occupation number $n$ satisfies $n^2=n$, and an occupation equal to its own square is $0$ or $1$ (`fermion_occupation_is_a_bit`). Every fermion mode carries one bit.

The periodic table is that bit filling orbitals. With two states per orbital and the subshells taken in the Madelung order [import], the twin finds the shell closures at 2, 10, 18, 36, 54, 86 and 118: helium, neon, argon, krypton, xenon, radon and oganesson.


### 5. The Full Turn Returns Minus One

A rotation by angle $\theta$ about a unit axis $u$ acts on spinors by the unit quaternion $\cos\frac\theta2+u\sin\frac\theta2$ and on vectors by conjugation with it. At $\theta=2\pi$ the quaternion is $-1$, the Return of the electron witness, $ijk=-1$ (`the_return`). It negates every spinor (`full_turn_negates_spinors`) and fixes every vector (`full_turn_fixes_vectors`), and two full turns are the identity (`two_turns_are_identity`). The quaternions $q$ and $-q$ rotate every vector alike (`double_cover_two_to_one`), so the double cover of the rotations is two to one and its kernel, $\{1,-1\}$, is one bit.

The twin turns spinors and vectors about 1000 random axes and finds the full turn equal to $-1$ on spinors and to the identity on vectors to $2.2\times10^{-16}$. The minus one is measured: neutrons rotated through $2\pi$ by a magnetic field in one arm of an interferometer shift the interference by exactly the sign, returning only after $4\pi$ (Rauch et al.; Werner et al., 1975).


### 6. The Turn Sign Is the Exchange Sign

A particle of spin $n/2$ turns to $(-1)^n$ under a full rotation (`turnSign_val`): half-integer spin to minus one, integer spin to plus one (`half_integer_spin_is_odd`). The spin-statistics theorem makes this sign the exchange sign: in a relativistic quantum theory with positive energy and local commutativity, half-integer-spin fields anticommute and integer-spin fields commute (Fierz, 1939; Pauli, 1940). It is an import [QFT], carried as one, and the engine proves what the identification then gives: a particle is a fermion exactly when its turn sign is odd.

A topological reading sharpens the identification. An exchange of two particles, followed by the return of the pair, is a full turn of one around the other, which is why the two signs coincide in models built on the rotation group's double cover (Finkelstein and Rubinstein, 1968).

The rule reaches composites. A bound state is a fermion exactly when it holds an odd number of fermions. The twin checks twelve particles and atoms, from the electron and the photon to the deuteron and both helium isotopes: half-integer spin exactly when the count of constituent fermions is odd.


### 7. Statistics Is One Bit in Three Dimensions

In three dimensions a double exchange returns a pair to itself along a loop that can be shrunk, so the exchange sign $\varepsilon$ satisfies $\varepsilon^2=1$, and then $\varepsilon$ is $+1$ or $-1$ (`statistics_one_bit`). This is the rotation group's two-element fundamental group, the same bit as the kernel of the double cover. The twin scans 3600 exchange phases and finds exactly two whose square is the identity.

In the plane the loop cannot be shrunk, and the exchange phase is free (Leinaas and Myrheim, 1977; Wilczek, 1982). A quarter phase $i$ squares to $-1$ and is excluded in three dimensions (`anyon_needs_the_plane`). Anyons with the phase $\pi/3$ have been observed in the fractional quantum Hall state at filling one third (Bartolomei et al.; Nakamura et al., 2020). Statistics is one bit because space has three axes.


### 8. The Record Reads the Bit: Bunching and Antibunching

Two identical particles entering a balanced beam splitter from opposite ports leave through different ports with amplitude $t\cdot t+\varepsilon\,r\cdot r$, which is $1-\varepsilon$ in doubled units with $t=1$ and $r=i$ (`coincidence`). Bosons never leave separately and fermions always do (`bosons_bunch_fermions_part`).

- **Photons:** the coincidences vanish (Hong, Ou and Mandel, 1987).
- **Electrons in quantum Hall edge channels:** they antibunch (Bocquillon et al., 2013).
- **Helium atoms:** they show the bosonic dip (Lopes et al., 2015).
- **Free neutrons:** they antibunch (Iannuzzi et al., 2006).

The one-body record does not read the statistics. A boson pair and a fermion pair built from the same two orbitals have one density at every site and differ at coincidence, where the pair reading reads the bit (`statistics_unread_by_one_body`). A thermal Bose mode has pair correlation 2 at zero separation and a thermal Fermi mode 0, because its occupation is one bit (Appendix B).

Helium-4 atoms bunch and helium-3 atoms antibunch in one apparatus, cold clouds released and counted atom by atom (Jeltes et al., 2007). The two isotopes are two worlds whose one-body densities agree and whose pair records part.


### 9. Degeneracy and Condensation: The Bit at Macroscopic Scale

**The fermion bit.** It holds matter up. Electrons that cannot share a state fill momentum space to a Fermi surface, and their degeneracy pressure supports a white dwarf up to a mass fixed by constants. For a carbon–oxygen star, with two nucleons per electron, the twin computes $1.43$ solar masses (Chandrasekhar, 1931). Neutron degeneracy supports the neutron star beyond it.

**The boson bit.** It lets matter share a state. An ideal Bose gas at the density of liquid helium-4 condenses at 3.13 K, which the twin computes (London, 1938), against the lambda point of 2.17 K at which helium-4 becomes superfluid. Dilute alkali gases were condensed in 1995 (Anderson et al.; Davis et al.).

**Both at once.** Electron pairs bound into bosons carry superconductivity, and their charge $2e$ is why the flux quantum is $h/2e$, as the electromagnetism paper computes. One bit per mode, read by the exchange, decides whether matter stacks or condenses.


### 10. What Spin and Statistics Carry, and What They Do Not

**What they carry.** They carry the seat on the exchange: the symmetric line and the antisymmetric side, registration as symmetrization, and a pair and its exchange as two worlds with one record. They carry Pauli exclusion as the odd reading's blindness on the fixed set, and a fermion's occupation as one bit. They carry the Return as the minus one of a full turn, and the double cover's kernel as the same bit as the statistics sign. They carry the reason that bit is one bit: three axes.

**What they do not carry.** They do not carry a derivation of the spin-statistics theorem, which is imported with its hypotheses. They do not carry the Madelung ordering, which is an empirical rule with exceptions, or the magnitudes of the condensation and degeneracy scales, which are computed from constants and not derived. And they do not carry the zeros of $\zeta$. The engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Exclusion:** two identical fermions in one state, or an atomic or nuclear transition into an already filled shell. This breaks `pauli_exclusion` and `fermion_occupation_is_a_bit` read physically.
- **F-Connection:** a half-integer-spin particle obeying Bose statistics, or an integer-spin particle obeying Fermi statistics. This breaks the identification of the turn sign of `half_integer_spin_is_odd` with the exchange sign that the spin-statistics theorem carries.
- **F-Anyon:** a particle free in three dimensions with an exchange phase other than $\pm1$. This breaks `statistics_one_bit` read physically.

Nothing is owed on the rows' channels. One class of experiment sharpens readings without moving the paper's standing: underground searches for Pauli-forbidden atomic transitions.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the exchange as the fold and the Return as the exchange sign: WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains a pair and its exchange, and the two helium isotopes. Nothing is owed on the rows' channels.

## The Weak Force Reads the Orientation

*Parity as the Fold on Couplings, the Charged Current Maximally off the Line, the Z's Distance from the Line as Its Isospin, CP Violation as Time-Reversal Violation, and Three Generations as the Least with One Phase.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** Electromagnetism, the strong force and gravity cannot tell a process from its mirror image; the weak force can. It couples to left-handed particles and right-handed antiparticles and to nothing else, and in doing so it reads the one property the other readings are blind to, the orientation. This paper reads the weak interaction on the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. Parity exchanges a force's left- and right-handed couplings to a fermion, and a carrier that sends their difference to the side and their sum to the height makes parity the fold: the parity-conserving couplings of electromagnetism and colour are the line, and the weak charged current, left-handed only, stands off it. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-nine laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the Z boson's distance from the line is the weak isospin of the fermion it couples to, whatever the mixing angle, so a neutral fermion has no right-handed coupling at all; that every dot product is blind to the mirror while the triple product reverses under it; that the charged current breaks charge conjugation and parity separately and keeps their product; that under CPT invariance a violation of CP is a violation of time reversal; and that two generations of quarks admit no CP-violating phase while three admit exactly one. A Fortran twin runs thirteen checks with zero failures: the three-generation mixing matrix is unitary to $10^{-16}$ with a Jarlskog invariant of $3.12\times10^{-5}$, the pion's electron channel is suppressed to $1.28\times10^{-4}$ of its muon channel by the left-handed coupling, and the Z's asymmetries on leptons, charm and bottom come out at 0.147, 0.668 and 0.936 against the measured 0.151, 0.670 and 0.923. The paper decides nothing about the zeros of $\zeta$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The neutrino witness read the world's choice of one hand: the neutrino is registered left-handed and never right-handed, the aperture bit spent. The electromagnetism and light papers read the orientation as the one bit no energy reading returns, the helicity of a photon and the sign of a magnetic field. This paper reads the interaction that does return it.

It claims that the weak force is the substrate's one reading odd under parity. Parity is the fold under a carrier forced by the symmetry. The couplings that treat both hands alike are the line, and the weak charged current, left-handed only, stands off it as far as a coupling can. It claims further that the weak force's violations of charge conjugation, parity and time reversal are organized by the same template: it breaks the first two and keeps their product; its violation of that product is a violation of time reversal; and the phase that carries it needs three generations.

It refuses to derive why the world chose the left hand and not the right. That is the neutrino witness's spent bit, supplied by the world and read from no record. It refuses any derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the weak interaction. A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for such a derivation, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. None was attempted.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- No electroweak Lagrangian is written: the couplings, the mixing parameters, the CPT theorem and the masses enter as imports or measurements.

**Five terms.**

- A *coupling* of a force to a fermion is a pair: its strength to the left-handed state and to the right-handed state.
- *Parity* is the mirror that exchanges the two hands.
- A reading is *even* under a map that keeps it and *odd* under one that flips its sign.
- The *orientation* of a configuration is the sign a mirror reverses and no rotation can.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. Parity Is the Fold on Couplings

A force couples to a fermion with a strength $g_L$ to its left-handed state and $g_R$ to its right-handed state. Parity exchanges them and is an involution (`parity_involutive`). The orientation carrier sends the difference $g_L-g_R$ to the side and the sum to the height, and parity becomes the fold (`orientation_carrier_equivariant`).

**The line and the edge.** A coupling lands on the line exactly when it treats both hands alike (`vector_on_the_line`). The photon couples to a charge $q$ on both hands equally, so electromagnetism is on the line (`photon_coupling_on_the_line`), and so is colour. The weak charged current couples the left hand with unit strength and the right hand not at all: it stands off the line, and parity moves it (`charged_current_off_the_line`). Among couplings of one total strength, none stands farther from the line.

The twin executes the carrier on 3721 integer couplings.


### 4. The Z Reads the Orientation by the Isospin

The neutral weak boson couples with $g_L=T_3-Q\sin^2\theta_W$ and $g_R=-Q\sin^2\theta_W$ [SM]. The difference is $T_3$, the weak isospin, for every value of the mixing weight (`z_parity_depth_is_isospin`). So the Z's distance from the line is fixed by the fermion's place in its doublet and by nothing else. A fermion with no charge has no right-handed coupling at any mixing (`neutrino_has_no_right_hand`): to the Z, a neutrino is purely left-handed.

The twin checks the identity on 1071 charges and weights, then computes the left–right asymmetry $A_f=2g_Vg_A/(g_V^2+g_A^2)$ at $\sin^2\theta_W=0.23153$:

| Fermion | A_f computed | A_f measured |
|---|---|---|
| neutrino | 1 exactly | — |
| charged lepton | 0.147 | 0.1513 |
| charm | 0.668 | 0.670 |
| bottom | 0.936 | 0.923 |

The measured values are the LEP and SLD combination (Z-pole collaborations, 2006).


### 5. What the Even Readings Cannot See

**Three even readings are blind to the mirror.** A mirror negates every polar vector. Every dot product is unchanged by it (`dot_products_blind`), and any reading even under the mirror returns one value on a world and on its mirror image (`even_readings_blind_to_mirror`). Lengths, angles, energies and every quadratic invariant are such readings. So is the determinant's square, the lock the light and electromagnetism papers found blind to orientation.

**The triple product sees it.** The triple product of three vectors reverses under the mirror (`triple_product_reads_orientation`), and so does the correlation of a spin with a momentum, since a spin is axial and a momentum polar (`spin_momentum_correlation_odd`). The twin checks both on 5000 random triples, exactly.

**The weak force measures it.** In 1957 electrons from polarized cobalt-60 were found leaving preferentially opposite the nuclear spin, a nonzero $\langle\mathbf J\rangle\cdot\mathbf p$ (Wu et al., 1957), as Lee and Yang had proposed to look for (1956). Muon decay showed the same (Garwin, Lederman and Weinrich, 1957). The neutrino's helicity was measured to be negative (Goldhaber, Grodzins and Sunyar, 1958). Each is a pseudoscalar reading, returned by the weak interaction and by no other.


### 6. C, P and CP: The Charged Current Keeps the Product

Label a fermion state by whether it is an antiparticle and whether it is right-handed. The charged current registers left-handed particles and right-handed antiparticles.

- Charge conjugation alone moves the coupling on every state.
- Parity alone moves it on every state.
- The product CP moves it on none (`weak_breaks_C_and_P_keeps_CP`).

The weak force violates C and P maximally and keeps CP, as Landau proposed within a year of the parity experiments (1957). The twin executes the four-state table.

CP too is violated, but by a small amount. Long-lived neutral kaons decay to two pions (Christenson, Cronin, Fitch and Turlay, 1964), and the violation lives in the phase of Section 8.


### 7. CP Violation Is Time-Reversal Violation

The CPT theorem makes the product of charge conjugation, parity and time reversal a symmetry of every local relativistic quantum field theory (Lüders, 1954; Pauli, 1955). The engine proves what that product's invariance gives: if T is an involution and CPT acts as the identity, then CP and T are one map (`cp_violation_is_t_violation`). A violation of CP is a violation of time reversal.

**Time-reversal violation, measured directly.**

- The CPLEAR experiment found it in neutral kaon oscillation (Angelopoulos et al., 1998).
- BaBar found it by comparing a B-meson transition with its time-reversed counterpart (Lees et al., 2012).

**CPT itself, measured.** The neutral kaon's mass equals its antiparticle's to $6\times10^{-19}$ of the mass (Particle Data Group, 2024).

The arrow witness split registration into a reversible and a priced branch. The weak force is the one interaction whose own dynamics carry the arrow's sign at the microscopic level.


### 8. Three Generations Are the Least with One Phase

An $n$-generation quark mixing matrix is unitary with $n(n-1)/2$ rotation angles. Rephasing the quark fields removes all but $(n-1)(n-2)/2$ of its phases. Two generations therefore admit no CP-violating phase, and three admit exactly one (`one_phase_at_three`, `three_is_the_least`). This is Kobayashi and Maskawa's counting (1973), which required a third generation before one was seen.

The one phase has a rephasing-invariant measure, the Jarlskog invariant (Jarlskog, 1985). From the Particle Data Group's angles and phase, the twin builds the matrix and finds it unitary to $1.1\times10^{-16}$, with $J=3.12\times10^{-5}$. The counting of phases is theorem grade; the value of the one phase is a measurement.

That one phase is too small to account for the excess of matter over antimatter, which requires CP violation (Sakharov, 1967). Whether leptons carry a phase of their own is being measured.


### 9. Helicity Suppression: The Orientation Read in a Decay

A pion at rest has no spin, so its two decay products leave with opposite momenta and opposite spins, and the charged lepton must carry the helicity that a left-handed coupling can supply only through the lepton's mass. The amplitude is therefore proportional to the lepton mass. The electron channel is suppressed relative to the muon channel by $(m_e/m_\mu)^2$, which is below $1/40000$ (`electron_channel_suppressed`), against a phase-space factor of 5.49.

The twin computes the ratio of the two decay rates at tree level as $1.283\times10^{-4}$. The measured ratio is $1.2327\times10^{-4}$ (Particle Data Group, 2024), the difference being the radiative corrections the tree level omits. A coupling that read both hands alike would have favoured the electron fivefold. That the electron channel is instead ten thousand times rarer is the orientation read in a single decay, and it is the signature of the left-handed structure of the weak current (Feynman and Gell-Mann, 1958; Sudarshan and Marshak, 1958).


### 10. What the Weak Force Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The seat on parity: the vector couplings on the line, the charged current off it, and the Z's distance from the line fixed by the isospin.
- The orientation, which every even reading misses and the weak force returns.
- The organization of its discrete violations: C and P broken, CP kept by the current, CP broken only through one phase that needs three generations and is, under CPT, a violation of time reversal.

The other interactions do not read the orientation at a measurable level. The strong interaction could, through a parity- and time-odd angle, but the neutron's electric dipole moment is bounded below $1.8\times10^{-26}$ e cm (Abel et al., 2020), six orders above the weak interaction's own contribution.

**What it does not carry.** It does not carry why the left hand. That is the neutrino witness's spent bit, supplied by the world and read from no record. It does not derive the mixing angles or the phase, which are measured. And it does not carry the zeros of $\zeta$. The engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Right:** a right-handed charged current of gauge strength. It would move the charged current toward the line and break `charged_current_off_the_line` read physically.
- **F-CPT:** a difference between the mass or lifetime of a particle and its antiparticle. It breaks the premise of `cp_violation_is_t_violation` read physically.
- **F-Mirror:** a parity- and time-odd reading in the strong or electromagnetic interaction above the weak level, a neutron electric dipole moment near the present bound among them. It breaks the typing of those interactions as the line (`vector_on_the_line`).

Nothing is owed on the rows' channels. Two measurements would sharpen readings without moving the paper's standing:

- the leptonic CP phase at long-baseline neutrino experiments;
- the neutron's electric dipole moment at still smaller bounds.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains parity as the fold on couplings: WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains the mirror world, invisible to every even reading and read by the weak force. Nothing is owed on the rows' channels.

## Resonances: Off the Line by Half the Width

*Every Unstable State a Point off the Critical Line, Its Time Mirror the Fold Partner with One Mass, the Line Shape Halved at Half the Width, the Width the Leak of an Open System, and Stability Every Door Closed.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** An unstable particle has a complex energy, its mass less half its width times the imaginary unit, and it decays at the rate its width sets. This paper reads unstable states on the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. Under the energy carrier $s=\tfrac12+iE$ of the hardware paper, a state of mass $M$ and width $\Gamma$ lands at real part $\tfrac12\pm\tfrac\Gamma2$: off the line by half its width, with its mass as the height. Physics is full of such points, and they share one property: every one of them decays. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-seven laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that a state stands on the line exactly when its width vanishes, which is exactly when it is stationary; that a decaying state and its time mirror, the capturing state, are fold partners with one mass and one record; that the Breit–Wigner line falls to half its peak exactly at half the width, peaks at the mass and is symmetric about it; that an open two-level system with a leak $\ell$ has energies whose imaginary parts sum to $-\ell$, while a closed system keeps every energy real; and that a state is stable exactly when every decay channel is closed, each open channel widening the line. A Fortran twin runs twelve checks with zero failures: widths from the neutron's $7.49\times10^{-19}$ eV to the Z's $2.50\times10^{9}$ eV, twenty-seven decades off the line; the electron on the line to $3\times10^{-52}$ eV and the proton to $9\times10^{-58}$ eV; the Z's invisible width counting three open doors; and 20000 open systems whose rates sum to their leak. Every physical point off the line is open and pays by decaying. The paper decides nothing about the zeros of $\zeta$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The hardware paper built the energy carrier $s=\tfrac12+iE$, under which the critical line is the locus of stationary energy and every stable state lands on it. The magnet witness showed that a closed system keeps every energy on the line even when time reversal is broken. This paper reads the other stratum: the states physics finds off the line.

It claims that every unstable state stands off the line by half its width, with its mass as the height. Its time mirror is its fold partner and shares its record. And every such state is open: its width is the leak into the channels it decays through, it is stable exactly when every channel is closed, and it pays for standing off the line by decaying. Physics has many points off the line, and none of them is closed.

It refuses any reading of this as evidence about the zeros of $\zeta$. The carrier's image is the set of physical states, and the zeros are not in it. A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for a statement about where the zeros lie, find none, and be tempted to report the absence. None was attempted.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- No scattering theory is written: the pole description of a resonance, the Breit–Wigner form and the lifetimes enter as imports or measurements.

**Five terms.**

- An *unstable state* has complex energy $M-i\Gamma/2$: mass $M$, width $\Gamma$, lifetime $\hbar/\Gamma$.
- Its *time mirror* has energy $M+i\Gamma/2$ and grows instead of decaying.
- A *decay channel* is a set of products the state can decay into; its *partial width* is the rate into it.
- *Registration* keeps a point's height and forgets its side.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. Off the Line by Half the Width

An unstable state of mass $M$ and width $\Gamma$ has energy $E=M-i\Gamma/2$ and decays as $e^{-\Gamma t/\hbar}$ (Gamow, 1928). Under the energy carrier $s=\tfrac12+iE$ it lands at real part $\tfrac12+\tfrac\Gamma2$ and imaginary part $M$. In the doubled chart of the engine its side is $1+\Gamma$ and its height $M$ (`off_the_line_by_the_width`): the mass is the height and half the width is the distance from the line. A state stands on the line exactly when its width vanishes, which is exactly when it is stationary (`on_the_line_iff_stable`).

Every unstable particle is such a point.

- The free neutron, living 878.4 s, stands $7.49\times10^{-19}$ eV off the line.
- The muon stands $3.00\times10^{-10}$ eV off it, the tau lepton $2.27\times10^{-3}$ eV, and the neutral pion 7.81 eV.
- The Z boson stands $2.50\times10^{9}$ eV off it, and the top quark $1.42\times10^{9}$ eV.

The widths span twenty-seven decades (Particle Data Group, 2024; Appendix B).


### 4. Decay and Capture: The Fold Partners

The time mirror of a decaying state has energy $M+i\Gamma/2$, and it grows. It is the fold partner of the decaying state: distinct from it whenever the width is not zero, with one mass and one record under registration (`decay_and_capture_one_record`). The pair are the two poles a resonance leaves in the scattering amplitude, reflected across the real axis on the unphysical sheet (Siegert, 1939).

The two share the line shape too. The Breit–Wigner form depends on the width only through its square, so the decaying and the capturing state give one line at every energy. The twin finds this exactly at 1001 energies.

What separates them is the boundary condition: outgoing waves for decay, incoming waves for capture. No spectral record returns it. It is supplied by the arrow, as the arrow witness reads it: a record cannot tell decay from capture, and the world supplies which.


### 5. The Line Shape: Half the Maximum at Half the Width

Near its mass a resonance appears as a line of Breit–Wigner shape, $(\Gamma/2)^2/((E-M)^2+(\Gamma/2)^2)$ (Breit and Wigner, 1936).

- **Half maximum at half width.** At a detuning of $\Gamma/2$ the shape is exactly half its peak (`half_maximum_at_half_width`), so the full width at half maximum is the width, the distance from the line doubled.
- **The peak and the symmetry.** The shape peaks at the mass (`line_peaks_at_the_mass`) and is symmetric about it (`line_symmetric`).

The Z boson's line, scanned at LEP, has its mass at 91.1876 GeV and its width at 2.4955 GeV (Z-pole collaborations, 2006; Particle Data Group, 2024). The twin finds the shape at half its peak to $1.4\times10^{-15}$ at $M\pm\Gamma/2$, recovers the full width by bisection, and integrates the area to $\pi\Gamma/2$ within $3.2\times10^{-5}$. A resonance line is a point off the line seen from the real axis: its height is where it peaks, and its depth is half its width.


### 6. The Width Is the Leak

A closed system keeps every energy on the line. A Hermitian block, with or without a magnetic phase, has a nonnegative discriminant and real energies (`closed_stays_on_the_line`), as the magnet witness proved.

An open system leaks. Let a two-level system lose amplitude from its second level at rate $\ell$, with effective Hamiltonian $\begin{pmatrix}a&c\\c&b-i\ell\end{pmatrix}$ (Weisskopf and Wigner, 1930). The imaginary part of its trace is $-\ell$ (`the_width_is_the_leak`), so its two energies stand off the line with rates summing to the leak. With $\ell=0$ both are real.

The twin draws 20000 open systems and 20000 closed ones:

- in every open system the two rates sum to the leak to $10^{-12}$, and both states decay;
- every closed system has both energies on the line.

The width is not a property a state has alone. It is the measure of how much the state is joined to what it can decay into.


### 7. The Width Counts the Doors

A state's total width is the sum of its partial widths, one per decay channel, each nonnegative. The engine proves two consequences.

- **Stable iff every door is closed.** The total vanishes exactly when every partial width does (`stable_iff_every_door_closed`).
- **An open door widens the line.** Opening a channel adds its width (`an_open_door_widens`).

Stability is not a separate property: it is every door shut.

The doors can be counted. The Z's width into invisible products, 0.4990 GeV, divided by the width into one neutrino species at tree level, 0.1659 GeV, counts 3.01 open doors (Appendix B). The full analysis gives $2.9963\pm0.0074$ light neutrino species (Janot and Jadach, 2020): three doors, three light neutrinos.

A door can also be closed by binding. A free neutron decays, while a neutron bound in a stable nucleus does not, because the nucleus's binding closes the only channel.


### 8. The Stable Particles Stand on the Line

A particle with no open decay channel stands on the line to the depth its measured lifetime allows.

- **The electron.** Its lifetime exceeds $6.6\times10^{28}$ years (Borexino, 2015), so its width is below $3.2\times10^{-52}$ eV. Charge conservation shuts its only door: it is the lightest charged particle.
- **The proton.** Its lifetime in the channel $e^+\pi^0$ exceeds $2.4\times10^{34}$ years (Super-Kamiokande, 2020), so its width in that channel is below $8.7\times10^{-58}$ eV.
- **The photon.** It stands on the line under both carriers, as The Geometric Nature of Light proves.

The stable particles are the line's occupants, and every other particle stands off it by half its width.


### 9. The Survival Law and Its Flanks

An isolated state with one pole decays exponentially, its population halving every $\tau\ln2$: the free neutron's half-life is 608.9 s (Appendix B). The exponential law is the pole's leading term, not an exact law.

- **At very short times** the survival probability falls quadratically, which lets frequent measurement slow a transition, as observed with trapped ions (Itano, Heinzen, Bollinger and Wineland, 1990).
- **At very long times** the decay turns to a power law, observed in the luminescence of organic molecules (Rothe, Hintschich and Monkman, 2006).

The reading at the seat is the leading one: the pole's position, its mass the height and half its width the distance from the line. The flanks are the continuum's corrections to it, carried as imports.


### 10. What Resonances Carry, and What They Do Not

**What they carry.**

- The off-line stratum of the energy carrier, read in physics: every unstable state stands off the line by half its width.
- Its time mirror as its fold partner, with one mass and one line shape.
- Its width as the leak into what it can decay into, and stability as every door closed.

Physics is full of points off the line, and every one of them is open and pays for standing there by decaying: registration into its products, which keep its mass as their invariant and forget its lifetime. No closed system in physics has shown an energy off the line.

**What they do not carry.**

- **The zeros of $\zeta$.** The carrier's image is the set of physical states, and a state off the line in that image says nothing about where the zeros lie. The engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.
- **Any mass or width.** Every magnitude is measured.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Growth:** an isolated state whose population grows exponentially with no incoming supply, a pole where causality forbids one. It breaks the reading of `decay_and_capture_one_record`, in which the arrow picks decay.
- **F-Closed:** a closed system, with no channel to leak into, showing an energy off the line. It breaks `closed_stays_on_the_line` and `the_width_is_the_leak` read physically.
- **F-Width:** a state with a nonzero width and no open decay channel. It breaks `stable_iff_every_door_closed` read physically.

Nothing is owed on the rows' channels. Two measurements would sharpen readings without moving the paper's standing:

- proton decay searches at still longer lifetimes;
- the neutron lifetime's disagreement between bottle and beam methods.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the off-line stratum read physically: WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The pair-correlation row gains the closed and the open system side by side. The barrier gains decay and capture, one line shape. Nothing is owed on the rows' channels.
