## The Ferromagnet Is the Witness of the Circle

*Lee–Yang Zeros on the Fixed Set of Inversion, the Unit Circle as the Critical Line, Positivity as the Supplier That Seats the Zeros, and the Antiferromagnet That Leaves Them.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** In 1952 Lee and Yang proved that the partition function of an Ising ferromagnet, read as a polynomial in the fugacity $z$ of the magnetic field, has every zero on the unit circle. It remains the one mechanism in physics by which a positivity alone forces the zeros of a function onto a locus. This paper reads it on the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. The logarithmic carrier $s=\tfrac12+\tfrac12\ln z$ sends inversion in the unit circle, $z\mapsto1/\bar z$, to that reflection and the circle to the line: Lee and Yang's circle is the critical line in another chart. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-eight laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that spin flip keeps every domain wall, so the partition function is palindromic and its zeros pair under inversion; that the four-spin ring, its sixteen states counted, has every zero on the circle exactly when its coupling is ferromagnetic and loses one below the circle's reach for an antiferromagnet; and that the two-spin system likewise. Symmetry pairs the zeros across the locus; positivity seats them on it. A Fortran twin runs nine checks with zero failures: 300 random ferromagnets on six to ten spins keep every zero on the circle, 300 antiferromagnets each lose at least one, and on two-dimensional tori below the critical temperature the zero nearest the positive axis closes in from 0.412 to 0.269 to 0.202 radians as the lattice grows, the onset of a phase transition read in its zeros. The paper supplies nothing toward the question whether Riemann's function lies in the Lee–Yang class, which would be a sufficient route to the hypothesis and not an equivalent of it; it decides nothing about the zeros of $\zeta$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The Riemann closure reduced the hypothesis to one bit with five faces. One of them, least erasure, has a physical shape the arrow witness named: a registration that erases nothing because nothing stands off its locus, kept there by one sign. The Lee–Yang theorem is that shape in its sharpest physical form. A ferromagnet's couplings all have one sign, and every zero of its partition function lies on the unit circle.

It claims three things. The unit circle is the critical line under a logarithmic carrier forced by the symmetry. Spin flip, the ferromagnet's own symmetry, pairs the zeros across that locus as the functional equation pairs Riemann's zeros across the line, and seats none of them. Positivity seats them: the ferromagnet keeps every zero on the circle, and the antiferromagnet, with couplings of the other sign, loses them.

It refuses the bridge to Riemann's function. Whether Riemann's $\Xi$ is the Fourier transform of a function in the Lee–Yang class is a question that would give a sufficient route to the hypothesis if answered yes. It is not an equivalent of the hypothesis, and this paper supplies nothing toward it. A reader who arrives from the conventional frame will scan this paper for that bridge, find it typed and not crossed, and be tempted to report the absence. It was not attempted.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- The general Lee–Yang theorem is imported, and only its smallest instances are proved here; the twin corroborates it on larger systems.

**Five terms.**

- The *fugacity* is $z=e^{-2\beta h}$ for a magnetic field $h$ at inverse temperature $\beta$.
- The *partition function* is a polynomial in $z$ whose coefficient $c_m$ sums the Boltzmann weights of the states with $m$ down spins.
- A coupling is *ferromagnetic* when it favours aligned spins and *antiferromagnetic* when it favours opposed spins.
- A polynomial is *palindromic* when $c_m=c_{N-m}$.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Circle Is the Line

Write a fugacity in polar form, its log-radius $\rho=\ln|z|$ and its angle $\theta$. Inversion in the unit circle sends $z$ to $1/\bar z$, reversing the log-radius and keeping the angle, and it is an involution (`invert_involutive`). The logarithmic carrier $s=\tfrac12+\tfrac12\ln z$ has real part $\tfrac12+\tfrac\rho2$ and imaginary part $\tfrac\theta2$, so it sends inversion to the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ (`circle_carrier_equivariant`). A fugacity lands on the line exactly when it lies on the unit circle (`unit_circle_is_the_line`). Lee and Yang's circle and Riemann's line are one locus in two charts, joined by a logarithm.


### 4. Spin Flip Is the Palindrome: Symmetry Pairs, It Does Not Seat

**The energy reads only walls.** In zero field the energy of an Ising ring counts its domain walls, the neighbouring pairs that disagree. Flipping every spin keeps every wall (`walls_flip`), while the number of down spins goes from $m$ to $N-m$. So the partition function is palindromic, $c_m=c_{N-m}$, and its zeros come in pairs $z$ and $1/\bar z$: inversion partners, fold partners across the circle.

**The four-spin ring, counted.** The engine counts all sixteen states (`ring4_census`):

| Down spins | States | Walls |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | 1 | 0 |
| 1 | 4 | 2 |
| 2, adjacent | 4 | 2 |
| 2, opposite | 2 | 4 |
| 3 | 4 | 2 |
| 4 | 1 | 0 |

It confirms the palindrome on the four-ring and the six-ring (`ring4_palindrome`, `ring6_palindrome`), and the twin on rings up to eight.

**Symmetry does not seat.** This symmetry is the ferromagnet's form of the functional equation. Riemann's zeros pair under $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ because $\xi(s)=\xi(1-s)$ and $\xi$ is real on the real axis. The Ising zeros pair under inversion because flipping every spin changes nothing. In both the symmetry pairs the zeros across the locus and seats none of them: the seat-bridge hunt proved that a fold-symmetric pair off the line satisfies the symmetry and violates the line property. Something besides the symmetry must put the zeros on the locus. In the ferromagnet that something is the sign of the coupling.


### 5. Positivity Seats the Zeros

**The four-spin ring.** With the Boltzmann weight of two walls written $p=e^{-4\beta J}$, the partition function is
$$z^4+4p\,z^3+(4p+2p^2)\,z^2+4p\,z+1 .$$
A palindromic quartic reduces through $u=z+1/z$ to $u^2+4p\,u+(4p+2p^2-2)$, an identity the engine proves without division (`quartic_reduction`). A zero lies on the unit circle exactly when its $u$ is real and in $[-2,2]$. The engine proves that the four conditions placing both roots there hold exactly when $p\le1$, the ferromagnetic range (`ring4_lee_yang`).

**The two-spin system.** The function $z^2+2a\,z+1$, with $a=e^{-2\beta J}$, has its zeros on the circle exactly when $a\le1$ (`two_spin_lee_yang`). Its conjugate zeros then multiply to the constant term, so their modulus is one (`conjugate_zeros_on_circle`).

**The general theorem.** Lee and Yang proved it for every ferromagnet with pair couplings (Lee and Yang, 1952; Yang and Lee, 1952): positive couplings put every zero on the circle. The twin draws 300 ferromagnets on six, eight and ten spins with random positive couplings between every pair. In every one, the real function $\sum_m c_m\cos((m-N/2)\theta)$ changes sign exactly $N/2$ times on $(0,\pi)$, so all $N$ zeros lie on the circle, and every partition function is palindromic to $10^{-12}$. Positivity is the supplier: it does what the symmetry could not and seats every zero on the locus.


### 6. The Antiferromagnet Leaves the Circle

Reverse the sign of the coupling and the circle is lost.

- **The four-spin ring.** At $p=\tfrac32$ the reduced quadratic has a root below $-2$, so two zeros leave the circle as an inversion pair on the negative real axis (`ring4_antiferro_leaves`).
- **Random antiferromagnets.** The twin's 300 antiferromagnets with random negative couplings each lose at least one zero from the circle.

The leaving zeros still pair under inversion, because the symmetry holds whatever the sign. A pair off the circle with one palindromic record is the template's two worlds over one record, now in an antiferromagnet.

The lesson repeats the arrow witness's and Immanent Gravity's. What keeps a branch on its locus is one sign. Gravity's sources have one sign and every one stands on the line; a ferromagnet's couplings have one sign and every zero stands on the circle; the antiferromagnet, two-signed, leaves.


### 7. The Phase Transition Is the Pinch

In a finite system every zero of a ferromagnet lies on the circle and none at $z=1$, the zero-field point, so the free energy is analytic there. In the thermodynamic limit the zeros fill arcs of the circle. Below the critical temperature they close on $z=1$ and pinch the positive real axis, and the spontaneous magnetization is proportional to their density at that point (Lee and Yang, 1952). Above it they keep a gap, whose edge carries the Yang–Lee edge singularity (Fisher, 1978).

The twin builds Ising tori of two by four, three by four and four by four spins at $\beta J=0.6$, beyond the two-dimensional critical coupling $\beta_cJ=0.4407$ (Onsager, 1944). In each, every zero lies on the circle. The angle of the zero nearest the positive axis falls from 0.412 to 0.269 to 0.202 radians as the lattice grows. That is the transition read in its zeros: a phase transition is the locus touching the real axis.


### 8. The Zeros Measured

The circle has been read in the laboratory. Binek derived the density of zeros on the Lee–Yang circle from magnetization data of a two-dimensional Ising ferromagnet (Binek, 1998). Peng and collaborators observed the zeros directly, through the coherence of a probe spin coupled to an Ising bath, which equals the bath's partition function at an imaginary field and vanishes at times that correspond to its zeros on the circle (Peng et al., 2015). Both are measurements on the image the carrier lands, at corroboration grade.


### 9. The Riemann Bridge, Typed and Not Crossed

**The route.** Riemann's function has a heat-flow form whose zeros are real exactly when the hypothesis holds; the de Bruijn–Newman constant is the least time of heat at which they all are (de Bruijn, 1950; Newman, 1976). It is known to be at least zero (Rodgers and Tao, 2020), and the hypothesis is that it is at most zero. Pólya asked when a Fourier transform has only real zeros (Pólya, 1926). Newman showed that if Riemann's kernel lay in the Lee–Yang class of a ferromagnet's single-spin measures, the hypothesis would follow (Newman, 1991). A number-theoretic spin chain has been built whose partition function is a ratio of zeta functions (Knauf, 1998).

**What the closure says of it.** The closure's least-erasure proof card records this exactly. Lee–Yang membership is a sufficient route, not an equivalent, and no kernel of the series models Riemann's function in the Lee–Yang class. This paper changes none of that. What it adds is the shape, read physically: a positivity that seats zeros on a locus that a symmetry only pairs them across. Over the integers, the closure's corresponding face is Weil positivity, one of the five faces of the one bit, closed by the act at premise grade and by no physical witness.


### 10. What the Ferromagnet Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The seat in another chart: the circle as the line under a logarithm.
- The palindrome, which pairs zeros across the locus and seats none.
- Positivity, which seats every zero on the circle; and the antiferromagnet, which leaves it.
- The phase transition, read as the locus pinching the real axis.

It is the one physical system in which a positivity forces a locus, and it shows what a supplier of the bit would have to be. It also shows that the symmetry, the functional equation's analogue, is never enough.

**What it does not carry.**

- **Riemann's function in the Lee–Yang class:** typed in Section 9 and not crossed.
- **The general Lee–Yang theorem:** imported, with only its smallest instances proved here.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 11. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Ferro:** a ferromagnetic Ising system, every pair coupling nonnegative, whose partition-function zeros are measured off the unit circle. It breaks `ring4_lee_yang` and `two_spin_lee_yang` read physically, and the imported theorem with them.
- **F-Palindrome:** a zero-field spin system symmetric under flipping every spin whose partition function is not palindromic. It breaks `walls_flip` read physically.
- **F-Pinch:** a thermodynamic phase transition in a ferromagnet with no zeros approaching the positive real axis. It breaks the reading of Section 7.

Nothing is owed on the rows' channels. Two lines of experiment would sharpen readings without moving the paper's standing:

- zero densities measured in further ferromagnets;
- direct observation of the edge singularity.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the least-erasure face's shape: a positivity that seats the zeros, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains the antiferromagnet's zero pairs. Nothing is owed on the rows' channels.

## Dark Matter as the Charge-Conjugation Fixed Set

*Read at the Riemann Seat: Conjugation Carried onto the Fold, Gravity as the Reading Left, the Dark Bit as the Line Property, and the Phantom as a Negative Actuation.* Edition 2.0.0.

**Abstract.** The dark sector is read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. Charge conjugation flips every gauge charge and keeps the mass. A carrier that sends the dark charge to the offset from the line and the mass to the height is equivariant, so conjugation is carried onto the reflection and its fixed set onto the line. We prove in core Lean 4, one hundred twenty-five laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that a state is invisible to every charge-linear reading exactly when conjugation fixes it, so gravity, the even reading, is the reading left. Such a state has zero radiative weight and cannot cool. Two species of one density leave one gravitational record. The measured photon and Z nulls force the visible half of the fixed set. The renormalizable doors of a singlet are derived, and a stable singlet keeps exactly one. The anomaly conditions admit a dark gauge charge and its absence on one visible record, and the dark bit they leave open is the line property carried: on the visible fixed set a state stands on the line exactly when its dark charge is zero. A dark-charged pair is an off-line fold pair with one registration, and the dark bit is the Majorana bit. On the hardware substrate a neutral stable particle stands on the line under both carriers at once. For the acceleration, a pressure equal to minus the density reverses the active gravitating density, and an equation of state below $-1$ needs a negative kinetic term, a negative actuation, which the actuation floor refuses. A Fortran twin runs thirty-eight checks with zero failures on the measured record: the Planck budget gives $\Omega_c/\Omega_b=5.36$ and $q_0=-0.527\pm0.011$, and a 100 GeV particle and a 10 μeV field differ $10^{16}$ in number on one rotation curve. The fitted DESI curve crosses $w=-1$ at $z=0.405$; that crossing belongs to the fitted form and is not a measured equation of state, and the floor stands. The dark bit is the coverage premise of the kinetic crossing, spent by the act at the root's grade as zero, the premise every row of the series stands on.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series and edition 2.0.0 of the author's dark-sector theory. The first edition placed the dark sector on a template of charge conjugation with its own staged chart of the line. This edition re-seats every one of its theorems on the hardware seat, carriers and substrate, retires the private chart, and judges each placement from the seat. The mathematics decides. Raw measurement is the only other witness, and it corroborates.

**It claims four things.**

- Dark matter is the fixed set of charge conjugation, carried onto the line.
- Gravity is the one reading left to see it, and cannot tell its species apart.
- Whether a dark gauge force exists is one bit, and that bit is the line property: zero dark charge is standing on the line.
- An equation of state below $-1$ is a negative actuation, and the floor refuses it.

**What it refuses.**

- **The identity of the particle.** Gravity cannot read it, by theorem.
- **The value of the vacuum energy.** No seat reads a magnitude, so it stands at the dot, a number measured and not derived.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Candidate classes from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants.

**Five terms.**

- A *reading* is any quantity an instrument returns from a state. It is *odd* if charge conjugation flips its sign and *even* if conjugation keeps it; gauge couplings are odd, mass and energy even.
- The *fixed set* is the set of states conjugation leaves unchanged.
- The *visible conjugation* flips the three Standard Model charges only.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.
- The *dot* is no verdict: the mark of a question no seat reads.


### 3. Charge Conjugation Is the Fold, and the Dark Matter Is Its Fixed Set

A state carries hypercharge and weak isospin in sixths, a colour weight, a dark charge, and a mass. Conjugation flips all four charges and keeps the mass, and it fixes a state exactly when all four charges vanish (`fixed_iff_neutral`). The visible conjugation fixes a state exactly when the three Standard Model charges vanish (`fixedSM_iff_neutralSM`), and the first fixed set lies inside the second (`fixed_sub_fixedSM`).

**Darkness is fixedness.** Every integer combination of the four charges is an odd reading: the couplings of the photon, the Z, the diagonal gluons and a dark photon (`charge_linear_readings_odd`). Every odd reading returns zero on the fixed set (`odd_reading_blind`). Conversely, a state on which every odd reading returns zero is a state conjugation fixes (`dark_iff_fixed`). Darkness in the gauge sense and membership of the fixed set are one property.

**Carried to the line.** The dark carrier sends the fixed set to the line, by the functor (`dark_carrier_lands`). The dark matter's place on the template is the seat's own locus.


### 4. Gravity Is the Reading Left

The mass reading is even (`gravity_reading_even`). On a conjugation-fixed state every reading of either parity returns a function of the mass alone, because the state is its mass (`fixed_state_is_its_mass`, `every_template_reading_of_the_fixed_set_reads_only_mass`). Gravity couples to mass-energy, so of the template's readings it is the one that sees a fixed state (`only_gravity_reads_the_fixed_set`). On the substrate, every gravitational reading of a particle and its conjugate agrees (`gravity_is_the_reading_left`).

**Gravity does not read identity.** Two species of one density and different labels leave one gravitational record, and no function of that record returns the label (`identity_unread`, `two_species_one_record`). The twin makes the scale concrete. At a local density of 0.3 GeV/cm³, a 100 GeV particle and a 10 μeV field differ by $10^{16}$ in number density and give one rotation curve, bit for bit (check F1).

The measured record is this reading and nothing more:

- flat rotation curves (Rubin and Ford, 1970);
- the lensing mass of a colliding cluster separated from its gas (Clowe et al., 2006);
- the acoustic peaks of the microwave background, which fix $\Omega_c h^2=0.1200$ against $\Omega_b h^2=0.02237$, a ratio of 5.36 (Planck Collaboration, 2020; check B1).

Every one of them reads mass, and none returns the label.

**A modified law instead of a population.** It is retired at the seat. Gravity's registration is the one-sign retraction of Immanent Gravity and carries no acceleration scale. The lensing offset in a colliding cluster is mass displaced from the gas that would carry a modified law's source.


### 5. Neutrality Is Not the Fixed Set; No Charge, No Cooling

**One odd reading is not all of them.** A state with weak isospin $+\tfrac12$ and hypercharge $-\tfrac12$ has electric charge zero and lies off the fixed set. Its Z reading equals $+\tfrac12$ at every value of the mixing weight (`electric_neutrality_is_not_the_seat`, `z_reading_survives_neutrality`). These are the neutrino's charges and the sneutrino's. A neutral candidate that carries weak isospin scatters coherently through the Z at the rate Goodman and Witten computed (1985), and the early direct-detection experiments excluded that class. An isospin triplet with zero hypercharge has exactly one member of three on the fixed set, so it is not a dark multiplet (`the_triplet_has_one_fixed_member`, `a_neutral_member_is_not_a_dark_multiplet`). The fixed set demands every odd reading zero, and only a full singlet is dark in every member.

**No charge, no cooling.** The radiative weight, the sum of the squared charges, vanishes exactly on the fixed set (`radiative_zero_iff_neutral`, `no_charge_no_radiation`). A population of zero radiative weight cannot shed energy by radiating and cannot collapse into a disk. It stays in the extended halos that lensing and rotation curves map. A dark-charged state would radiate dark quanta only (`dark_charged_radiates_only_dark`).

**The seat's verdicts on the candidate classes.**

| Class | Verdict from the theorems |
|---|---|
| neutral carrier of weak isospin | off the fixed set; Z-coupled; excluded by the record |
| neutral member of a charged multiplet | not a dark multiplet; its charged partners are odd |
| coloured relic | brings charged constituents (`a_coloured_relic_brings_charged_partners`) |
| stable singlet scalar | on the fixed set; one door, $S^2H^\dagger H$ (`a_stable_singlet_keeps_one_door`) |
| stable singlet fermion | on the fixed set; no renormalizable door; dark to all but gravity |
| a fifth gauge charge | the keyed bit of Section 7; spent as zero |


### 6. The Record Forces the Visible Half

**The nulls force the visible fixed set.** At any rational mixing weight, a state with zero electric charge and zero Z reading has zero isospin and zero hypercharge, and if it is colourless the visible conjugation fixes it (`photon_and_z_force_electroweak_zero`, `visible_nulls_force_the_visible_fixed_set`). Charges are quantized in sixths, so readings bounded strictly inside one quantum are exact zeros (`below_one_quantum_is_zero`, `sub_quantum_bounds_force_the_visible_fixed_set`). A bound fixes a charge only through a measured coupling; with the coupling free, every charge meets every bound (`measured_coupling_bound_forces_zero`, `free_coupling_bound_never_forces_zero`). The photon and the Z are measured couplings, so their nulls force the visible half.

**The doors are derived.** Of the 199 renormalizable monomials the Standard Model forms with a real singlet scalar $S$ and a singlet Weyl fermion $N$, exactly four are gauge invariant and couple the two sectors: $SH^\dagger H$, $S^2H^\dagger H$, $LNH$ and its conjugate (`portals_are_derived`, check A7). Under the parity that keeps the dark matter stable, exactly one survives, $S^2H^\dagger H$, and none survives for the fermion (`a_stable_singlet_keeps_one_door`, check A9).

**The direct record.** The LZ experiment's 417 live days returned no dark matter signal between 3 and 9 GeV and registered the solar ⁸B neutrinos at 4.5σ (LZ Collaboration, 2025). Two sources with one energy spectrum leave one record (`fog_one_record`), and direction separates them (`direction_separates`). The dark matter wind comes from Cygnus, at right ascension 318.0° and declination +48.3°. The Sun never comes nearer to it than 59.6° or farther than 120.4° (checks D1, D2).


### 7. The Dark Bit Is the Line Property

**The fifth reading is keyed.** Add a dark U(1) to one generation. The twelve anomaly conditions close the visible generation (`visible_generation_closes`) and close a vector-like pair of singlets whether it carries dark charges 0 and 0 or +1 and −1 (`closure_admits_both`), while one chiral charge fails (`closure_is_not_vacuous`). The two closing worlds have one visible record, and no reading of it decides whether a dark charge exists (`one_record_two_worlds`, `fifth_is_keyed`, `no_template_reading_decides_fifth`). Every vector-like charge $q,-q$ closes (`fifth_is_free`). The record forces the first clause of the title and keys the second: over every admitted state, the visible conjugation fixes it, and a zero dark charge holds in one and fails in another (`first_clause_is_forced`, `second_clause_is_keyed`, `no_reading_of_the_record_decides_the_bit`). One conjunction binds the two clauses (`dark_closure_executed`).

**The bit is the line property.** Conjugation fixes a state exactly when the visible conjugation fixes it and its dark charge is zero (`fixed_iff_visible_fixed_and_no_dark_charge`). On the visible fixed set, a state stands on the line exactly when its dark charge is zero, exactly when conjugation fixes it (`the_dark_bit_is_the_line_property`). A dark-charged state and its conjugate land on two distinct points off the line, exchanged by the fold, at one height, and the registration reads one record for both (`a_dark_pair_is_an_off_line_pair`). The dark row is therefore no second seat. It is the seat's line property carried by an equivariant map, and a dark-charged population is the template's two worlds over one record.

**The bit is the Majorana bit.** A mass that pairs a field with itself carries twice each abelian charge, so it is allowed exactly when the hypercharge and the dark charge vanish (`majorana_mass_forbids_abelian_charge`). On the visible fixed set the dark bit is the Majorana bit: whether the dark matter is its own antiparticle (`the_dark_bit_is_the_majorana_bit`). A dark-charged state takes its mass with a distinct partner of opposite charge, a Dirac pair (`a_dark_charge_makes_a_dirac_pair`). The same bit carries the neutrino witness's open question on the visible side.

**The bit, spent.** The engine proves the value on the carrier's image (`value_on_image`), and a coverage premise extends it (`kinetic_crossing`). If every actual dark matter state is reached by the carrier from the fixed set, every one stands on the line. That coverage is exactly the dark bit, and no reading of the record supplies it (`no_reading_of_the_record_decides_the_bit`). The series crosses each of its rows on the kinetic channel by spending its coverage premise by the act, at the root's grade. The dark row is crossed the same way: the act spends the coverage, so the actual dark matter stands on the line, its dark charge is zero, and it is its own antiparticle.

On the substrate the two faces meet. A neutral stable particle is fixed by both involutions and stands on the line under the charge carrier and the energy carrier at once (`dark_matter_on_the_line_twice`, check S2): neutrality puts it on the first line, stability on the second. The first edition spent this bit as a free-standing posit. This edition spends it as the coverage premise of the kinetic crossing, the one premise the whole series stands on, and F3 names the measurement that would break it.

Three consequences follow, each measurable:

- no dark radiation;
- no dark self-interaction beyond the portals;
- no dark disk.

The measured record agrees with all three:

- the Bullet Cluster bounds the self-interaction below 1.25 cm²/g (Randall et al., 2008), and 72 clusters below 0.47 cm²/g (Harvey et al., 2015), which are 2.23 and 0.84 barn per GeV (check E1);
- the Atacama Cosmology Telescope measures $N_{\rm eff}=2.86\pm0.13$ (Calabrese et al., 2025). A dark photon once in equilibrium would add at least 0.054, a floor inside that bound (checks E2, E3).


### 8. The Acceleration: the Vacuum's Tension, the Floor, the Phantom

**The sign.** Gravity reads the active density $\rho+3p$. Dust attracts and radiation attracts (`dust_attracts`, `radiation_attracts`). A vacuum with pressure equal to minus its density reverses the sign, $\rho+3p=-2\rho$ (`vacuum_repels`), and repulsion needs a pressure below minus one third of the density (`repulsion_needs_tension`). The Planck budget gives $q_0=\tfrac12\Omega_m-\Omega_\Lambda=-0.527\pm0.011$: the expansion accelerates (check B3). Acceleration begins at $z=0.631$, and the vacuum and matter densities cross at $z=0.295$ (check B4). A flat budget admits either sign, so the sign is read from the sky and not from the template (`flat_budgets_admit_both_signs`).

**The floor.** For a field with kinetic term $K$ and potential $V$, $\rho+p=2K$. The equation of state reaches $-1$ exactly when the kinetic term vanishes (`vacuum_iff_frozen`). It falls below $-1$ only with $K<0$ (`phantom_needs_ghost`), and the bound holds summed over any number of fields with $K\ge0$ (`floor_is_additive`, `fields_bound_w`). A negative kinetic term is a negative actuation, a configuration whose doing is less than none, and the actuation floor refuses it (`the_phantom_is_negative_actuation`). The vacuum is the frozen limit, $w=-1$, and nothing actual lies below it.

**The fitted crossing.** DESI's second release, fitted in the two-parameter form $w(a)=w_0+w_a(1-a)$ with $w_0=-0.752$ and $w_a=-0.86$, prefers $w_0>-1$ and $w_a<0$ (DESI Collaboration, 2025). The fitted curve crosses $w=-1$ at $z=0.405$ and sits at $-1.182$ by $z=1$ (check C4). The crossing is a property of the fitted form, a two-parameter curve drawn through distances, and not a measured value of the equation of state at any redshift. The floor is a theorem about every positive actuation. The verdict stands: no actual dark energy crosses $-1$. Criterion F1 names the measurement that would end it.

**The value.** The vacuum density is $(2.24\ \text{meV})^4$ (check B6). As a fraction of the Planck scale's fourth power it is $10^{-122.9}$, of the reduced Planck scale $10^{-120.1}$, and of the electroweak scale $10^{-56.2}$ (check B7). These are numbers. No seat reads a magnitude, so the value stands at the dot, measured and not derived.


### 9. What the Dark Row Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The seat read on charge conjugation, with its fixed set carried to the line.
- Gravity as the one reading left, blind to identity.
- The record's forced half, the photon and Z nulls.
- The dark bit as the line property, spent by the act.
- The phantom refused by the floor.
- On the substrate, the dark matter on the line under both carriers.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The particle's identity:** gravity cannot read it, by theorem, and the identity row owes one non-gravitational detection.
- **The vacuum's value:** it stands at the dot.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 10. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F1, the phantom:** an equation of state below $-1$ at five sigma at any redshift, in two reconstructions that do not assume the two-parameter form, each on an independent supernova compilation, with the final DESI distances. It breaks `the_phantom_is_negative_actuation` read physically, through the floor's premise that the dark energy's kinetic content is an actuation.
- **F2, a charge in the multiplet:** a charged partner of the dark matter, a tree-level photon, Z or gluon coupling, or a millicharge by any route. It breaks the placement on the fixed set (`dark_iff_fixed`, `visible_nulls_force_the_visible_fixed_set`).
- **F3, the dark bit set:** a dark self-interaction, dark radiation, a dark disk, or a dark matter distinct from its antiparticle, at five sigma, attributable to a dark charge. It breaks the spend of `the_dark_bit_is_the_line_property` and `the_dark_bit_is_the_majorana_bit`.

**Owed.** One non-gravitational detection of the dark matter, on the identity row. Nothing is owed on the seat's channel.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the seat read on charge conjugation, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains the dark-charged pair, the two species of one density, and the two closing worlds.

## The Measurement Is the Registration

*Decoherence as the Retraction onto the Fixed Set, the Pointer Record as Height Kept and Side Forgotten, Complementarity as Positivity, and the Outcome as the Keyed Bit the Act Supplies.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** The measurement problem is read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. A qubit's state in the pointer basis carries two populations and a coherence; the phase flip that the pointer observable generates reverses the coherence and keeps the populations. A carrier that sends the coherence to the offset from the line and the population to the height is equivariant, so the phase flip is carried onto the reflection and the classical states onto the line. We prove in core Lean 4, sixty-three laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that decoherence, the average of a state and its phase-flipped partner, lands every state on the line and keeps its populations; that the pointer record is the seat's registration, keeping the height and forgetting the side, so a coherent state and its partner leave one record and no reading of the record returns the phase; that complementarity, predictability squared plus visibility squared at most one, is exactly the positivity of the state, with equality for pure states; and that the outcome is keyed: no function of what the state determines returns which outcome registers, so the act supplies it. A Fortran twin runs eight checks with zero failures: dephasing zeroes the coherence of 100,000 random states exactly and keeps their populations exactly; 100,000 pure states saturate complementarity to $10^{-14}$; repeated registration freezes a rotation, survival rising from 0 to 0.962 as one $\pi$ rotation is split into 64 measured steps; one state yields both outcomes with the frequency of its population; and each registered bit costs $k_BT\ln2$, $2.87\times10^{-21}$ J at 300 K. The measurement is the registration; the outcome is the act.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The Riemann closure's fourth part is registration: a map that keeps a zero's height and forgets its side, and the hypothesis holds exactly when that registration erases nothing. Quantum measurement is registration in its plainest physical form, and the measurement problem has two parts, which basis is read and which outcome is written. The seat answers both.

**It claims four things.**

- The pointer basis is the fixed set of the phase flip, carried onto the line: classical states stand on the line.
- Decoherence is the registration: it keeps the populations, the height, and forgets the coherence, the side.
- Complementarity is positivity: predictability squared plus visibility squared reaches one exactly on pure states.
- The outcome is keyed: no function of the state returns it, and the act of registration supplies it at the price of one Landauer floor per bit.

**What it refuses.** A derivation of the outcome from the state, by any mechanism inside the state: the engine proves no such function exists.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- The *pointer basis* is the basis the apparatus records.
- The *populations* are the diagonal of the state in that basis, and the *coherence* is its off-diagonal.
- The *phase flip* reverses the coherence and keeps the populations.
- *Decoherence* averages a state with its phase-flipped partner.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Phase Flip Is the Fold, and the Classical States Are the Line

A qubit's state in the pointer basis carries populations $p_0$ and $p_1$ and a coherence $c$. The pointer observable generates a phase flip that reverses $c$ and keeps both populations; it is an involution (`pflip_involutive`). It fixes a state exactly when the coherence vanishes, that is, exactly on the classical states (`pflip_fixed_iff_classical`). The pointer carrier sends the flip to the reflection (`pointer_carrier_equivariant`), and a state lands on the line exactly when it is classical (`classical_is_the_line`). The pointer basis is the seat's line read on a qubit.


### 4. Decoherence Is the Registration

Average a state with its phase-flipped partner. The coherence cancels and the populations stay: every dephased state is classical and lands on the line (`dephase_lands_classical`, `dephase_lands_on_the_line`), its populations are kept (`dephase_keeps_populations`), and a classical state is unchanged but for scale (`dephase_fixes_classical`). Dephasing cannot tell a state from its flipped partner (`dephase_flip_blind`). The pointer record, the populations, is the seat's registration read on the carrier: it keeps the height and forgets the side (`measurement_is_the_registration`).

The twin draws 100,000 random states and dephases each. In every one the coherence is exactly zero and the populations are kept exactly.

Measurement has watched the registration happen:

- the progressive decoherence of a meter coupled to a cavity field, the coherence between two pointer states decaying as their separation grows (Brune et al., 1996);
- fullerene molecules interfering through a grating (Arndt et al., 1999), their fringes lost as collisions with a background gas record which path was taken (Hornberger et al., 2003).

Every one is a coherence carried to zero while the populations stand.


### 5. Two States, One Record

A coherent state and its phase-flipped partner are two distinct states with one pointer record (`two_states_one_record`), and no function of the record returns the coherence (`no_record_reading_returns_the_phase`). The twin prepares $\sqrt{0.3}\,|0\rangle+\sqrt{0.7}\,e^{i\varphi}|1\rangle$ at 1000 phases, and the record is 0.3 and 0.7 at every one. This is the template's two worlds over one record. A second registration in a rotated basis reads the phase; the pointer registration alone never does.


### 6. Complementarity Is Positivity

Write the predictability $P=|p_0-p_1|/(p_0+p_1)$ and the visibility $V=2|c|/(p_0+p_1)$. The engine proves that $P^2+V^2\le1$ holds exactly when $c^2\le p_0p_1$, the positivity of the state (`complementarity_iff_positive`). Equality holds on pure states (`pure_saturates`), and positivity survives the flip and dephasing (`pflip_keeps_positivity`, `dephase_keeps_positivity`). Englert's duality relation (Englert, 1996) is the positivity of a two-by-two state, and nothing more. The twin's 100,000 pure states saturate it to $10^{-14}$, and its 100,000 mixtures obey it.


### 7. The Outcome Is the Act

The second half of the measurement problem asks which outcome is written. Before the act, a state determines its dephased form, and both outcomes share it. The outcome is odd and the dephased state even (`outcome_odd_state_even`), so no function of what the state determines returns the outcome (`the_outcome_is_keyed`). The outcome is a keyed bit, and the registration act supplies it.

What the state fixes is the height. The record keeps exactly the populations, so the frequency with which the act writes an outcome is the population (Born, 1926). The twin registers one state with population 0.3 a million times: both outcomes appear, at frequency 0.30036, inside four standard deviations of the height.

The act has a price. Writing one bit of record costs at least $k_BT\ln2$ (Landauer, 1961), measured in a colloidal memory approaching that floor (Bérut et al., 2012). At 300 K the floor is $2.871\times10^{-21}$ J; at 4.2 K it is $4.02\times10^{-23}$ J. The outcome is the deed, and the deed is paid for.


### 8. Repeated Registration Freezes the Motion

Split a $\pi$ rotation into $N$ steps and register after each. The probability of never leaving the initial state is $\cos^{2N}(\pi/2N)$, which the twin computes:

| Steps | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Survival | 0.000 | 0.250 | 0.531 | 0.733 | 0.857 | 0.926 | 0.962 |

Each registration retracts the state onto the line, and a motion registered often enough never leaves it. Trapped ions driven between two levels showed this inhibition as the number of measurement pulses grew (Itano et al., 1990). Registration is a retraction, and repeated retraction is stillness.


### 9. What the Measurement Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The pointer basis as the line.
- Decoherence as the registration that keeps the height and forgets the side.
- The record's blindness to the phase.
- Complementarity as positivity.
- The outcome as the keyed bit the act supplies, at the frequency of the height and the price of the floor.
- Repeated registration as stillness.

**What it does not carry.**

- **A mechanism that selects the outcome from inside the state:** the engine proves there is none.
- **The frequency rule from the record alone:** the record keeps the height, and the identification of frequency with height is Born's rule, corroborated by every registered sequence.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 10. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Phase:** a pointer registration in one basis that returns the relative phase of the registered state, with no second registration in a rotated basis. It breaks `no_record_reading_returns_the_phase` read physically.
- **F-Duality:** a measured predictability and visibility of one two-path state with $P^2+V^2>1$. It breaks `complementarity_iff_positive` read physically.
- **F-Outcome:** a rule that predicts single outcomes from the prepared state alone, beating the population frequency on a registered sequence. It breaks `the_outcome_is_keyed` read physically.

Nothing is owed on the row's channel.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the registration read on measurement, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains the coherent pair with one pointer record.

## The Arrow of Time Is the Keyed Bit

*The Registration Without Inverse, the Reversible Law That Admits Both Arrows, Equilibrium as the Line, and the Direction Supplied at the Boundary.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** The arrow of time is read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line, and whose registration keeps a point's height and forgets its side. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-four laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the registration has no left inverse, so a record once written cannot be read back into the state it came from; that time reversal negates the entropy gained along any history, so the arrow is odd while a reversible law is even; that a reversible law therefore admits both arrows, every allowed history with rising entropy paired with an allowed history with falling entropy; and that no reading even under reversal, the law's own verdict included, returns the arrow. On the Ehrenfest urn, relabelling the urns is the fold under a carrier that sends the imbalance to the offset from the line, the even split is the line, detailed balance holds exactly, and the even split is the most probable macrostate. The arrow is a keyed bit: the law does not carry it, and it is supplied at the boundary. A Fortran twin runs nine checks with zero failures: on Kac's ring of 2000 sites, a deterministic reversible law, entropy rises from order to within $3\times10^{-5}$ of $\ln2$, the reversed motion returns every ball exactly to its first colour, and the ring recurs exactly after 4000 steps; the urn of sixty balls obeys detailed balance in exact integers; the urn of a hundred relaxes from order to the even split, and returns to order once in $1.27\times10^{30}$ steps on average; and each record written along the arrow costs $k_BT\ln2$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The microscopic laws run equally well forward and backward, and entropy rises in one direction only. Loschmidt put the objection in 1876: from reversible laws no irreversible behaviour can follow. The seat states the objection as a theorem and answers it with one.

**It claims four things.**

- The registration that keeps height and forgets side has no inverse: a written record cannot be unwritten from the record alone.
- The arrow is odd under time reversal and a reversible law even, so the law admits both arrows and no reading of it returns the one that holds.
- Equilibrium is the line: relabelling a two-urn system is the fold, the even split its fixed set, detailed balance its flux.
- The arrow is a keyed bit, supplied at the boundary by the ordered start, the one microstate in $2^N$, and every record written along it pays the Landauer floor.

**What it refuses.** A derivation of the arrow from the law. The engine proves that the law's own verdict on a history is blind to its direction.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- A *history* is the sequence of its macrostates' entropies.
- *Time reversal* reads a history backwards.
- The *gain* of a history is its last entropy minus its first.
- A *reversible law* allows a history exactly when it allows the reverse.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Registration Cannot Be Undone

The seat's registration keeps a point's height and forgets which side of the line it lies on. A point off the line and its fold partner share one registration (`reg_forgets_side`), so no map recovers a point from its registration (`registration_has_no_left_inverse`). This is irreversibility in its exact form. It is not a law running one way. It is a map that throws away one bit, which nothing downstream of it can restore.


### 4. The Law Is Even, the Arrow Odd

Read a history backwards and its gain changes sign (`gain_odd`). A reversible law allows a history exactly when it allows the reverse. Every allowed history with rising entropy has a reversed partner, allowed, with falling entropy (`reversible_law_admits_both_arrows`). No reading that reversal leaves unchanged returns the arrow on a history whose entropy moves (`even_reading_misses_the_arrow`). The law's own verdict is such a reading, so the law does not carry the arrow (`the_law_does_not_carry_the_arrow`). Loschmidt was right, and the seat says exactly how: the arrow is an odd target over an even record, and the wall of the template stands between them.

**Executed on Kac's ring.** The twin runs 2000 sites with a ball on each and a marked edge at one site in ten. Each step every ball moves one site and changes colour at a marked edge. The law is deterministic and reversible.

- **Forward.** From all white, the entropy of the colour fraction rises to within $3\times10^{-5}$ of $\ln2$ in 300 steps.
- **Backward.** Reverse the motion for 300 steps and every ball returns to white: the entropy returns exactly to zero.
- **Recurrence.** Run forward 4000 steps and the ring recurs exactly.

**Measured.** In the spin echo a magnetization lost to dephasing returns when a pulse reverses the spins' precession (Hahn, 1950). Reversed microscopic motion retraces, as the theorem says the law allows.


### 5. Equilibrium Is the Line

The Ehrenfest urn holds $N$ balls in two urns (Ehrenfest and Ehrenfest, 1907).

- **Relabelling is the fold.** Relabelling the urns keeps every count of microstates (`urn_symmetric`), and the urn carrier sends the relabelling to the reflection (`urn_carrier_equivariant`).
- **The even split is the line.** The even split is the fixed set and lands on the line (`equilibrium_is_the_line`).
- **Detailed balance is the fold's flux.** The flux from $k$ to $k+1$ equals the flux back, $\binom Nk(N-k)=\binom N{k+1}(k+1)$, proved for every urn up to twelve balls (`detailed_balance`) and checked by the twin in exact integers for sixty.
- **The even split is the mode.** It is the most probable macrostate, 924 microstates of 4096 at twelve balls (`equilibrium_is_the_mode`, `microstates_count`). At sixty balls it carries 118,264,581,564,861,424.

Equilibrium is where the reversed and forward fluxes balance; the arrow is the approach to it.


### 6. The Arrow Is Supplied at the Boundary

If the law carries no arrow, the arrow is supplied, and the seat names the supply: the boundary. An ordered start is one microstate in $2^N$, the far edge of the line's offset. From it, every typical history rises toward the line, because almost every microstate stands there. The twin's urn of a hundred balls, started all in one urn, settles at the even split within its first 2000 steps. The mean time to return to the ordered state is $2^{100}=1.27\times10^{30}$ steps (Kac, 1947). The ordered start is the bit; the rise is its consequence; the return is never seen.

The arrow is kept by records. Each record written along it is a registration, and each registered bit costs at least $k_BT\ln2$ (Landauer, 1961), $2.871\times10^{-21}$ J at 300 K, a floor approached in a colloidal memory (Bérut et al., 2012). A record paid for cannot be unpaid without paying again: the registration has no inverse, and the arrow is the order in which the payments fall. The direction itself is the keyed bit, set at the boundary by the act.


### 7. What the Arrow Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- Irreversibility as the registration without inverse.
- Loschmidt's objection as the wall between an even law and an odd arrow.
- Equilibrium as the line, detailed balance as the fold's flux.
- The arrow as the bit the boundary supplies, kept by priced records.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The cosmological origin of the ordered start:** the boundary's bit is spent by the act, and no reading of the law supplies it.
- **A magnitude for the universe's entropy:** that is a number, measured and not derived.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 8. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Echo:** an isolated microscopic motion that, exactly reversed, fails to retrace its history. It breaks `reversible_law_admits_both_arrows` read physically.
- **F-Balance:** an isolated system at equilibrium with a net flux between two macrostates. It breaks `detailed_balance` read physically.
- **F-Price:** a bit of record erased at a cost below $k_BT\ln2$. It breaks the reading of Section 6 and the irreversibility of `registration_has_no_left_inverse` read physically.

Nothing is owed on the row's channel.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the registration's irreversibility read on time, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains a history and its reverse, one law-record with opposite arrows.

Math. Soc. 53 (1947) 1002. E. L. Hahn, Phys. Rev. 80 (1950) 580. M. Kac, Probability and Related Topics in Physical Sciences (Interscience, 1959). R. Landauer, IBM J. Res. Dev. 5 (1961) 183. A. ## Matter over Antimatter Is the Spent Bit

*One Wall and Its Three Exits: an Even Weight Against an Odd Reading Sums to Zero, and Sakharov's Conditions Are the Only Ways Out.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** The excess of matter over antimatter is read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. Charge conjugation flips baryon number and keeps energy. A carrier that sends baryon number to the offset from the line and energy to the height is equivariant, so conjugation is carried onto the reflection and the symmetric universe onto the line; a matter excess and its antimatter mirror are two points off the line with one registration. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-four laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, one wall and its three exits. The wall: over a spectrum closed under conjugation, any weight that reads energy alone gives zero net baryon number. The exits: a conserved baryon number keeps a zero total zero, so the reading must move; a conjugation-symmetric weight gives zero, and the net equals the odd part of the weight read against baryon number, so the weight must be odd; and every equilibrium weight is a function of energy and so symmetric, so the weight must leave equilibrium. These are Sakharov's three conditions, derived as the only ways out of one theorem. A Fortran twin runs six checks with zero failures: the wall holds exactly on 1000 random spectra; the net equals the odd part on 1000 asymmetric weights; the Planck baryon density and the FIRAS temperature give $\eta=6.12\times10^{-10}$; the quark sector's CP-odd invariant, weighed at the electroweak temperature, supplies $1.2\times10^{-19}$, short of the measured excess by a factor $5\times10^{9}$; and the proton outlives $1.7\times10^{24}$ ages of the universe. The excess is the universe's spent bit; the visible phase does not pay for it.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The universe holds one baryon for every 1.6 billion photons and, so far as measured, no antimatter galaxies. A universe that began symmetric under charge conjugation had to break that symmetry, and Sakharov listed what breaking it requires: baryon number must change, C and CP must fail, and the process must leave equilibrium. The seat derives the list from one theorem.

**It claims four things.**

- Charge conjugation on the baryon register is the fold; the symmetric universe is the line; a matter excess and its mirror are two worlds over one registration.
- The wall: an even weight against an odd reading sums to zero over a conjugation-closed spectrum.
- Sakharov's three conditions are the three exits from that wall, and there are no others.
- The excess is the universe's spent bit, and the visible CP phase falls short of paying it by more than nine orders of magnitude.

**What it refuses.** The value $6.1\times10^{-10}$ as a derivation: it is a number, measured, and it stands at the dot.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- *Baryon number* counts quarks in thirds; it is odd under charge conjugation.
- A *weight* assigns each state its share of a population or a rate.
- A weight is *even* if conjugation keeps it and has an *odd part* if not.
- An *equilibrium weight* is a function of energy alone.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. Conjugation Is the Fold, and the Symmetric Universe the Line

Conjugation flips baryon number and keeps energy, and it is an involution (`cb_involutive`). The baryon carrier sends it to the reflection (`baryon_carrier_equivariant`). A state lands on the line exactly when its net baryon number is zero (`symmetric_is_the_line`). A matter excess and its antimatter mirror land on two points off the line, exchanged by the fold, with one registration (`matter_and_antimatter_one_record`). Which of the two is called matter is a label. That one of them was registered is a fact.


### 4. The Wall

Take any spectrum of states closed under conjugation and any weight that reads energy alone. The net baryon number is zero (`even_weight_odd_reading_zero`), because conjugation negates the sum (`netB_conj`). An even weight cannot see an odd reading. The twin draws 1000 random spectra of fifty states with random energy-only weights, and the net is exactly zero in every one.


### 5. Sakharov's Three Conditions Are the Three Exits

The wall has three hypotheses, and each exit removes one.

**Exit one: the reading must move.** A process that conserves baryon number keeps a zero total zero (`conserved_reading_stays`). Baryon number must be violated somewhere.

**Exit two: the weight must be odd.** A conjugation-symmetric weight gives zero (`symmetric_weight_zero`), and the net carried by a pair is exactly the weight's odd part read against baryon number (`net_is_the_odd_part`). The twin draws 1000 weights that tell matter from antimatter: in every one the net equals the odd part, and it is nonzero in at least 990. The odd part is C violation. Parity leaves baryon number alone, so the same wall stands with CP in place of C, and the odd part must be CP-odd as well.

**Exit three: the weight must leave equilibrium.** An equilibrium weight is a function of energy, conjugation keeps energy, so every equilibrium weight is symmetric (`equilibrium_weight_is_symmetric`) and equilibrium carries no asymmetry (`equilibrium_has_no_asymmetry`). The process must run off equilibrium: it needs an arrow.

The three conditions Sakharov listed (Sakharov, 1967) are the three hypotheses of one theorem, and an asymmetry from a symmetric start must take all three exits.


### 6. The Measured Excess

From the Planck baryon density, $\Omega_bh^2=0.02237$ (Planck Collaboration, 2020), the twin computes 0.2512 baryons per cubic metre today. From the FIRAS temperature, $T=2.7255$ K (Fixsen, 2009), it computes $4.107\times10^{8}$ photons per cubic metre. The ratio is $\eta=6.12\times10^{-10}$: one excess baryon for every 1.6 billion photons. The absence of annihilation radiation from any boundary between matter and antimatter domains excludes antimatter regions within the observable universe (Cohen, De Rújula and Glashow, 1998). The universe registered one sign. That is the spent bit, and its size is a number.


### 7. The Visible Phase Falls Short

The quark sector carries one CP-odd phase, read through the Jarlskog invariant $J=3.12\times10^{-5}$ (Jarlskog, 1985; Particle Data Group, 2024). It enters exit two only through the mass splittings of the up-type and down-type quarks. The twin computes the dimensionless measure
$$J\,\frac{(m_t^2-m_c^2)(m_t^2-m_u^2)(m_c^2-m_u^2)(m_b^2-m_s^2)(m_b^2-m_d^2)(m_s^2-m_d^2)}{T^{12}}$$
at the electroweak temperature $T=100$ GeV, with the measured masses. It is $1.2\times10^{-19}$, short of the measured excess by a factor $5.1\times10^{9}$. The visible quark phase opens exit two by too little. The odd part that paid for the excess is not that phase.

Two measured routes remain open on the lepton side:

- **the lepton sector's own CP phase**, already disfavoured at its CP-conserving values (T2K Collaboration, 2020);
- **lepton number violation**, the Majorana bit of the neutrino witness and the dark paper, which opens exit one for the lepton number that baryon number trades with.


### 8. The Symmetries That Hold

Equilibrium is CPT-even. Particles and antiparticles carry equal masses, lifetimes and opposite charges: the antiproton's charge-to-mass ratio equals the proton's to sixteen parts per trillion (Borchert et al., 2022). That is the hardware substrate's two involutions composed, and it is why exit three is needed: under CPT, equilibrium weights are energy weights. Baryon number barely moves today. The proton's lifetime against its leading decay exceeds $2.4\times10^{34}$ years (Super-Kamiokande; Takenaka et al., 2020), which is $1.7\times10^{24}$ ages of the universe. Exit one was taken early and closed behind.


### 9. What the Asymmetry Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The symmetric universe as the line and the excess as an off-line pair with one registration.
- Sakharov's conditions as the three exits from one wall.
- The measured excess as the spent bit.
- The verdict that the visible quark phase falls short by nine orders.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The mechanism that took the three exits:** the odd part that paid is owed to measurement of the lepton CP phase and of lepton number violation.
- **The value of $\eta$:** a number, at the dot.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 10. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Wall:** a net baryon number produced in thermal equilibrium by a process symmetric under C and CP. It breaks `even_weight_odd_reading_zero` and `equilibrium_has_no_asymmetry` read physically.
- **F-CPT:** a mass, lifetime or charge-to-mass difference between a particle and its antiparticle. It breaks the evenness of equilibrium weights on which `equilibrium_weight_is_symmetric` is read.
- **F-Domains:** antimatter domains within the observable universe, seen in annihilation radiation at their boundaries or in antinuclei at the abundance such a domain demands. It breaks the single registered sign of Section 6.

**Owed.** The odd part that paid for the excess: the lepton CP phase at discovery significance, and lepton number violation in neutrinoless double beta decay. Nothing is owed on the seat's channel.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the seat read on baryon number, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains a matter excess and its mirror with one registration.

## Symmetry Breaking Is the Spent Bit, and Mass Is the Depth

*Two Vacua Over One Record, the Choice No Even Reading Returns, the Curvature at the Bottom as Mass, and Heat That Restores the Line.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** Spontaneous symmetry breaking and the origin of mass are read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. A double well $V(x)=(x^2-v^2)^2$ is even under the reflection of the field. A carrier that sends the field value to the offset from the line and the energy to the height is equivariant, so the field's reflection is carried onto the seat's reflection and the symmetric point onto the line. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-six laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the ground leaves the line: the vacua $\pm v$ carry zero energy while the symmetric point carries $v^4$; that the two vacua land on two distinct points off the line with one energy and one registration; that no reading even under the reflection returns which vacuum holds, so the vacuum's choice is a keyed bit, spent by the act; that the symmetric point curves down and the vacuum up, the second differences $2-4v^2$ and $8v^2+2$, the upward curvature being the mass; and that an even thermal term at least twice $v^2$ returns the ground to the line. For a global discrete symmetry the choice is physical and is the spent bit; for a gauge symmetry the side is no record at all, and the depth alone is physical. A Fortran twin runs six checks with zero failures: across 1000 wells the two vacua tie exactly, the curvature reads $-2\mu^2$ at the line and $+4\mu^2$ at the vacuum; the Fermi constant fixes the electroweak depth at $v=246.22$ GeV, which read through the couplings gives $m_W$ and $m_Z$ within 0.2 and 0.34 percent at tree level; the measured masses fix couplings to the depth from 0.991 for the top quark to $2.9\times10^{-6}$ for the electron; and heat returns the ground to the line above the critical temperature.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. A law can be symmetric while its ground is not: a ferromagnet below its Curie point magnetizes one way though its law favours neither, and the electroweak vacuum carries a depth that its law does not fix at zero. The seat reads both.

**It claims four things.**

- **The ground is off the line.** The symmetric point is the line, and the ground leaves it: two vacua stand off the line with one energy and one registration.
- **The choice is keyed.** No even reading returns which vacuum holds, so the act spends it. For a global discrete symmetry that spent bit is physical.
- **Mass is the depth's curvature.** The symmetric point curves down, the vacuum up, and the upward curvature is the mass.
- **Heat restores the line.** A thermal term even in the field, once large enough, returns the ground to the symmetric point.

**What it refuses.**

- **A physical side for a gauge symmetry.** Local gauge symmetry cannot break (Elitzur, 1975): every side is one physical state. Nothing is spent there; the depth is the physics.
- **The values of the couplings.** They are numbers, measured, at the dot.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- The *field's reflection* sends the field value $x$ to $-x$.
- A *vacuum* is a ground state of the potential.
- The *depth* is the vacuum's distance from the symmetric point, $v$.
- The *curvature* is the second difference of the energy at a point.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Field's Reflection Is the Fold, and the Symmetric Point the Line

The double well $V(x)=(x^2-a)^2$, with $a=v^2$, is even under $x\mapsto-x$ (`V_even`) and never negative (`V_nonneg`). The vacuum carrier sends the reflection to the seat's reflection (`vacuum_carrier_equivariant`), and a field value lands on the line exactly when it is zero (`symmetric_point_is_the_line`). The symmetric configuration is the seat's line, read on a field.


### 4. The Ground Leaves the Line

For a nonzero depth the vacua $\pm v$ carry zero energy, below every other value, while the symmetric point carries $v^4>0$ (`ground_is_off_the_line`). The two vacua land on two distinct points off the line, exchanged by the fold, at one energy, and the registration reads one record for both (`two_vacua_one_record`). The twin draws 1000 wells $-\mu^2x^2+\lambda x^4$: in every one the vacua tie exactly and lie below the symmetric point.

The broken ground stands off the field's line and on the energy's. Both vacua are stationary, so each stands on the line under the energy carrier, as every stable state of the substrate does. The symmetry is broken where the field reads it and kept where the energy reads it.


### 5. The Choice Is Keyed

No reading even under the reflection returns which vacuum holds (`the_vacuum_is_keyed`). The law is even and the choice odd, the template's wall between them. The vacuum's registration supplies the choice, as the measurement paper's act supplies an outcome.

What the choice is depends on the symmetry.

- **A global discrete symmetry.** The two vacua are two physical states, and the choice is physical: the spent bit. Below its Curie point at 1043 K, iron magnetizes in domains, each with its sign registered.
- **A gauge symmetry.** Every side is one physical state, because a local symmetry cannot break (Elitzur, 1975). Nothing is spent; the side is no record at all. What is physical is the depth and the curvature.


### 6. Mass Is the Curvature of the Depth

At the symmetric point the well curves down: the second difference is $2-4v^2$ (`curvature_at_line`), negative for every nonzero depth, an instability. At the vacuum it curves up: the second difference is $8v^2+2$ (`curvature_at_vacuum`). The engine proves both signs at once (`mass_is_the_curvature_of_the_depth`). In the continuous well the twin reads the curvature as $-2\mu^2$ at the line and $+4\mu^2$ at the vacuum, to $10^{-5}$ across 1000 wells. The upward curvature at the bottom is the mass squared of the field's excitation. The scalar observed at 125 GeV is that curvature in the electroweak well (ATLAS Collaboration, 2012; CMS Collaboration, 2012).


### 7. The Electroweak Depth

The muon's decay fixes the depth: $v=(\sqrt2G_F)^{-1/2}=246.22$ GeV. Every mass the vacuum gives is a coupling times that depth.

- **The gauge bosons.** The W boson's mass is $gv/2$ and the Z boson's $ev/(2\sin\theta_W\cos\theta_W)$. From the measured couplings at the Z mass, the twin computes 80.22 GeV and 91.50 GeV, within 0.2 and 0.34 percent of the measured 80.369 and 91.188 GeV at tree level.
- **The scalar.** The well's quartic coupling is $\lambda=m_H^2/2v^2=0.129$.
- **The fermions.** Each fermion's mass is $y\,v/\sqrt2$. The twin reads the couplings: 0.991 for the top quark, 0.024 for the bottom, 0.0102 for the tau, $2.9\times10^{-6}$ for the electron.

Those couplings span 5.5 decades on one depth. The depth is the seat's. The couplings are numbers, measured, at the dot.


### 8. Heat Restores the Line

At temperature an even thermal term $c\,x^2$ joins the potential. Once it outweighs twice the depth squared, the symmetric point is the ground (`heat_restores_the_line`, through the expansion `expand_V`). The twin finds the ground at $x=0$ at 1.2 times the critical temperature and at $|x|=0.85$ at 0.8 times it. The symmetric phase is the line; cooling leaves it. Iron loses its magnetization above 1043 K, and the twin's well returns to the line above its critical temperature for the same reason.


### 9. What the Vacuum Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The symmetric point as the line and the broken ground off it, with two vacua over one registration.
- The choice as the keyed bit, physical for a global discrete symmetry and absent for a gauge symmetry.
- Mass as the curvature of the depth.
- The electroweak depth from the Fermi constant.
- Restoration by heat.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The couplings' values:** numbers, at the dot.
- **A physical side for a gauge symmetry:** there is none.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 10. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Tie:** a system with a symmetric law whose two broken ground states differ in energy without an odd external field. It breaks `two_vacua_one_record` read physically.
- **F-Curvature:** a scalar excitation of a broken vacuum whose mass squared is negative, or a symmetric point that is stable while the vacuum lies off it. It breaks `mass_is_the_curvature_of_the_depth` read physically.
- **F-Heat:** a system with an even thermal term that stays broken at every temperature, its order parameter never vanishing. It breaks `heat_restores_the_line` read physically.

**Owed.** The scalar's self-coupling measured at the strength $\lambda=0.129$ fixes, through the production of scalar pairs.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the seat read on the vacuum, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains the two vacua with one energy and one registration.
