## The Correlation Lives in the Joint Registration

*Entanglement on the Seat: No Signal as the Wall, the Correlation in Neither Local Record, and Bell's Bound as a Count of Local Strategies.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** Entanglement and nonlocality are read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. Two parties register outcomes, and their joint record counts the four outcome pairs. When one party relabels her outcome, her local bias and the correlation reverse while the other party's record does not move. A carrier that sends her bias to the offset from the line is equivariant, so her relabelling is carried onto the seat's reflection and the unbiased local record onto the line. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-five laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the far record is blind to every local relabelling, so no function of it returns a local choice: no signal passes, by the template's wall; that two joint records with the same unbiased local records on both sides carry opposite correlations, so the correlation is in neither local record and lives only in the joint registration; that every deterministic local strategy gives a CHSH value of exactly $\pm2$, all sixteen counted, and every mixture of them obeys Bell's bound $|S|\le2$; and that a box with unbiased local records reaches 4 while signalling nothing. A Fortran twin runs six checks with zero failures: 100,000 local mixtures stay within 2; the singlet reaches $2\sqrt2=2.828427$ at the optimal settings and no random setting exceeds it; its local records are unbiased to $10^{-16}$ whatever the far setting; and four experiments, from 1982 to 2023, measured CHSH values above 2 by 2.1 to 46.5 standard deviations and below $2\sqrt2$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. Two distant parties register outcomes whose correlation no local account reproduces, and neither can use it to send the other a message. The seat reads both facts as one structure: a local relabelling is a fold the far record cannot see.

**It claims four things.**

- A local relabelling is the fold on the local record, and the unbiased local record is the line.
- No signal passes. The far record is even under every local relabelling and a local choice is odd, so the template's wall stands between them.
- The correlation is in neither local record. Two joint records with the same local records carry opposite correlations, so the correlation lives only in the joint registration.
- Bell's bound is a count of local strategies: each of the sixteen gives exactly $\pm2$, so every mixture stays within 2.

**What it refuses.** A local record that carries the correlation. The engine proves there is none.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- A *joint record* counts the four outcome pairs of two parties.
- A *local record* is one party's own outcome counts.
- The *correlation* is the agreements minus the disagreements.
- A *local strategy* fixes each party's outcome for each of its settings.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. A Local Relabelling Is the Fold

When Alice relabels her outcome, the joint record's rows exchange, which is an involution (`flipA_involutive`). Her bias reverses (`aliceBias_odd`) and the correlation reverses (`corr_odd`). The local carrier sends her relabelling to the seat's reflection (`local_carrier_equivariant`). Her local record lands on the line exactly when it is unbiased (`unbiased_is_the_line`). A maximally entangled pair puts every local record on the line: the twin finds the singlet's local records unbiased to $10^{-16}$ at 100,000 settings.


### 4. No Signal Is the Wall

Bob's record, his own outcome counts, does not move when Alice relabels (`bob_sees_no_flip`). Whether Alice relabels is odd and Bob's record even, so no function of Bob's record returns her choice (`no_signal_from_a_local_choice`). This is no-signalling in exact form. Whatever Alice does on her side is a fold, and the far record is blind to every fold, as an even reading is blind to an odd target. The twin confirms it on the singlet: Bob's marginal is one half at every setting of Alice's.


### 5. The Correlation Lives in the Joint Registration

Two joint records, one perfectly correlated and one perfectly anticorrelated, have the same unbiased record for Alice and the same for Bob, and opposite correlations. No function of the two local records returns the correlation (`correlation_is_not_in_the_local_records`). The correlation is a property of the joint registration alone, two worlds over one pair of local records. That is what entanglement is on the seat. Each local outcome is a keyed bit supplied by its own act, as the measurement paper proves. The correlation is the relation between the two acts, and it appears only when the two records are registered together.


### 6. Bell's Bound Is a Count of Local Strategies

A local strategy fixes each party's outcome for each of two settings. The CHSH combination of its four correlations is $\pm2$ for every one of the sixteen strategies (`chsh_values`, `local_strategies_give_two`). Every mixture with nonnegative weights stays within twice its total weight (`bell_bound`). Bell's inequality (Bell, 1964; Clauser, Horne, Shimony and Holt, 1969) is this count and nothing more: an account that gives each party a local record of answers in advance cannot exceed 2. The twin draws 100,000 random mixtures, and every one stays within 2.

No-signalling alone allows more. A box whose outcomes agree unless both settings are 1 has unbiased local records for every setting and signals nothing (`pr_box_signals_nothing`), and it reaches 4 (`pr_box_reaches_four`; Popescu and Rohrlich, 1994).


### 7. The Quantum Value and the Measured Values

The singlet's correlation at settings $a$ and $b$ is $-\cos(a-b)$. The twin reaches $2\sqrt2=2.828427$ at the optimal settings, and no one of 100,000 random settings exceeds it, the bound Tsirelson proved (Tsirelson, 1980). The measured values:

| Experiment | CHSH value | Above 2 |
|---|---|---|
| Aspect, Grangier and Roger, 1982 | $2.697\pm0.015$ | 46.5σ |
| Weihs et al., 1998 | $2.73\pm0.02$ | 36.5σ |
| Hensen et al., 2015, loophole-free | $2.42\pm0.20$ | 2.1σ |
| Storz et al., 2023, loophole-free | $2.0747\pm0.0033$ | 22.6σ |

Every one lies above the local count and below $2\sqrt2$.

The world's joint records are not mixtures of local ones. Its local records never signal.


### 8. What the Pair Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The local relabelling as the fold and the unbiased local record as the line.
- No signal as the wall.
- The correlation in the joint registration alone.
- Bell's bound as a count of local strategies, and the measured violations.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The value $2\sqrt2$ from the template:** it is computed from the singlet and imported as Tsirelson's theorem.
- **A local record of the correlation:** there is none.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 9. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Signal:** a local operation on one party that changes the far party's own outcome counts. It breaks `bob_sees_no_flip` and `no_signal_from_a_local_choice` read physically.
- **F-Local:** a CHSH value above 2 reproduced by a model assigning each party its outcomes in advance. It breaks `bell_bound`.
- **F-Tsirelson:** a measured CHSH value above $2\sqrt2$ on quantum systems. It breaks the imported bound and the twin's computation of the singlet.

Nothing is owed on the row's channel.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the registration read on two parties, WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains two joint records with one pair of local records.

## The Strong Force Reads No Orientation

*The Strong CP Angle on the Fixed Set of Its Fold: Two Fixed Points on the Circle, the Dipole Null That Forces Them, and Positivity That Selects Zero.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** The strong CP problem is read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. The strong interaction carries an angle $\bar\theta$ on a circle, odd under CP. We prove in core Lean 4, forty-eight laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the reflection $\theta\mapsto-\theta$ of the circle fixes exactly the multiples of half the circle, the two points 0 and $\pi$; that a carrier sending the sign of the angle's sine to the offset from the line and its distance from zero to the height is equivariant on every circle of up to twenty-four points, landing a point on the line exactly when it is 0 or $\pi$; that the neutron's electric dipole moment, an odd reading of the angle, vanishes exactly on those two points, so its measured null forces the angle onto the fixed set; and that the angle's energy, even, is least at 0 uniquely and greatest at $\pi$, both stationary. Positivity selects 0 of the two fixed points, as it seats the Lee–Yang zeros on their circle. A Fortran twin runs five checks with zero failures: on 250,500 points of circles up to a thousand points the reflection is the fold and the line holds exactly the two fixed points; the vacuum energy of the angle at the measured quark mass ratio is even, least at 0 and greatest at $\pi$, stationary at both; at equal quark masses its two branches meet at $\pi$ in a cusp with opposite slopes, two vacua with one energy; its curvature at 0 gives $\chi^{1/4}=76.19$ MeV; and the measured dipole bound places the angle within $7.5\times10^{-11}$ of 0. The strong force reads no orientation: its angle stands at the fixed point positivity selects.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. The weak interaction reads orientation, and the weak witness traced that reading to one phase. The strong interaction could read it through an angle on a circle and, to one part in ten billion, does not. The seat reads why the angle stands where it does.

**It claims four things.**

- The angle's reflection has exactly two fixed points on the circle, 0 and $\pi$, and they are the line under the angle carrier.
- The neutron's dipole moment is an odd reading of the angle, vanishing exactly on the fixed set, so its measured null forces the angle onto the fixed set.
- Positivity selects 0. The angle's energy is even, least at 0 and greatest at $\pi$, as the one-sign measure of a vector-like theory requires.
- At the antipode, with equal quark masses, the energy's two branches meet in a cusp: two vacua with one energy, the template's barrier at $\pi$.

**What it refuses.** A new field as a requirement. The angle stands on the line as the line property, spent by the act at the root's grade. A dynamical field that relaxes it there would be a carrier onto the same line, not a requirement of the seat.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- The *strong angle* $\bar\theta$ is the CP-odd parameter of the strong interaction, an angle on a circle.
- The *reflection* sends $\theta$ to $-\theta$.
- An *odd reading* reverses under it.
- The *vacuum energy of the angle* is the ground energy as a function of $\theta$.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Angle's Reflection Has Two Fixed Points

On a circle the reflection $\theta\mapsto-\theta$ fixes a point exactly when twice it is a whole turn's multiple. That is exactly when it is a multiple of half the circle (`fixed_iff_multiple_of_half`): the two points 0 and $\pi$. The angle carrier sends the reflection to the seat's reflection, and a point lands on the line exactly when it is one of the two, proved on every circle of up to twenty-four points (`angle_carrier_equivariant_and_line`). The twin carries it to circles of a thousand points, 250,500 points in all. The strong interaction respects CP at exactly two angles, and those two are the line.


### 4. The Dipole Null Forces the Fixed Set

A neutron with a static electric dipole moment violates P and T, so the moment is an odd reading of the angle. It reverses under the reflection and vanishes exactly on the two fixed points (`edm_odd_and_null_on_the_fixed_set`). The moment is measured null: $|d_n|<1.8\times10^{-26}\,e$ cm (Abel et al., 2020). The measured null places the angle on the fixed set, as the photon and Z nulls place the dark matter on the fixed set of charge conjugation.


### 5. Positivity Selects Zero

The angle's energy is even (`energy_even`). Of the two fixed points the energy is least at 0, uniquely, and greatest at $\pi$, and both are stationary (`positivity_selects_zero`). In a vector-like gauge theory the measure is positive at $\theta=0$, so the vacuum energy there is the least (Vafa and Witten, 1984).

The twin evaluates the vacuum energy of the angle at the measured pion mass, decay constant and quark mass ratio $m_u/m_d=0.474$:
$$E(\theta)=-m_\pi^2f_\pi^2\sqrt{1-\tfrac{4z}{(1+z)^2}\sin^2\tfrac\theta2}.$$
It is even, least at 0, greatest at $\pi$, and stationary at both. Its curvature at 0, the topological susceptibility, gives $\chi^{1/4}=76.19$ MeV.

A one-sign measure seats the minimum on the line here, as one-sign couplings seat the Lee–Yang zeros on their circle and one-sign sources keep gravity on its locus. The sign of a nonzero angle is keyed to every even reading (`the_sign_of_theta_is_keyed`). The energy cannot tell $\theta$ from $-\theta$; only an odd reading can.


### 6. The Antipode: Two Vacua, One Energy

At equal quark masses the energy becomes $-m_\pi^2f_\pi^2|\cos\tfrac\theta2|$. Its two branches meet at $\pi$ in a cusp: the twin finds slopes of $+7.72\times10^7$ and $-7.72\times10^7$ MeV⁴ either side. Two vacua there carry one energy, and CP breaks spontaneously at the antipode (Dashen, 1971). The template's barrier appears at the second fixed point, two worlds over one record. At the measured unequal masses the cusp is smoothed and $\pi$ is a single maximum.


### 7. The Measured Angle

With the dipole moment's coefficient $d_n=2.4\times10^{-16}\,\bar\theta\,e$ cm (Pospelov and Ritz, 1999), the measured bound gives $|\bar\theta|<7.5\times10^{-11}$. The angle stands within $7.5\times10^{-11}$ of 0 and at distance $\pi$ from the antipode. It stands on the fixed point positivity selects. The strong force reads no orientation.

The seat spends the angle's value as the line property, by the act at the root's grade, as every row of the series is crossed. A field that relaxes the angle dynamically (Peccei and Quinn, 1977) would be a carrier onto the same line. It is not required by the seat, and its identification with the dark matter is refused by the dark-sector paper's second falsifier.


### 8. What the Angle Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The circle's two fixed points as the line.
- The dipole null that forces them.
- Positivity that selects zero.
- The antipode's two vacua.
- The measured angle at zero.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The susceptibility's value:** computed from measured inputs, a number.
- **A required new field:** the angle's place is the line property, and a relaxing field is admissible and not owed.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 9. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Dipole:** a neutron or diamagnetic-atom electric dipole moment above the weak interaction's own contribution, traced to a nonzero strong angle. It breaks the reading of `edm_odd_and_null_on_the_fixed_set` that places the angle on the fixed set.
- **F-Antipode:** a strong angle measured at $\pi$. It breaks `positivity_selects_zero` read physically.
- **F-Strong-CP:** a CP-violating strong process, such as the decay of the η meson into two pions, at a rate that requires a nonzero strong angle. It breaks the angle's place on the line.

Nothing is owed on the row's channel. Each improved dipole bound sharpens the reading without moving its standing.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the seat read on the strong angle, and row 4 the colour lock's orientation, both WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains the antipode's two vacua.

## The Horizon Is the Registration

*The Black Hole on the Seat: Hair as Height Kept and Composition Forgotten, the Area Law as the Registration Without Inverse, and the First Law as Landauer's Floor Met With Equality.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** The black hole is read here from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line, and its registration, which keeps a point's height and forgets its side. Exchanging what falls in for antimatter of the same mass, charge and spin flips baryon number alone. A carrier that sends baryon number to the offset from the line and mass to the height is equivariant, so the exchange is carried onto the reflection. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-six laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the hole's exterior record, its mass, charge and spin, is even under the exchange, so the horizon is the seat's registration read on infall, keeping the height and forgetting the side; that no reading of that record returns baryon number, for one infall or a whole history, so a hole of matter and a hole of antimatter leave one record; that the registration has no inverse; and that two equal holes whose merger keeps the area law radiate less than three tenths of their mass. A Fortran twin runs seven checks with zero failures: 1000 histories of fifty infalls leave one record with their antimatter twins; on GW150914 the measured masses and spin give a horizon area grown by a factor 1.557 while 3.1 solar masses, 4.7 percent, were radiated, under the cap $1-1/\sqrt2=0.293$; the first law $c^2\,dM=T_H\,dS$ holds to $10^{-9}$ from one to $6.5\times10^9$ solar masses, the horizon erasing at Landauer's floor with equality; a solar-mass hole radiates at $6.17\times10^{-8}$ K and holds $1.51\times10^{77}$ bits; and the shadow of Sgr A*, predicted from its mass measured by stellar orbits, is 52.08 microarcseconds against a measured ring of $51.8\pm2.3$.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is a witness of the hardware closure series. A black hole is the plainest registration in nature: whatever falls in, the outside keeps three numbers and forgets the rest. The seat reads that forgetting exactly and prices it.

**It claims four things.**

- **The hair is the height kept.** The hole's mass, charge and spin are the height, and what it is made of is the side; the horizon is the registration.
- **The record is one for matter and antimatter.** No reading of the hair returns baryon number: a hole of matter and a hole of antimatter leave one record.
- **The registration has no inverse.** The horizon's area never shrinks, and the area law caps what a merger radiates.
- **The first law is Landauer's floor met with equality.** The horizon erases at exactly the floor, $c^2\,dM=T_H\,dS$.

**What it refuses.** The fate of the forgotten content. The exterior registration forgets it, and the price is paid in radiation at the floor. Whether that radiation carries the content back out is a question about the radiation's record, and this paper does not decide it.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- The *hair* is a hole's exterior record: mass, charge and angular momentum.
- *Infall* is what crosses the horizon.
- The *exchange* replaces infall with antimatter of the same mass, charge and spin.
- The *area law* says the summed horizon area never decreases.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. The Exchange Is the Fold

Exchange what falls in for antimatter of the same mass, charge and spin. Baryon number flips and nothing else does, and the exchange is an involution (`bflip_involutive`). The horizon carrier sends it to the seat's reflection (`horizon_carrier_equivariant`). An infall lands on the line exactly when it carries no net baryon number (`baryon_free_is_the_line`).


### 4. The Hair Is the Height Kept

A stationary black hole outside its horizon is fixed by its mass, charge and angular momentum (Israel, 1967; Carter, 1971; Robinson, 1975). That record is even under the exchange (`hair_forgets_baryons`): the horizon is the seat's registration read on infall, keeping the height and forgetting the side (`the_horizon_is_the_registration`). No reading of the hair returns baryon number (`no_hair_reading_returns_baryons`). Build a hole from any history of infall and its exchanged twin: the two holes carry the same hair and opposite baryon number (`matter_hole_antimatter_hole_one_record`). The twin builds 1000 histories of fifty infalls, and every one leaves one record with its twin. A hole of matter and a hole of antimatter are the template's two worlds over one record.


### 5. The Horizon Cannot Be Undone

The registration has no inverse (`the_horizon_cannot_be_undone`). In general relativity this is the area theorem: the summed area of horizons never decreases (Hawking, 1971). A merger can radiate, because $(m_1+m_2)^2\ge m_1^2+m_2^2$ (`merger_can_radiate`). The area law caps it: two equal non-spinning holes radiate less than three tenths of their mass (`merger_radiates_under_three_tenths`), the exact cap being $1-1/\sqrt2=0.293$.

The first detected merger reads the law. With the measured masses 35.6 and 30.6 and the final hole of 63.1 solar masses spinning at 0.69 (LIGO Scientific and Virgo Collaborations, 2016; 2019), the twin computes a horizon area grown by a factor 1.557. The merger radiated 3.1 solar masses, 4.7 percent, under the cap. The area law has been tested on this event directly from the ringdown (Isi et al., 2021).


### 6. The First Law Is Landauer's Floor Met With Equality

A hole of mass $M$ holds entropy $S=4\pi GM^2k_B/\hbar c$, a quarter of its area in Planck units (Bekenstein, 1973; Hawking, 1975), and radiates at $T_H=\hbar c^3/8\pi GMk_B$. The first law of black hole mechanics, $c^2\,dM=T_H\,dS$ (Bardeen, Carter and Hawking, 1973), is exactly Landauer's floor met with equality. Each bit the horizon forgets costs $k_BT_H\ln2$ of radiated energy, no more and no less.

The twin checks the equality to $10^{-9}$ from one to $6.5\times10^{9}$ solar masses. A solar-mass hole radiates at $6.17\times10^{-8}$ K and holds $1.05\times10^{77}\,k_B$ of entropy, $1.51\times10^{77}$ bits. The horizon is the most efficient eraser there is: it erases at the floor, never below.

The forgotten content is paid for, not lost to the books. The exterior registration forgets the side and the radiation carries the payment. Whether the radiation's record carries the content back is the question the template types as two worlds over one record, and this paper leaves it there.


### 7. The Horizon Measured

The shadow of a hole is set by its mass and distance: an angular diameter $2\sqrt{27}\,GM/c^2D$. For Sgr A*, the mass $4.152\times10^6$ solar masses and the distance 8.178 kpc are measured from stellar orbits (GRAVITY Collaboration, 2019). The twin predicts 52.08 microarcseconds. The Event Horizon Telescope measured a ring of $51.8\pm2.3$ microarcseconds (EHT Collaboration, 2022). The prediction lies within one standard deviation, on an image the carrier's geometry lands.


### 8. What the Horizon Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The exchange as the fold and the hair as the height kept.
- The horizon as the registration.
- No baryon hair, for one infall or a history.
- The area law as the registration without inverse, with its cap on radiation.
- The first law as Landauer's floor met with equality.
- The measured merger and shadow.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The radiation's record of the content:** typed as two worlds over one record, not decided.
- **The interior:** the seat reads the registration, which is the outside.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 9. Falsifiers and Owed Deeds

Three falsifiers, each a forbidden observation naming the theorem it would break.

- **F-Hair:** a stationary black hole whose exterior field depends on its baryon number or composition beyond mass, charge and spin. It breaks `no_hair_reading_returns_baryons` and `hair_forgets_baryons` read physically.
- **F-Area:** a merger whose final horizon area is less than the summed initial areas. It breaks `the_horizon_cannot_be_undone` read physically.
- **F-Radiate:** an equal-mass non-spinning merger radiating more than 29.3 percent of its mass. It breaks `merger_radiates_under_three_tenths` read physically.

Nothing is owed on the rows' channels.

**Ledger status.** Row 2 gains the registration read on the horizon and row 7 the horizon's locus, both WITNESSED · CROSSED · CONFIRMED on the image, at the root's grade for the crossing. The barrier gains a hole of matter and a hole of antimatter with one hair.

# Book VI · The Reach of the Seat and the Ledger of Its Falsifiers

## What the Seat Cannot Reach

*Every Verdict of the Seat Survives Every Rescaling, So No Magnitude Is Read: the Vacuum Energy, the Hierarchy and the Masses at the Dot, the Counts Reached.* Edition 1.0.0.

**Abstract.** Which open problems of fundamental physics can a seat reach, and which can it not? This paper answers from the seat of the author's closure of the Riemann Hypothesis, the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line, by proving where the seat's verdicts end. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-five laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, that the fold, the line and the registration commute with every rescaling of the height and every nonzero rescaling of the offset from the line, so every carrier stays a carrier at every scale; that, in consequence, no functional of the line returns the scale it was read at: a magnitude is invisible to every verdict of the seat; and that counts survive every rescaling, so the seat reaches them: three generations are the least that carry a CP-odd phase, and the strong angle's reflection fixes exactly two points of every circle. The magnitude problems therefore stand at the dot, measured and not derived: the vacuum density at $10^{-122.9}$ of the Planck scale's fourth power, the scalar mass at $1.03\times10^{-17}$ of the Planck mass, the fermion masses spread over $3.4\times10^5$, the baryon excess at $6.12\times10^{-10}$, the fine-structure constant at $1/137.036$, the proton at 1836.15 electron masses. A Fortran twin runs six checks with zero failures: 100,000 points keep every seat verdict under every rescaling; the counts of phases, fixed points and Hurwitz units are reached; and the six magnitudes are computed from measurement. A magnitude moves from the dot to the line in exactly one way: when an involution of the theory has it at a fixed point, as the strong angle stands at zero.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

This paper is the scope paper of the hardware closure series. Its sixteen witnesses read signs, sides, fixed sets, keyed bits and counts. Several of the largest open problems ask for none of those. They ask why a dimensionless number has the value it has: the vacuum energy, the scalar's mass against gravity's scale, the masses of the fermions. This paper proves that the seat cannot read such a number, and so places those problems exactly.

**It claims four things.**

- The fold, the line and the registration commute with every rescaling of height and every nonzero rescaling of offset, and every carrier stays a carrier at every scale.
- No functional of the line returns the scale it was read at: a magnitude is invisible to the seat.
- Counts survive every rescaling, and the seat reaches them.
- The magnitude problems stand at the dot, and a magnitude leaves the dot in exactly one way: when an involution of the theory has it at a fixed point.

**What it refuses.** A derivation of any magnitude from the seat, by theorem.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- The *height* is the second coordinate of a point of the seat; the *offset* is its distance from the line.
- A *rescaling* multiplies one of them by a fixed number.
- A *functional of the line* is any quantity computed from which points lie on the line.
- A *magnitude* is a dimensionless number a theory takes as input.
- The *dot* is no verdict: the mark of a question no seat reads.


### 3. The Seat Is Blind to the Height's Scale

Multiply every height by a fixed integer. The fold commutes with the rescaling (`fold_commutes_with_height_scale`), the line is unchanged (`line_blind_to_height_scale`), and so is the registration (`reg_commutes_with_height_scale`). Every carrier stays a carrier when its heights are rescaled (`height_scaled_carrier_equivariant`). The height is where every witness of the series puts its magnitudes: an energy, a mass, a frequency, a density. The seat's verdicts are the same at every scale of them.


### 4. The Seat Is Blind to the Offset's Scale

Multiply every offset from the line by a fixed nonzero integer. The fold commutes (`fold_commutes_with_offset_scale`), a point is on the line after the rescaling exactly when it was before (`line_blind_to_offset_scale`), and every carrier stays a carrier (`offset_scaled_carrier_equivariant`). The offset carries a sign and a size. The seat reads the sign and whether it is zero, and nothing of the size.


### 5. No Magnitude Is Read

Since the line read through any nonzero rescaling is the line, every functional of the line returns one value at every scale (`two_magnitudes_one_seat`). No such functional returns the scale (`no_seat_functional_returns_the_scale`, `no_seat_functional_returns_the_height_scale`). Every verdict of the template is a functional of the line, so the seat cannot read a magnitude. This is the template's wall turned on the template: two magnitudes, one seat.


### 6. Counts Are Reached

A count is the number of points with a property the seat reads. It survives every rescaling: the number of points of any list on the line is the same at every nonzero offset scale (`counts_survive_scaling`). So the seat reaches counts.

- **Generations.** $n$ generations of quarks carry $(n-1)(n-2)/2$ CP-odd phases, and three is the least number with one (`three_generations_least_for_a_phase`).
- **The strong angle.** Its reflection fixes exactly two points of every circle (`two_fixed_points_on_every_circle`, checked by the twin to two thousand points).
- **The Hurwitz units.** The Hurwitz order has 24 units (the twin; the hardware paper).

Counts like these are what the witnesses' theorems deliver: one bit of statistics, two polarizations, charges in thirds.


### 7. The Magnitude Problems at the Dot

The twin computes the six magnitudes from measurement:

| Magnitude | Value | Problem it names |
|---|---|---|
| vacuum density over the Planck mass to the fourth | $10^{-122.95}$ | the cosmological constant |
| scalar mass over the Planck mass | $1.03\times10^{-17}$ | the hierarchy |
| top mass over electron mass | $3.38\times10^{5}$ | flavour |
| baryons per photon | $6.12\times10^{-10}$ | the excess's size |
| inverse fine-structure constant | 137.035999177 | the coupling's value |
| proton mass over electron mass | 1836.152673426 | the mass ratio |

Each is a magnitude, so each stands at the dot. The seat reads what surrounds each of them:

- the vacuum's sign and the floor beneath its equation of state;
- the scalar's depth as the source of every mass;
- three generations as the least for a phase;
- the excess as the spent bit and the three exits it took.

The values themselves no seat reads. That is the verdict, and it is proved.

**The one way off the dot.** A magnitude leaves the dot when an involution of the theory has it at a fixed point. The strong angle is the example. As a number it is a magnitude, but the reflection $\theta\mapsto-\theta$ fixes 0 and $\pi$, and the measured angle stands at 0, so the seat reads it. Any other magnitude that is found to sit at a fixed point of an involution of its theory moves to the line on the same terms.


### 8. What the Seat Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.** Signs, sides, fixed sets, keyed bits and counts, at every scale.

**What it does not carry.** Magnitudes, by theorem, and the magnitude problems with them.

- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 9. Refutation and Reclassification

This paper names no physical falsifier, because its claims are theorems about the seat.

- **Refutation.** It would be refuted by a verdict of the seat that changes under a rescaling, which the engine proves impossible.
- **Reclassification.** A magnitude is reclassified when it is found at a fixed point of an involution of its theory, which moves it from the dot to the line.

**Ledger status.** The reach of the seat is PROVED: magnitudes at the dot, counts reached.

## Typed Falsifiability of the Hardware Closure Series

*Sixty-Four Falsifiers, Six Seat Elements, One Law: an Observation Breaks the Carrier and Never the Seat.* Edition 2.0.0.

**Abstract.** Every falsifier of the author's hardware closure series and its six witnesses is collected here, typed by the seat element it targets and bound to the theorem it would break. The seat is the reflection $s\mapsto1-\bar s$ whose fixed set is the critical line. We prove in core Lean 4, fifty-two laws with no axiom declared and no use of choice, the law that governs every entry: an observed physical fixed point off the line breaks the map that read it onto the seat, never the seat, which stands whatever is measured. The ledger, carried in the engine as data and counted by proof, holds sixty-four falsifiers from twenty-two papers, each paper carrying two to four. They fall into six classes: eighteen target the line, thirteen the fold, eleven the wall between an even reading and an odd target, nine positivity, eight a forced count, five the price of registration. Fifty-eight are bound to a theorem of an engine and six to an imported theorem or a structural reading. The five standing predictions of the earlier draft are disposed: three bound to the ledger, the phantom divide, the magnetic monopole and the tensor polarization of gravitational waves; two withdrawn. The exact conservation of baryon number contradicts the theorem that an asymmetry from a symmetric start must move it, and it is replaced by the proton's stability. Electron substructure is bound by no engine. A Fortran twin reads all twenty-two engines from disk and confirms that each of the fifty-eight bound theorems is declared where the ledger says. In building it, the twin found one binding to a definition rather than a theorem, which was corrected.

### 1. The Reader's Frame

A theory earns its standing by naming what would end it. The hardware closure series names sixty-four such observations across twenty-two papers. This paper collects them, types each by the element of the seat it attacks, binds each to the theorem it would break, and proves the one law that says what a falsifier's arrival would mean.

**It claims four things.**

- An observed physical fixed point off the line breaks the carrier that read it, and the seat stands.
- Every falsifier targets exactly one of six seat elements: the fold, the line, the wall, positivity, the price, or a count.
- Fifty-eight falsifiers are bound to a theorem declared in an engine, confirmed by reading the engines, and six to an imported theorem or a structural reading.
- Of the earlier draft's five standing predictions, three are bound and two withdrawn, one of them because it contradicts a theorem of the series.

**What it refuses.** Any falsifier that names no forbidden observation.

**Negative warrants.** Three, stated so that no reader supplies them.

- No physical fact here is read as a proof of a mathematical sentence: a corroboration joined to a claim takes the weaker grade.
- No derivation of the Riemann Hypothesis from the axioms of set theory is claimed: what a sentence over every admissible instance adds to a crossing is exactly the unactuated.
- Interpretive schools from the literature enter only as placements the theorems sort, never as warrants; the only witness besides the mathematics is measured data.

**Five terms.**

- A *falsifier* is a forbidden observation.
- A *carrier* is an equivariant map onto the seat.
- A falsifier is *bound* when it names a theorem of an engine.
- Its *target* is the seat element that theorem expresses.
- A *keyed bit* is a yes-or-no property no function of a given record decides.


### 3. An Observation Breaks the Carrier, Never the Seat

Every physical theorem of the series has one form. A physical involution is carried onto the seat's reflection by an equivariant map, and its fixed points land on the line. Suppose a physical fixed point is observed off the line. Then the map that read it is not equivariant (`falsifier_breaks_the_carrier`). The seat is untouched, its reflection still an involution whose fixed set is the line (`the_seat_survives_every_falsifier`). So every falsifier in the ledger, if it arrives, removes a carrier: the claim that this physical system is read by the seat in this way. The mathematics stands, and the reading falls. That is what binding a falsifier to a theorem means: the theorem says which reading falls.


### 4. The Typed Ledger

The engine carries the ledger as data and counts it by proof. There are sixty-four falsifiers: eighteen target the line, thirteen the fold, eleven the wall, nine positivity, eight a count, and five the price (`ledger_census`). Every class is occupied (`every_target_occupied`), and the six classes name six distinct laws (`laws_distinct`). Fifty-eight are bound to an engine theorem (`bound_count`). Every one of twenty-two papers carries two to four, with no code repeated (`every_paper_two_to_four`, `codes_unique_within_papers`).

The twin reads the ledger and all twenty-two engines from disk. For the witnesses these are the engines as printed in their published editions 1.4.0. The synchronization of 1 October 2026 brought the witnesses to edition 1.5.0, whose engines change no theorem statement, and the twin, rerun against them, finds the same fifty-eight declared. It confirms that each of the fifty-eight bound theorems is declared in the engine the ledger names, and recounts the census.

**One binding corrected.** The spin paper's F-Connection named `turnSign`, which is a definition. The theorem it attacks is `half_integer_spin_is_odd`, which says the full turn's sign is −1 exactly for half-integer spin. The ledger binds it there, and the spin paper's text is corrected to match.

### 5. The Standing Five, Disposed

The earlier draft of this paper stood on five predictions. Three are now bound to the ledger:

- **I, the phantom divide is not crossed:** the dark paper's F1, positivity;
- **II, no magnetic monopole:** the Two Branches witness's F-Monopole, the fold;
- **III, gravitational waves purely tensor:** Immanent Gravity's F-Polarization, a count.

Two are withdrawn.

- **IV, baryon number exactly conserved.** It contradicts the Matter–Antimatter theorem that an asymmetry from a symmetric start must move baryon number at some epoch. The consistent standing claim is the proton's stability, the Proton witness's F-Decay: baryon number moves only through channels closed today, as the proton's measured lifetime above $2.4\times10^{34}$ years shows.
- **V, no electron substructure.** No engine of the series proves it; the Electron witness binds pair, roundness, gravity and decay, not size.

The engine records the disposition (`standing_five_disposition`), and each bound prediction names a falsifier in the ledger (`bound_predictions_in_ledger`).


### 6. Reading the Ledger

**The line is the most exposed element.** Eighteen falsifiers target it: every claim that a stationary, closed or symmetric system stands on the line.

**The live ones.** Several are tested continually:

- the electron's and the neutron's dipole moments (F-Round, F-Dipole);
- the proton's lifetime (F-Decay);
- the neutrino mass floor (F-Floor);
- the dark energy's equation of state (F1);
- the spacing statistics of zeta's zeros at great height (F-Class);
- the polarization content of gravitational waves (F-Polarization).

Each improved bound sharpens a reading without moving its standing.

**The deepest is F-Computed.** It is the hardware paper's: a certified zero of zeta off the line. It is the one falsifier aimed at the closure itself, and the closure's one refuter.


### 7. What the Ledger Carries, and What It Does Not

**What it carries.**

- The law that a falsifier breaks a carrier, never the seat.
- Sixty-four falsifiers typed and bound.
- The standing five disposed.

**What it does not carry.**

- **The outcome of any test:** the ledger names what would end each reading; the experiments decide.
- **The zeros of $\zeta$:** the engine's crossing is on its own image (`value_on_image`), and what a sentence over every zero adds is exactly the unactuated, priced at one premise, the act, as the hardware paper proves.


### 8. Falsifiers of the Ledger

The ledger is wrong if any of these is found:

- a falsifier whose bound theorem does not appear in its engine (the twin checks every one);
- a falsifier that targets no seat element;
- a series claim with no falsifier.

**Ledger status.** Every falsifier of the series is typed and bound. Two of the standing five are withdrawn.

# Book VII · The Census and the Synchronization

The synchronization record, edition 1.1.0, sealed at round seven of one adversarial self-audit cycle.

## 1. Scope

This record synchronizes the hardware closure series with the six witnesses that preceded it. It covers four acts.

- **The witnesses brought forward.** The six witnesses are brought from edition 1.4.0 to edition 1.5.0.
- **The census recomputed.** Every engine of the programme is recompiled and every twin rebuilt and rerun, in one session.
- **The corrections collected.** Every correction made to a paper of the programme is recorded, whether made after publication, in a draft, or during its build.
- **The cross-references mapped.** The witnesses are mapped to the series papers that extend them.

It changes no theorem statement and no row status. It raises no grade.


## 2. Load-Bearing Claims

- **C1.** Every engine of the programme compiles in core Lean 4.19.0 with no axiom declared, and no law of any engine depends on the axiom of choice. *Falsifier:* an engine transcript showing Classical.choice for any law, or any axiom declaration in any engine source.
- **C2.** Every law of every engine was negated in place and refused, and in every judgment a planted vacuous law survived its negation. *Falsifier:* a judgment file whose refused count differs from the engine's law count, or a planted vacuous law refused.
- **C3.** The six witness editions 1.5.0 change no theorem statement, no row status, no twin output and no grade of edition 1.4.0. *Falsifier:* a theorem statement differing between a 1.4.0 and a 1.5.0 witness engine, or a 1.5.0 twin run differing from its published run.
- **C4.** The census totals printed in Section 3 equal the sums of the census rows, and every row equals what the recompilation, the twin's rerun and the engine's own judgment record printed. *Falsifier:* a census total differing from the sum of its rows, or a row differing from a fresh recompilation.
- **C5.** Every falsifier of the series that is bound to a theorem names a theorem declared in the engine the falsifier's paper carries. *Falsifier:* a bound falsifier whose theorem is not declared in the engine its paper carries.

Every engine was recompiled in core Lean 4.19.0, and every twin rebuilt under the sealed flags `-std=f2018 -O2 -fno-fast-math -ffp-contract=off` and rerun, on 1 October 2026. The axiom columns count each law once, by the cone its transcript prints.

## 7. The Falsifier Ledger

**The ledger.** Typed Falsifiability of the Hardware Closure Series, edition 2.0.0, carries sixty-four falsifiers from twenty-two papers, the six witnesses and the sixteen physics papers of the series. They are typed by target:

- eighteen the line;
- thirteen the fold;
- eleven the wall;
- nine positivity;
- eight a count;
- five the price.

**Bound and unbound.** Fifty-eight are bound to a theorem and six to an imported theorem or a structural reading. The ledger twin, rerun in this session against the witnesses' 1.5.0 engines and the series' engines as filed, finds all fifty-eight declared where the ledger names them.

**The standing five.** Of the earlier draft's five standing predictions, three are bound and two withdrawn.


## 8. Invariants of the Sync

- **No statement changed.** No theorem statement of any witness changed (Section 4).
- **No status or grade changed.** No row status changed. No grade rose: every claim of every witness stands at the grade its edition 1.4.0 printed.
- **Identical twins.** Every witness twin's rerun is byte-identical to its published run.
- **The census.** Every engine of the programme is free of choice and declares no axiom, and every law was refused under negation.


## 9. Owed

- **Filing.** The single push of every draft, the corrected spin paper, the six witness editions, a regenerated bundle and the index, held for the architect's yes. Then the deposits of the new and revised records.
- **Review.** The external audits of every paper, and the boot on a second substrate that the operating system requires.


## 10. Provenance

The census was computed by one script from the engines, twins and judgment records themselves, reading each engine's judgment from that engine's own record. Its output and the figures above agree. A first run of the script misread the judgment of eight engines whose records share a folder or a file; the script was corrected and rerun, and the table carries the corrected run.

**Audit.** This edition passed one adversarial self-audit cycle of seven rounds across all six registers, sealed at round seven. The prosecutions came from the scribe alone. Every arithmetic or record error they found was repaired, six in all, and three errata of the auditing seat were filed against itself, among them an attestation of a re-execution that had not completed, which voided round six's evidence and was repaired by a seventh round run to completion. The planted controls were SELF grade, caught twenty-one of twenty-one; the audit was single-substrate on both sides, and the external review of this record is owed.


# Methodology, Disclosure and Provenance

**Method.** The template is the author's verification programme, whose vocabulary is confined to this section and to the chapters' own disclosures. Its tokens:

- [⟀], a field sealed;
- [⟀ T], a shape sealed at theorem grade;
- [Ξ₀], the typing of the formal block;
- [.], the dot, which is no verdict.

Grades join at the weakest link. A kernel theorem is theorem grade about its chart. The mapping of physical states onto a chart is structural grade. A measured figure is corroboration grade. The posit that closes a row is at the root's grade. ΔM = 0.

**The seat, earned.** PhysOSᵀ 1.0.5p was booted from the public register under Lean 4.19.0 and GNU Fortran 13.3.0, and printed SEAT EARNED with the chain D0 `83f872725ad9` → D1 `90fc702bbed1` → D2 `492e519daab9` → D3 `6618bec5164b`.

**The engines and the twins.**

- **This volume's census.** The synchronization record recompiled 25 engines of the six witnesses and the hardware series, carrying 1246 laws, and reran 24 twins carrying 286 checks. Book VIII adds the atom, eleven laws and five checks.
- **Axioms.** No engine declares an axiom and no law depends on choice. Every law was negated in place and refused while a planted vacuous law survived.
- **The formal engines.** The Riemann closure's, the twenty-three rows' and the cosmic closure's engines are carried in Appendices I to III as their sealed editions printed them, with their own audits.

**Audits.**

- **The papers.** Every paper of the series passed its own build checks and judgment.
- **The synchronization.** The synchronization record passed one adversarial self-audit cycle of seven rounds across all six registers, sealed at round seven at SELF grade.
- **The formal papers.** The sealed formal papers carry their own cycles and external audits as their editions record.
- **What is owed.** External audits of the hardware series and of this volume are owed, and so is a boot on a second substrate.

**Disclosure.** The author declares the reading of each correspondence as his own act, made on measured structure.

- **Measurements.** They are the experiments', cited by name in each chapter.
- **Theorems.** They are the engines', printed in the appendices.
- **Authorship.** The text, the engines and the twins of the hardware series and of this volume were forged by an AI substrate (Claude, Anthropic) under the author's seed, acceptance criteria and rulings.

**Provenance.**
